iṭikkaḻakam
pāṇṭiyanāṭṭiṉ perumpakuti maṭṭumiṉṟi, paḵṟuḷiyāṟum paṉmali yaṭukkattuk kumarikkōṭum mūḻkavē, pāṇṭiyar kuṭiyil eñciyiruntavaṉ taṉ pēriḻappi yeṇṇi varunti ataṟku oruvāṟu īṭuceytu koḷvatupōl, vaṭakkiṟ ceṉṟu paṉimalik kuvaṭṭilum kaṅkiyāṟṟaṅkariyilum taṉkayal muttiriyip poṟittu mīṇṭāṉ. aṉṟu aṅku vallara ciṉmiyālum perumpakuti kāṭāyiruntamiyālum, ciṟitum avaṉukku etirppillātu pōyiṟṟu. araciyal akkālattil virivaṭiyā tiruntataṉālum. vaṭanāvalap pakuti mikat toliviluḷḷamiyālum moḻipeyar tēya mākiyālum, avaṉ aṅkut taṉ kuṭiyiṉaṉ oruvaṉit tuṇiyariyaṉāka irutti viṭṭut teṉṉāṭṭiṟkē mīṇṭu ati āṇṭu vantāṉ.
paḵṟuḷi yāṟṟuṭaṉ paṉmali yaṭukkattuk
kumarikkōṭuṅ koṭuṅkaṭal koḷḷa
vaṭaticik kaṅkiyum imayamuṅ koṇṭu
teṉṟici yāṇṭa teṉṉavaṉ vāḻi
eṉṟu (cilap. 11 : 19 - 22) iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ kūṟutal kāṇka.
kaṭalkōṭkut tappiya kuṭikaḷik kuṭiyamarttuvatilum, tali nakaramip patilum, āṭcittuṇivarit tērnteṭup patilum, paṭitokuppatilum, nīṇṭa kālañ ceṉṟataṉāl, pāṇṭiya ṉāl nēraṭiyākat tamiḻnilam muḻuvatuṅ kavaṉikka muṭiya villi. nāvalantēyap pakutikaḷiyāṇṭa irutuṇi yari yarum, annilimiyip payaṉpaṭuttik koṇṭu iruvēnta rākap pirintupōyiṉar. kīḻnāṭu nelviḷivāṟ cōḻa nāṭeṉṟum, mēlnāṭu maliccarivāṟ cāral nāṭeṉṟum, peyarpeṟṟiruntataṉāl; kīḻivēntaṉ cōḻaṉ eṉṟum, mēlivēntaṉ cēraṉ eṉṟum, āḷkuṭippeyar peṟṟaṉar.
col = nel. col - coṉṟi = cōṟu. col = (coṟu) - cōṟu. col - (cōl) - (cōḷ) - cōḻ - cōḻam - cōḻaṉ.
o.nō : kal (karumi) - kaḷ - kāḷ - kāḻ = karumi
kāḻ - kāḻakam = karumi.
kil (tōṇṭu) - kīḷ - keḷ - kēḷ - kēḻal = maṇṇit tōṇṭum āṇ (paṉṟi).
tul (poruntu) - toḷ - tōḷ - tōḻaṉ.
pul (tuḷi) - pol - pōḷ - pōḻ = piḷavu, tuṇṭu, vār.
cōḻanāṭu nelvaḷamum ataṉāṟ cōṟṟuvaḷamum mikkiruntatu. mutaṉmutalil iyaṟkiyākavum nel aṅku mikutiyāka viḷintiruttal vēṇṭum.
vēḻa muṭittu malināṭu mētakka
cōḻa vaḷanāṭu cōṟuṭittu - pūḻiyarkōṉ
teṉṉāṭu muttuṭittu teṇṇīr vayaṟṟoṇṭi
naṉṉāṭu cāṉṟō ruṭittu.
eṉṟu auviyār oruvar pāṭiyiruttal kāṇka.
neṟpayir eṅkum mikutiyāka viḷikkappaṭum ikkālattilum, cōḻa nāṭṭiṉ karuvakamākiya tañci māvaṭṭamē tamiḻakattiṉ neṟkaḷañciya māyiruttal kāṇka. iṉṟu itaṟkuk kāviri yāṟṟu vaḷamē aṭippaṭik karaṇiyamākum.
cōḻaṉukkuk kiḷḷi, ceṉṉi, vaḷavaṉ eṉṟum kuṭippeya ruṇṭu.
kuṭamali yeṉṉum mēṟkut toṭarcci malikkuk kīḻppuṟa māyiṉum mēṟpuṟamāyiṉum, cēranāṭṭiṉ perumpakuti ammaliyiṉ aṭivārac carivē. maliyaṭivāram cāral eṉappaṭum.
cāral nāṭa naṭunāḷ  (kuṟun.195).
cāral nāṭa cevviyi yākumati  (mēṟpaṭi-11 : 2).
cāral nāṭa vāralō eṉavē  (mēṟpaṭi-141 : 8).
eṉpaṉa malippakka nāṭṭic cāral nāṭu eṉak kūṟutal kāṇka.
cāral - cēral - cēralaṉ. cēral - cēraṉ. cēramakaṉ - cēramāṉ.
cēral, cēralaṉ, cēraṉ, cēramāṉ eṉa nālvaṭivilum cēraṉkuṭip peyar vaḻaṅkum.
kuṭakkōc cēral iḷaṅkō vaṭikaṭku  (cilap.pati:1)
cēralar, cuḷḷiyam pēriyāṟṟu veṇṇuri kalaṅka  (akam. 149:7-8).
kulavuvēṟ cēraṉ koṭittiṟaṅ kēṭṭu  (cilap. 23:62)
pāṉmi naṇpāṟ cēramāṉ tōḻareṉṟu pārparavum  (pe.pu.kaḻaṟiṟ. 66)
cēraṉukku utiyaṉ, kuṭanāṭaṉ, kōti, poṟiyaṉ, maliyaṉ, vāṉavaṉ, vāṉavarampaṉ, villaṉ eṉṉum kuṭippeyarkaḷu muṇṭu.
pāṇṭiyaṉukkup pōṉṟē cēracōḻarkkum nāṟpaṭiyum aimperuṅkuḻuvum eṇpērāyamum aivaki yuṟutic cuṟṟamum amintaṉa.
mūvēntarkkum, aracac ciṉṉam patteṉṟum araciyaluṟuppu ēḻeṉṟum koḷḷappaṭṭaṉa.
mukuṭam (muṭi), ceṅkōl, māli, muttiri, kuṭi, koṭi, muracu, tēr, yāṉi, kutiri eṉpaṉa pattuvakic ciṉṉam. koṭiyum muttiriyum kuṟivaṭivil oṉṟēṉum, poruḷ vaṭivilum payaṉpāṭṭu vakiyilum vēṟām.
nāṭu, kuṭi, poruḷ, paṭi, araṇ, amiccu, naṭpu eṉṉum ēḻum araciyaluṟuppām. nāṭillāmaṟ kuṭiyilliyātaliṉ, nāṭṭik kuṭiyuḷ aṭakki,
paṭikuṭi kūḻamiccu naṭpara ṇāṟum
uṭiyāṉ aracaruḷ ēṟu.
eṉṟār tiruvaḷḷuvar (kuṟaḷ.381). āyiṉum, nāṭum ōr uṟuppām eṉpati aṟivittaṟkē, ati 74-ām atikārat talippākkiṉār. nāṭillāmaṟ kuṭiyilli; āyiṉ, kuṭiyillāmal nāṭuṇṭu.
aracaṉiṉ ciṉṉam (aṭiyāḷam) vēṟu; aracaṉiṉ araciya luṟuppu vēṟu. araciyaluṟuppu ēḻum cērntu ōr uṭampum, aracaṉ ataṉ uyirum, ākum.
maṉṉa ṉuyirttē malartali yulakam. (puṟam.186).
uṟuppuñ ciṉṉamum oṉṟeṉa mayakki, peyar (nāmam), nāṭu, ūr, āṟu, mali, ūrti, paṭi, muracu, tār, koṭi eṉṟu tiruvācakamum,
āṟum maliyum yāṉiyuṅ kutiriyum
nāṭu mūrum koṭiyu muracum
tārun tērun tacāṅka meṉappaṭum,
eṉṟu tivākara vuriccoṟ ṟokutiyum,
mali, yāṟu, nāṭu, ūr, mali, kutiri, yāṉi, koṭi, muracu, tāṉi eṉṟu cūṭāmaṇi yuriccoṟ ṟokutiyum;
yāṉi, nāṭu, ūr, āṟu, mali, kutiri, tēr, muracu, tār, koṭi eṉṟu veṇpāp pāṭṭiyalum,
maliyē yāṟē nāṭē yūrē
paṟiyē pariyē kaḷiṟē tārē
peyarē koṭiyē eṉṟivi tacāṅkam.
eṉṟu paṉṉirupāṭṭiyalum,
malinati nāṭūr vaṉitār ivuḷi
kolimata kaḷiṟu koṭimura cāṇi
iviyē tacāṅkam eṉmaṉār pulavar.
eṉṟu ilakkaṇa viḷakkamum, kūṟum. ivaṟṟiṉ porun tāmiyi eṇṇik kāṇka.
muṭikōl māli muttiri kuṭikoṭi
muracutēr yāṉi kutiri ciṉṉam.
paṭikuṭi kūḻamiccu naṭparaṇ nāṭō
ṭuṭiyāṉ aracaruḷ ēṟu.
ivi uric ceyyuḷ eṉak koḷka.
mukuṭam eṉpatu teṉcollē.
muku - mukiḷ. mukiḷtal = arumputal. mukiḷ-mukiḻ = arumpu. 
mukiḻ-mukiḻam-pērarumpu. mukiḻ-mukiḻi. mukiḻittal = arumputal. mukiḷ-mukuḷ-mukuḷam = pērarumpu, moṭṭu. mukuḷ-mukuḷi. mukuḷittal = ka.arumputal. u.kuvital. mukuḷikkum..... aravinta nūṟāyiram (taṇṭi.62)
mukuḷam-mukuṭam = kuvinta aracar maṇimuṭi. mukuṭamum peruñcēṉiyum (pārata.kuru.14).
mukuṭam - va. mukuṭṭa.
mukuṭam-makuṭam = maṇimuṭi. arakkaṉṟaṉ makuṭam (kamparā. mutaṟpō. 246).
makuṭam-va. makuṭṭa.
parē tam camaṟkiruta moḻiyil (Sanskrit Language) mukuṭam teṉcollēyeṉṟu kāṭṭiyiruppatik kāṇka. (pak.385).
nāṭṭut taṭṭallāta ciṟanta kulakkutirikaḷ puravi, pāṭalam, kōṭakam, ivuḷi, vaṉṉi, kutiri, pari, kantukam, kaṉavaṭṭam, kōram mutaliya palavakikaḷāka vakukkappaṭṭiruntaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ, pāṇṭiyaṉ kutiri kaṉavaṭṭam; cōḻaṉ kutiri kōram; cēraṉ kutiri pāṭalam; ciṟṟaracar kutiri kantukam.
ticipaṟṟip pāṇṭiyanāṭu teṉpulam eṉṟum, cōḻanāṭu kuṇapulam eṉṟum, cēranāṭu kuṭapulam eṉṟum, collap paṭṭaṉa. pāṇṭiyaṉukkut teṉṉaṉ allatu teṉṉavaṉ eṉṉum peyarum eḻuntatu. munnāṭum, taṉittaṉi nāṭeṉṟum oruṅkē tamiḻakam eṉṟum peyarpeṟṟaṉa.
kaṭal kōṭkuppiṉ eñciyirunta paḻampāṇṭi nāṭṭup pakutiyiṉ ūṭu, kumari eṉṉum pēriyāṟu ōṭiṟṟu.
teṉāa turukeḻu kumari (puṟam. 6:2) eṉpataṉāl ataṉ perumiyum vēkamum aṟiyappaṭum.
vāra ṇāciyōr maṟiyōm pāḷaṉ
āraṇa vuvātti apañcikaṉ eṉpōṉ
pārppaṉi cāli kāppukkaṭi kaḻintu
koṇṭōṟ piḻitta taṇṭa mañcit
teṉṟicik kumari yāṭiya varuvōḷ
eṉpataṉāl (maṇi.13:3-7), paṇṭināḷil, vaṭanāṭṭiṟ kaṅkiyāṟṟiṉ kariyil vāḻntōrum, tam tīviṉi pōkka vantu nīrāṭumāṟu, kumariyāṟu oru ciṟanta tirunīrniliyāka viruntatiyaṟiyalām.
pāṇṭiyaṉ taṉ talinakarik kumariyāṟṟiṉ kayavāyil amittatākat terikiṉṟatu. kayavāy eṉpatu āṟu kaṭaloṭu kalakkumiṭam.
kaṭaṉmaṇ ṭaḻuvattuk kayavāy kaṭuppa  (malipaṭu.528).
pāṇṭiyaṉ talinakar kaṭalvaḻiyāka varuvōrkku vāyil pōl iruntamiyāl, katavam allatu katavapuram eṉṟu peyarpeṟṟirukkalām. kapāṭapuram eṉṉum vaṭacol alivāy eṉpataṉ moḻipeyarppākavum irukkalām.
kaṭattal kaṭantucellutal. kaṭa-kaṭi = vāyil. kaṭa-kaṭavu = kaṭantu cellum vaḻi. kaṭavu-katavu = vāyil, vāyilaṭippu. o. nō : door = door-shutter, entrance. katavu-katavam = peruṅkatavu.
katavam-va. kavāṭa-kapāṭa (muṟimāṟṟup pōli). vaṭamoḻiyil iccoṟku mūlam illi.
kumarimali, kaṭalkoṇṭa nāṭṭil evvāṟu amin tiruntatu eṉṟu āyumiṭattu, annāṭṭiṉ pakutikaḷi naṉkaṟintu kāṭṭu eliyaṭṭu (Scott Elliot) eḻutiyuḷḷa maṟinta kumarikkaṇṭam eṉṉum āṅkila nūluṭpōnta paṭattiṉālē, oru peru maliyāṉatu mēlikkaṭalil toṭaṅki ...... maṭa kācukkar (Madagascar) eṉṟa āppirikkat tīvuvari ceṉṟatākat terikiṉṟatu..... immaliyit tamiḻiṟ kumariyeṉṟum vaṭa moḻiyil makēntiram eṉṟum, muṉṉōr kūṟiṉāreṉpa taṟkuk kāraṇamuṇṭu. civatarumōttiram eṉṉuñ civavupāka mattil, potiyiṟkut teṉpāl makēntira muṇṭeṉṟum, an nūluriyuḷ teṟkumutal vaṭakku īṟāka aḵtu iruntateṉ ṟum, kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ataṉaṭivārattuḷḷa tēcam poṉmaya māṉa ilaṅki yeṉṟum kuṟikkappaṭukiṉṟatu. eṉṟu, pērā kā.cuppiramaṇiyap piḷḷi ci.ci.nū.pa. kaḻakappatippāṉa tolkāppiya eḻuttatikāra nacciṉārkkiṉiyattiṟkut tām varintuḷḷa ārāycci muṉṉuriyiṟ kūṟiyuḷḷār.
uṉṉatatteṉ mayēntiramē yuyarmaliyañ ciyakiri
viṉṉaviluñ cuttikamē yirukkumuyar vintiyamē
paṉṉupukaḻ mikupāri yāttiramē yeṉappakarnta
iṉṉakiri yēḻumutaṟ kumaritalat ticintaṉavē.
aṅkametir niraṉiṟiyāṟ camākkiyamu maṇimaliyuñ
caṅkamuntaṇ kumutamunal varākameṉun talantāṉun
tuṅkamali potitteṉpāṟ ṟoṭarntavaṭi vārattiṉ
aṅkaṉaka vilaṅkiyumēḻ variccāra laṭittēcam.  (civatarumōttiram, kōpuraviyal, 47, 48)
icceyyuṭkaḷiṉiṉṟu, iṭikkaḻakak kālattil, kumari muṉikkut teṟkil paḻiya kumarimalit toṭariṉ pakuti yākavō taṉiyākavō orumaliyiruntateṉṟum, atiṉiṉṟē kumariyāṟu tōṉṟik kiḻakku nōkki yōṭiya teṉṟum, ilaṅki intiyāvōṭu iṇintiruntateṉṟum, kumari yāṟum poruniyāṟum ilaṅkiyūṭum ōṭiyirukkalāmeṉ ṟum, pāṇṭināṭu kumarimuṉiyiṉiṉṟu 500 kal tolivu teṟkē nīṇṭiruntirukkalāmeṉṟum, uyttuṇarappaṭum.
cōḻaṉukku uṟiyūrum, cēraṉukkut tirucci māvaṭṭattic cērnta karuvūrum, talinakarāyiruntaṉa. pāṇṭiyaṉ talinakar, āṭcikkum nīrvāṇikattiṟkum oruṅkē payaṉpaṭṭatu. nelli maturi mukavi māvaṭṭaṅkaḷiṉ mēl pakutiyum koṅkunāṭum vēmpāy (Bombay) mānilattiṉ mēlpākamum cēra nāṭāyiruntaṉa.
katavapuram kaṭṭiyamikkappaṭṭup pallāṇṭu ceṉṟapiṉ, iraṇṭām kaḻakam amikkappaṭṭatu. ataṉ uṟuppiṉar aimpattoṉpatiṉmar eṉṟum, avaruḷḷiṭṭup pāṭiṉār mūvāyiratteḻunūṟṟuvar eṉṟum, akkaḻakatti naṭattivanta pāṇṭiyar veṇṭērc ceḻiyaṉ mutal muṭattirumāṟaṉ īṟāka aimpattoṉpatiṉmar eṉṟum, avaruṭ pāvaraṅkēṟi ṉār aivar eṉṟum, kaḻakam irunta kāla nīṭci mūvāyiratteḻu nūṟṟāṇṭu eṉṟum, iṟiyaṉārakap poruḷuri kūṟukiṉṟatu. talikkaḻaka varalāṟṟiṟ pōṉṟē, itaṉ varalāṟṟilum pala ceytikaḷ taḷḷattakkaṉa. kaḻaka miruntatu maṭṭum uṇmiyāṉa ceytiyākum. iṭikkaḻaka nūlniliyattil irunta nūlkaḷ eṇṇāyiratteccam eṉṟu, oru cevimarapuc ceyti vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟatu. centamiḻp pāṇṭi nāṭṭiṉ māperum pakutiyik kaṭal koṇṭuviṭṭataṉāl, talikkaḻakattil 549-āka irunta pulavar toki 59-ākak kuṉṟiṟṟu.
mūvēntarum orē kuṭiyiṉarum orē moḻiyiṉarumātalāl, tammuṭ peṇkoṇṭuṅ koṭuttum iyaṉṟavari oṟṟu miyip pēṇi vantaṉar. āyiṉum, orōvoru camiyattu, paḻaviṟaltāyattō ṭamiyātu putuviṟal tāyatti nacciya pērācip peruvalivēntaṉ, ēṉivēnta ṉoruvaṉoṭu porutu avaṉ nāṭṭik kippaṟṟuvataṟkut tōṟṟuvāyāka, taṉnāṭṭup pālinila maṟavari yēvi avaṉ nāṭṭu mulli nilattu ānirikaḷik kavarntu varacceytu, avaṉip pōrukkut tūṇṭuvatum nērntatu. iti,
veṭci tāṉē kuṟiñciyatu puṟaṉē
uṭkuvarat tōṉṟum īrēḻ tuṟittē  (tol. 1002)
vēntuviṭu muṉiñar vēṟṟuppulak kaḷaviṉ
ātan tōmpal mēvaṟ ṟākum.   (tol. 1003)
vañci tāṉē mulliyatu puṟaṉē.   (mēṟpaṭi 1007).
eñcā maṇṇaci vēntaṉi vēntaṉ
añcutakat talicceṉ ṟaṭalkuṟit taṉṟē.  (tol. 1008)
eṉpavaṟṟāl aṟiyalām.
oru vēntaṉ iṉṉoru vēntaṉukkup peṇ koṭukka maṟutta pōtum, oru vēntaṉ koṭuṅkōlāṭci ceyyiṉ oru ceṅkōl vēntaṉ avaṉit tiruttumpōtum, pōr nikaḻum. āyiṉ, aṉṟu niri kavartalum niri mīṭṭalumiṉṟi nēraṭi yākap pōr toṭukkappaṭum.
pālinilat talivar, vēntarāl ēvappaṭumpōtu maṭṭumaṉṟi, valimi yaṟṟa vēntar āḷumpōtum, aṭuttuḷḷa mulli nilattu ānirikaḷik kavarvatu vaḻakkam.
iṅṅaṉam orupuṟam pāli maṟavarāl niri kavarappaṭṭum, maṟṟorupuṟam kōnāy puli mutaliya kāṭṭuvilaṅkukaḷāl mantiyāṭumāṭukaḷ aṭikkappaṭṭum, iṭarppaṭṭu vanta iṭiyar, taṟkāppu viṉiyilum taṭuppu viṉiyilum toṭarntu īṭupaṭṭataṉāl, nāḷaṭivil kuṟiñcinilak kuṟavarkkum pāli nila maṟavarkkum eḷḷaḷavum iḷikkāta kāḷiyarum āḷiyarum āyiṉar. āyiṉum, pāli nilattār pōl valiyap pōrkkuc ceṉṟatilli. āyiṉ, vanta pōri viṭṭatilli. āyarpōl āycciyarum maṟamikunta pālinilat tiṟkum ceṉṟu āṉintu viṟṟaṉar.
pāli nilattārpōṟ cūṟiyāṭaliyum pōrpuritaliyum vāḻkkit toḻilākak koḷḷātu, āṭavar āṭumā ṭerumiyākiya munnirikaḷi mēyttum, vāṉavārip payir kaḷi viḷittum; peṇṭir pāl tayir mōr veṇṇey neyyākiya āṉintik kuṟiñci pāli marutam ākiya munnilattum viṟṟum; amitiyāka vāḻkki naṭattivanta āyar, tam maṟattik kuṉṟāmaṟ kāttaṟkum tam uṭal valimiyi mēṉmēl vaḷarttaṟkum, ēṟutaḻuval eṉṉum peṇkōṭal muṟiyi ēṟpaṭuttiṉar.
āyar kulattil viṉivala pāṅkarallāta uyar kuṭiyiṟpiṟanta ovvoru peṇṇiṟkum, piṟantavuṭaṉ avvappeṇṇiṉ peyarāl ovvoru cēṅkaṉṟu otukkappeṟṟatu. ak kaṉṟu kaḷi, vēliyiṟ paḻakkātum vitiyaṭikkātum ciṟanta vūṭṭaṅ koṭuttu vaḷarttu vantaṉar. avi koḻuttup paruttu, kāḷip paruvattil, kaṭinteṭutta karuṅkālit tūṇkaḷ pōṉṟa kālkaḷuṭaṉum, uruṇṭu tiraṇṭa uṭaluṭaṉum, matarttuc civanta kaṇkaḷuṭaṉum. kaṇṭār añcum kaṭun tōṟṟatti aṭintaṉa. āṇṭutōṟum, pūppaṭintu maṇat tiṟkēṟṟa kaṉṉiyarkkuriya kāḷikaḷi yellām, kompu tiruttik kūrākki, oru kuṟitta naṉṉāḷil, aḻakākac cuvaṭik kappaṭṭa oru toḻuvattiṟkuḷ aṭittu, ovvoru kumariyi yum maṇakka virumpum āyarkulak kumarar avvak kumarikkuriya kāḷiyit taṉippaṭappiṭittaṭakki niṟuttumāṟu, avaṟṟit tiṟantu viṭṭaṉar. avi irumaruṅkuṅ kūṭiniṟkum peruṅ kūṭṭattik kaṇṭum, akkūṭṭattāriṉ āravārattoṭu kūṭiya palliyap pērōciyik kēṭṭum, maruṇṭu miraṇṭu, kūṟṟavaṉ tūtarpōṟ koṭiya pārviyuṭaṉ, vāli muṟukkiyum kālik kiḷappiyum uṭali vaḷittum kaḻuttit tirittum, kuḷampu paṭṭa viṭamellām tīppaṟakkak kutit tōṭiyapōtu, ēṟutaḻuvuṅ kumarar palar etir niṉṟa kompip paṟṟiyum, aḷḷiyiṟ pāyntu kaḻuttit taḻuviyum, piṉ ceṉṟu kāli vāriyum, piṟavāṟum, tām kuṟitta kāḷiyi aṭakki niṟutta iyaṉṟavari muyaṉṟaṉar. cilar kaṇṭa aḷavil añci niṉṟu viṭṭaṉar. cilar maṟu viḻāviṟkuk kaṭatti vittaṉar. cilar ciṟu puṇṇoṭu tirumpiṉar. cilar viḻuppuṇ paṭṭaṉar. cilar kuṭal carintu aṅkēyē māṇṭaṉar. cilar veṉṟaṉar. veṉṟavar tam vilimatippillāp paricip peṟṟu viṇṇiṉpan tuyttaṉar.
kaṭalkōḷiṉ piṉ, kaṭalkōḷaccattālum makkaṭ perukkiṉ viḷivākavum, peruṅkūṭṭattār vaṭanāvalañ ceṉṟu, kuṭiyamarntaṉar. aṅkut tam tāynāṭṭi niṉivu kūrtaṟku, toḻuni yāṟṟaṅ kariyaṭuttu oru nakaramittu ataṟku maturi eṉṟu peyariṭṭaṉar. atu teṉvāriyil muḻukip pōṉa paḵṟuḷi maturiyi nōkki, tamiḻarāl vaṭamaturi eṉap paṭṭatu. ataṉāl, paḵṟuḷi maturiyum vaṭamaturi nōkkit teṉmaturiyeṉappaṭṭatu. ākavē, vaṭa teṉ eṉpaṉa uṟaviyal aṭikaḷē eṉa aṟika.
matu eṉṉum ōr aracaṉ peyarāl avaṉ talinakar matupuri eṉappaṭṭateṉṟum, atu piṉṉar maturi - matrā  eṉṟu tirintateṉṟum, kūṟuvatu poruntātu. 
māyaṉi maṉṉum vaṭamaturi mintaṉi  (tiv.tiruppā. 5)
eṉṟu āṇṭāḷ teṉmaturiyoṭu oppunōkkiyē pāṭutal kāṇka.
pāṇṭiyaṉ kaṭalkōṭkuppiṉ paṉimali ceṉṟu ataṉ mēṟ kayaṟpoṟi poṟittu, kaṅkiyāṟṟaṅkari nakaril taṉ uṟaviṉa ṉit tuṇiyariyaṉāka amarttiviṭṭu vantatu pōṉṟē, cōḻaṉum tāṉ vēntaṉāṉapiṉ paṉimalimēṟ pulippoṟi poṟittu, kaṅkināṭṭi āḷumāṟu oru paṭinikarāḷiyi amartti viṭṭu vantāṉ. iṅṅaṉam, pāṇṭiyar kuṭiyāṉa tiṅkaḷ marapum cōḻar kuṭiyāṉa katiravaṉ marapum vaṭanāṭṭil niṟuvappeṟṟaṉa.
vaṭanāṭu moḻipeyar tēyamāyiṉum, tamiḻarum aṅkuk kuṭiyiruntataṉālum, tirimoḻiyāḷarkkum tamiḻ ōraḷavu viḷaṅkiyataṉālum, kaṟṟār aṉivarkkum tamiḻē ilakkiya moḻiyāyiruntamiyālum, cēra cōḻa pāṇṭiyam pōlum vallaracu aṅkiṉmiyālum, cillāyiram āṇṭukaḷ tamiḻaracu aṅkuc cevvaṉ naṭipeṟṟatu.
talikkaḻakattiṟ pōṉṟē, iṭikkaḻakattilum munnāṭṭiyuñ cērnta tamiḻakat talimip pulavarellārum kalantiruntu, mutunūlāyntum putunūliyaṟṟiyum vantaṉar.
pāṇṭiya nāṭṭiṉ perumpakutiyi ēṟkeṉavē kaṭal koṇṭa taṉālum, katavapuram kaṭal vāyiliruntataṉālum, āḻ kaṭaliṟ cellum nāvāyum vaṅkamum pōṉṟa peruṅkalaṅkaḷ tamiḻakat tiṟ puṇarkkappaṭṭataṉālum, pāṇṭiyaṉ muṉ viḻippāyin tatu, maṟukaṭalkōḷ nēriṉ kuṭumpattoṭu tappumāṟu, oru peruṅkalatti eṉṟum aṇiyamāy vittiruttal vēṇṭum.
iraṇṭām kaṭalkōḷ
tamiḻilakkiyattiṟ iraṇṭām kaṭal kōḷ, tōrā. ki.mu.2500-il nikaḻntiruttal vēṇṭum. akkaṭalkōḷāl, nākanāṭu eṉṟu collappaṭum kīḻttici nilappakuti, ēṟattāḻa 1200 kal tolivu parappuḷḷatu mūḻkip pōyiṟṟu. atuvē,
tīṅkaṉi nāva lōṅkumit tīviṭi
yiṉṟēḻ nāḷi lirunila mākkaḷ
niṉṟunaṭuk keyta nīṇila vēntē
pūminaṭuk kuṟūum pōḻtat tinnakar
nākanaṉ ṉāṭṭu nāṉūṟi yōcaṉi
viyaṉpā talattu vīḻntukē ṭeytum.
eṉṟu maṇimēkaliyil (9:17-22), muṉṉaṟivippup pōṟ kūṟappaṭṭa piṉṉaṟivuppuc ceyti.
katavapuramum kumariyāṟṟiṟkut teṉpāl nilamum mūḻkip pōyiṉa. ilaṅki intiyāviṉiṉṟum pirintuviṭṭatu. vaṅkak kuṭākkaṭal, putitākat tōṉṟiyataṉāl, tolkati (purāṇa) muṟiyil cakararoṭu toṭarpupaṭuttit toṭukaṭal eṉap paṭṭatu. toṭutal tōṇṭutal.
vaṭāatu paṉipaṭu neṭuvari vaṭakkun
teṉāa turukeḻu kumariyiṉ teṟkuṅ
kuṇāatu kariporu toṭukaṭaṟ kuṇakkuṅ
kuṭāatu toṉṟumutir puvattiṉ kuṭakkum
eṉṟu (puṟam.6), kārikiḻār pāṭutal kāṇka. urukeḻu kumari eṉpataṟku, uṭkun tiṟam poruntiya kaṉṉiyāṟu eṉṟē paḻiya uriyāciriyar kūṟuvatiyum nōkkuka.
kumariyāṟirukkavum ataṉ kayavāyilirunta katavapuram mūḻkiyatu, kāviriyāṟirukkavum ataṉ kayavāyilirunta kāvirip pūmpaṭṭiṉam mūḻkiyatu pōlām.
kōvalaṉ kālattilēyē vaṅkakkaṭal iruntataṉāltāṉ avaṉ māmaṉ mānāykaṉ (mānāvikaṉ) nīrvāṇikat talivaṉā yirukkavum, cātuvaṉ kalattiṟ ceṉṟu kīḻittīvukaḷuṭaṉ vāṇikañ ceyyavum, iyaṉṟatu.
kīḻttici nāṭṭār nāka vaṇakkamum nākamuttiriyuṅ koṇṭiruntataṉāl, nākar eṉappaṭṭār. avarnāṭu nākanāṭu eṉappaṭṭatu. avaruḷ nākarikarum anākarikarumāka irucār māntarum iruntaṉar.
kīḻnila maruṅkiṉ nākanā ṭāḷum
iruvar maṉṉavar (maṇi. 9:55)
eṉṟum,
nāka nāṭu naṭukkiṉ ṟāḷpavaṉ
vāki vēlōṉ vaḷivaṇaṉ   (maṇi. 24:54-5)
eṉṟum, kuṟikkappaṭṭavar nākarika makkaḷ.
nakka cāraṇar nākar   eṉṟum (maṇi.16:15)
nakka cāraṇar nayamilar tōṉṟi

ūṉuṭi yivvuṭam puṇaveṉ ṟeḻuppalum
eṉṟum (maṇi. 56-59), kuṟikkappaṭṭavar naravūṉuṇṇikaḷāṉa vilaṅkāṇṭi mākkaḷ.
vaṅkak kaṭal tōṉṟumuṉ aṅkirunta nilapparappil vāḻnta māntar, kīḻikkari neṭukalum kuṭiyēṟiyataṉāl, avar cērnta vūrkaḷ nākarkōvil, nākūr, nākappaṭṭiṉam, nākapuri eṉap peyarpeṟṟaṉa.
aṅkametir eṉṉuñ civatarumōttirac ceyyuḷiṉ (kōpura. 48) ciṟappuriyil kaṉakamayamāṉa ilaṅkiyeṉṉun tēcam, iratṉapūmiyuṭaṉ kūṭiyiññūṟu yōcaṉi camuttirattukkuṭ pukuntirukkumeṉka. eṉṉuṅ kuṟippum,
cēṇṭoṭar cimiyat teyva makēntirat
tumparc ceṉṟāṉ   (makēn.25)
kīṇṭatu vēli naṉṉīr kīḻuṟak kiṭanta nākar
vēṇṭiya vulaka meṅkum veḷippaṭa maṇikaḷ miṉṉa  (kaṭaltāvu. 21)
eṉṉum kamparāmāyaṇa aṭikaḷum, iṅkuk kavaṉikkattakkaṉa. āriyat tolkati mayakkiṉāl, iṭikkālap perum pulavarum, nākar eṉṉum māntariṉattārip pāmpiṉamākak karutiviṭṭaṉar. kīḻnāṭu eṉpatiyum kiḻakku nāṭeṉṟu koḷḷātu, nilattiṟkum nīrukkum kīḻuḷḷa nāṭeṉṟu koṇṭuviṭṭaṉar.
kaṭalkōḷ nikaḻntavuṭaṉ, pāṇṭiyaṉ muṉṉarē tāṉ aṇiyamāka vittirunta peruṅkalattilēṟi veḷḷattik kaṭantu, koṟkittuṟippakkam vantu cērntiruttal vēṇṭum. ataṉpiṉ, kaṭalkōṭkut tappiya taṉ kuṭikaḷ vāḻvataṟku, cōḻa nāṭṭilum cēra nāṭṭilum teṉkōṭip pakutikaḷi veṉṟu koṭuttāṉ eṉpatu,
malitiri yūrntutaṉ maṇkaṭal vuvaliṉ
meliviṉṟi mēṟceṉṟu mēvārnā ṭiṭampaṭap
puliyoṭu viṉṉīkkip pukaḻpoṟitta kiḷarkeṇṭi
valiyiṉāṉ vaṇakkiya vāṭāccīrt teṉṉavaṉ.
eṉṉum mullik kalit (104) taravāl aṟiyak kiṭakkiṉṟatu. mēvār nāṭu, valiyiṉāṉ vaṇakkiya eṉṉuṅ kuṟippuk kaḷāl, cēra cōḻar nilantara iṇaṅkāmiyum pāṇṭiyaṉ porutu veṉṟatum, aṟiyappaṭum.
aṭiyiṟ ṟaṉṉaḷavu .............. teṉṉavaṉ vāḻi, eṉṉum cilappati kārap pakutiyuriyil, aṅṅaṉamākiya nilakkuṟikkuc cōḻa nāṭṭelliyilē muttūrk kūṟṟamum cēramāṉāṭṭuk kuṇṭūrk kūṟṟamu meṉṉu mivaṟṟi, iḻanta nāṭṭiṟkāka vāṇṭa teṉṉavaṉ vāḻvāṉāka veṉṟavāṟu. eṉṟu aṭiyārkku nallār varintiruppatu, iraṇṭām kaṭal kōṭkup piṟpaṭṭa ceytiyiyē kuṟikkum.
mutaṟ kaṭalkōṭkuppiṉ nikaḻntatu pōṉṟē, iraṇṭām kaṭalkōṭkup piṉṉum, teṉṉāṭṭu makkaḷ vaṭaticiyum vaṭanāṭṭu makkaḷ vaṭamēlit ticiyum paravic ceṉṟaṉar. kaṭalkōṭ ceyti pāpilōṉiyum eṭṭiṟṟu.
mutaṟ kaṭalkōḷ māpēraḷaviṉatāyiruntu ñālattiṉ mēṟ puṟattip palaviṭattum mika māṟṟiyiruppiṉum, atu mikap paḻaṅkālattatātaliṉ, atu paṟṟiya ceyti piṟanāṭṭu varalāṟu kaḷil iṭam peṟavilli. āyiṉ, iraṇṭām kaṭalkōṭ ceyti, mutaṟkaṇ pāpilōṉiya nāṭṭilum piṉṉar yūtēyā vilum ataṉpiṉ piṟanāṭukaḷilum paravi, avvan nāṭṭuc ceytiyāka nilittu viṭṭatu. pāpilōṉiyāvil. ki.mu.2000 āṇṭukaṭku muṉpē, kaṭalkōḷ pōṉṟa oru toṭarmaḻi veḷḷak kati vaḻaṅki vantirukkiṉṟatu. atiyē yūtar koṇṭu kūṟiyatākak terikiṉṟatu.
teṉ kaltēyat talinakarākiya ūr eṉṉum pāpilōṉiya nāṭṭup pērūril, āpirakām ki.mu. 1996-il piṟantāṉ. avaṉ marapil vanta mōcē (ki.mu.1571-1451), eḻutiṉatākac collap paṭum tiruppottakam (Bible), paṭippiyal (Genesis) 7-ām atikārattil uḷḷa veḷḷak kati, pāpilōṉiyak katikku ēṟattāḻa 500 āṇṭu piṟpaṭṭatu.
veḷḷakkati vaḻaṅkum nāṭukaḷilellām, kalattiṉ vāyi lākak kaṭalkōṭkut tappiya pāṇṭiyaṉ pōl ovvoruvaṉ collappaṭukiṉṟāṉ. kaltēyar (pāpilōṉiya) nāṭṭil cicutturacu (Xisuthrus) allatu acicu atarā (Hasis adra); yūta nāṭṭil nōvā (Nova); kirēkka nāṭṭil ocīcecu (Ogyges) allatu tiyūkkēliyaṉ (Deucalion); cīṉa nāṭṭil pōki (Fohi). piṟkālattil vēta āriyar intiyāviṟku vantapiṉ ōraḷavu uṇmiyaṟintataṉāl, cattiyavirataṉ eṉṉum tirāviṭa pati eṉṟaṉar.
palvēṟu nāṭukaḷiṟ kaṭalkōṭku allatu veḷḷattiṟkuk kuṟikkappaṭṭa kālam-
	nāṭu	kālam
	pāpilōṉiyā	tōrā	.	ki.mu. 3000-itaṟkuc caṟṟu muṉpu.
	yūtēyā (Bible)			  	2348
	ilaṅki			  	2387
	tamiḻakam	tōrā.		  	2500
oyaṉṉecu pōṉṟē, palar kiḻakkiṉiṉṟu pāracīkak kuṭāk kaṭal vaḻiyākap piṉṉar iṭiyiṭṭiṭiyiṭṭu vantatāka, pāpi lōṉiyac ceṅkaṟ paṭṭayaṅkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉa.
tiruppottakam (Bible), paṭippiyal (Genesis), 6-ām atikāram mutaliru tirumoḻikaḷ, māntar ñālattiṉmēṟ perukat toṭaṅki, avarkaṭku makaḷir piṟantapōtu, tēvakumarar māntar makaḷiri mika aḻakuḷḷavareṉṟu kaṇṭu, avarkaḷuḷ tamakkup peṇkaḷit terintu koṇṭārkaḷ. eṉṟiruppatu, kumari nāṭṭiṉiṉṟu nākarika vaḷarcciyillā niliyil mēṉāṭu ceṉṟa māntar vaḻiyiṉar, nāḷaṭivil veḷḷiyarāki, piṟkālattil aṅkuc ceṉṟa nākarika māntariṉ makaḷiri maṇantu koṇṭa ṉar eṉpatiyē, kuṟikkum.
atē atikāram 4-am tirumoḻiyil, akkālattil arakkar ñālattiliruntaṉar eṉpatu, āppirikka māntar akkālatti lum karuttum paruttum iruntatiyē kāṭṭum.
iṉi, 7-ām 8-ām atikāraṅkaḷil veḷḷac ceytiyiyum, 
9-ām 10-ām atikāraṅkaḷil nōvāviṉ marapup perukkatti yum, kūṟiyapiṉ, 11-ām atikāram 2-ām tirumoḻi, makkaḷ kiḻakkē yiruntu vaḻinaṭantu varukiyil, ciṉeyār nāṭṭiṟ camanilattik kaṇṭu, aṅkē kuṭiyiruntaṉar. eṉṟiruppatu, kaṭalkōṭkuppiṉ, nāvalan tēyattiṉiṉṟu oru kūṭṭattār nila vaḻiyāka mēliyāciyā ceṉṟu taṅkiṉar eṉpatiyē, terivikkum.
kaṭalkōḷaccattiṉāl maṭṭumaṉṟi, vāṇikañ ceytaṟkum, tamiḻar palar vaṭakkē ceṉṟu kaṅki nāṭṭiṟ kuṭiyēṟiṉar.
kuṟiñcit teyvamākiya cēyōṉ vaṇakkattiṉiṉṟē civaneṟi tirintataṉāl, civaṉukkum maliyakamē ciṟanta irukkiyākak koḷḷappaṭṭatu. paṉimali kaṭalaṭiyiṉiṉṟeḻunta piṉpum, kumarimaliyiṉ perumi kuṉṟātiruntataṉāl, aḵtiruntavari pāṇṭiyaṉ perumitattōṭiruntāṉ. aḵtu mūḻkiyapiṉ, avaṉ taṉ nāṭṭiṉ ciṟumiyiyum tāḻviyum nīkkuvataṟkāka maṭṭumaṉṟi, taṉ kuṭi toṉṟu toṭṭu vaḻipaṭṭuvanta civaṉukku oru takunta irukki yamikkavumē, paṉimaliyik kikkoṇṭāṉ. ammaliyiṉ mēṟpakuti veṇpaṉikkaṭṭi mūṭi eṉṟum veṇṇiṟamāyt tōṉṟuvatāl, veḷḷimali yeṉappaṭṭatu. ataṉ koṭumuṭiyē civaṉirukkiyākavum maṇṇulakap pēriṉpa niliyamākavum, civaneṟiyārāṟ karutap peṟṟatu. pāṇṭiyar paṉmuṟi paṉimalimēṟ kayaṟpoṟi poṟittatu, vēttiyal maṭṭumiṉṟit tēviyal toṭarpuṅ koṇṭatākum. ikkālat tamiḻakac civamaṭaṅkaḷum veḷḷi malit toṭarpu kūṟutal kāṇka.
civaṉaṭiyārkkuc ciṟanta akkamaṇi (uruttirākkam), paṉimali yaṭivārattuḷḷa nēpāḷa nāṭṭilēyē toṉṟu toṭṭu viḷikiṉṟatu. paḵṟuḷiyāṟu mūḻkiya piṉ kaṅkiyāṟē nāvalan tēyap pēriyāṟāṉataṉālum, veḷḷimaliyit taṉ kuṭumi yākak koṇṭa paṉimaliyiṉiṉṟu atu tōṉṟi varuvatālum, civaneṟiyārkku atuvē taliciṟanta tirunīrniliyāyiṟṟu. itaṉāl, camayappaṟṟālum tamiḻar palar, ciṟappākap peruñ celvar, kaṅki nāṭṭiṟ kuṭiyēṟiṉar. ataṉ viḷivākat tirukkētāram vāraṇāci (kāci) mutaliya civanakarkaḷ vaṭa nāṭṭil tōṉṟiṉa.
kāvirip putalvar eṉpatupōl, tamiḻaka vēḷāḷaruṭ cilar tammik kaṅki kulattār eṉṟu collik koṇṭaṉar. kaṅkiyammaṉ eṉṉum nāṭṭuppuṟat teyvamum tōṉṟiṟṟu.
civaneṟiyārkku nēpāḷattiṉiṉṟu akkamaṇi varuvatu pōṉṟē, aracarkkum teyvap paṭimikaṭkum vīca veṇkavari yum, kūntalillāp peṇṭir koṇṭi muṭikkak karuṅkavari yum, paṉimali yaṭivārattiṉiṉṟum tipēttiṉiṉṟum vantiruk kiṉṟaṉa.
naranti naṟumpul mēynta kavari
kuvaḷip piñcuṉi paruki yayala
takarat taṇṇiḻaṟ piṇiyoṭu vatiyum
vaṭatici yatuvē vāṉṟō yimayam (puṟam.132)
eṉpataṉāl, kavarimayir tarum erumi paṉimali yaṭivārattiṟ pulmēyntatit tamiḻar kaṇṇārak kaṇṭami aṟiyappaṭum.
kavari muccik kārviri kūntal
ūcal mēvaṟ cēyiḻi makaḷir (patiṟ.43:1-2)
eṉpatu, paṇṭikkālap peṇṭirum kavarimuṭi yaṇintatit terivikkum.
kavarimā eṉṉum erumikkuk kiyākku (gyak) eṉpatu tipēttil vaḻaṅkum peyar. āṅkilattil atu yākku (yak) eṉṟum, tamiḻil āku eṉṟum tiriyum.
āku kavari cīkaram cavari.  (piṅ. 8:135).
itil, āku eṉpatu mutalākupeyarāka muṭiyik kuṟittatu.
kavarimā oruvaki erumiyātalāl, kallāṭar tamiḻnāṭṭu erumiyiyum kavari eṉṟu kuṟittuviṭṭār.
paṭintucē ṭeṟiyuñ ceṅkaṭ kavariyum  (kallā. 53:30).
itip piṅkalamum kārāṉ makiṭam kaviri kārā eṉṟu piṉpaṟṟi viṭṭatu.
vaṇikar maṭṭumaṉṟi, aṭiyārum pulavarum aracarum paṭi maṟavarum aṭikkaṭi vaṭanāṭu ceṉṟu vantataṉāl, annāṭṭu iyaṟkiyamippum araciyalum makkaḷ vāḻkki muṟiyum ciṟappu nikaḻccikaḷum, tamiḻarkkut terintu ilakkiyattilum kuṟikkappaṭṭaṉa.
kaṅki cintāṟukaḷum, cōṇi, vāraṇi, aci, toḻuṉi mutaliya kaṅkik kiḷikaḷum paṇṭit tamiḻp pulavarkkut terintiruntaṉa.
kaṅki nāṭṭil mutaṟkaṇ kumarināṭṭiṉiṉṟu ceṉṟa tamiḻarē kuṭiyiruntamiyāl, ūrppeyarkaḷ perumpālum ūr, nakar, puram, puri eṉat tamiḻīṟē peṟṟaṉa. mutaliṟ puram eṉpatu kōpuramuḷḷa nakariyum puri eṉpatu kōṭṭiyuḷḷa nakariyuṅ kuṟittu, piṉṉarp potu vīṟukaḷāyiṉa. puram=uyarvu, uyarnta kaṭṭiṭam. puri=vaḷivu, vaḷinta (cūḻnta) kōṭṭi. pātiripuram eṉpatu piṟkālattil pāṭalipura eṉat tirintatu. kāḷivaṇakkaṅ koṇṭa tamiḻar cilar, vaṅkattiṟ kuṭiyēṟik kaṅkik kariyiṟ kāḷi kōviluṭaṉ amitta nakarē kāḷikkōṭṭam. kāḷi (pālinilat) tamiḻt teyvam. kāḷi kōṭṭam eṉṉum iraṇṭum tūya teṉcol. kāḷik kōṭṭam eṉpatu, iṉṟu āṅkilac col vaḻiyākak kalkattā eṉat tirintuḷḷatu. kaṅkik kayavāy aṭuttut tamluk allatu tamulk eṉṉum peyar koṇṭuḷḷa tuṟi nakarp peyar, tamiḻakam eṉṉum colliṉ tiripāyirukkalām. tamalitti eṉṟu pālimoḻiyilum, tamralipti eṉṟu camaṟkirutattilum vaḻaṅkum iṭappeyar, tamiḻnatti allatu tamiḻulātti eṉpatu pōṉṟa teṉcolliṉ tiripā yirukkalām.
vēmpāy (Bombay) mānilap pakuti muḻutum patiṉeṇkuṭi vēḷir paraviyiruntataṉāl, vēḷakam eṉappaṭṭatu.
kaṇṇaṉ āṇṭakuccaranāṭṭu mēṟpakutiyiṉ talinakarp peyarāṉa tuvāraki eṉṉum col, tuvari eṉṉum tamiḻccolliṉ tiripē. erumi (micūr) nāṭṭut tuvari nakaril ki.pi.2-ām nūṟṟāṇṭu āṇṭukoṇṭirunta iruṅkō vēḷ, taṉ āḷkuṭiyiṉ 49-ām talimuṟiyiṉaṉ eṉṟu collappaṭuvatāl, avaṉ kuṭi mutalvaṉ kaṇṇaṉ kālatta vaṉākavē yiruntiruttal vēṇṭum. atu ki.mu.1000.
cempupuṉin tiyaṟṟiya cēṇeṭum purici
yuvarā vīkit tuvari yāṇṭu
nāṟpat toṉpatu vaḻimuṟi vanta
vēḷiruḷ vēḷē ............................. (puṟam. 261)
eṉṟu iruṅkōvēḷ kapilarāl viḷikkap peṟṟamiyiyum, avaṉ vēḷ eṉṟu peyar peṟṟiruntamiyiyum nōkkuka.
oṭṭara (Orissa) nāṭṭut talinakariṉ peyarāṉa kaṭakam (Cuttack) eṉṉum col, kōṭṭi matilik kuṟikkum tūya teṉ collē. matilāṟ cūḻappaṭṭataṉāl annakar kaṭakam eṉap peyar peṟṟatu.
iṉṟu teluṅku nāṭṭup pakutikaḷāka virukkum nellūr kuṇṭūr māvaṭṭaṅkaḷ, muṉṉart tamiḻnilamā yiruntati, avaṟṟiṉ peyarkaḷē terivikkum. nellūr māvaṭṭattūṭu ōṭum vaṭapeṇṇiyāṟu, tamiḻnāṭṭuc teṉpeṇṇi yāṟṟuṭaṉ oppunōkki yiṭappeṟṟa peyar koṇṭateṉpatu, collāmalē aṟiyappaṭum.
karunāṭakam (kaṉṉaṭam), tuḷu, kuṭakam mutaliya nāṭukaḷ kaṭik kaḻakak kālattilum tamiḻnilaṅkaḷākavē yiruntaṉa.
pāṇṭiyaṉ veṟṟic ceyal
vāṉiyinta virumunnīrp
pēeniliiya virumpuvattuk
koṭumpuṇari vilaṅkupōḻak
kaṭuṅkāloṭu karicēra
neṭuṅkoṭimici yitiyeṭut
tiṉṉiciya muracamuḻaṅkap
poṉmalinta viḻuppaṇṭa
nāṭāra naṉkiḻitarum
āṭiyaṟ perunāvāy
maḻimuṟṟiya malipuriyat
tuṟimuṟṟiya tuḷaṅkirukkit
teṇkaṭaṟ kuṇṭakaḻic
cīrcāṉṟa vuyarnelliṉ
ūrkoṇṭa vuyarkoṟṟava  (maturik. 75-88)
eṉṟu, māṅkuṭi marutaṉār taliyālaṅkāṉattuc ceru veṉṟa neṭuñceḻiyaṉi, avaṉ muṉṉōruḷ oruvaṉ ceyta veṟṟic ceyali avaṉmēlēṟṟikkūṟi viḷittār. avveṟṟic ceyal, kaṭalkaṭantu ceṉṟu, cāvakam eṉṉum cālit tīvik kip paṟṟiyatākum.
cāli eṉpatu cennel eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum teṉcol, piṟkālattiṟ pāṇṭiyaṉoṭu ceṉṟa pirāmaṇap pūcakaṉ oruvaṉ, cāli eṉpatu oru tavacap peyarāyiruttalāl, ati vaṭamoḻiyil yava eṉṟu moḻipeyarttāṉ. atu piṉṉar java-jāva eṉat tirintu tamiḻiṟ cāvakam eṉṉum vaṭivu koṇṭatu. cālittīviṉ talinakar cāliyūr.
cālittīvip pāṇṭiyaṉ kippaṟṟiyapiṉ, tamiḻar aṅkuc ceṉṟu kuṭiyēṟiṉar. ataṉāl, attīviṉ pala pirivuppeyarkaḷ iṉṟum pāṇṭiyaṉ, matiyaṉ, pukār, pāṇṭiya vācam, maliyaṉkō, kantaḷi, cempūṭcēy eṉṟu tamiḻppeyarkaḷē koṇṭu viḷaṅkukiṉṟaṉa eṉṟum; mīṉaṉ kāppu eṉṉumiṭat tuḷḷa maliyar, tam muṉṉōr intiyāviṉiṉṟu vantatākak kūṟukiṉṟaṉar eṉṟum; keriṉi (Gerini) eṉṉum āciriyar, aṅku vaḻaṅkum maliyaṉ kōlaṉ (Maleon Kolan) eṉṉuṅkuṭip peyari, maliyar cōḻar eṉṉum tamiḻaracar kuṭippeyar kaḷuṭaṉ iṇittuk kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉar eṉṟum; tiruviciyam (srī vijaya) eṉṉum ariyat talivaṉukkuc cuṟavakkoṭiyum tirumāṟa vicayōttuṅkaṉ (srī māṟa vijayōttuṅkaṉ) eṉṉum peyarum uṇṭeṉṟum; rā.rākaviyaṅkār tām eḻutiyuḷḷa tamiḻ varalāṟu eṉṉum nūliṟ kūṟiyuḷḷār. (pak.338-9)
mīṉaṉ mīṉavaṉ; atāvatu mīṉakkoṭiyuṭiya pāṇṭiyaṉ, mīṉaṉ kāppu eṉpatu pāṇṭiyaṉ kāvaluḷḷa iṭam eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum.
tamiḻar paṭippaṭiyākap pakkattut tīvukaḷilum nilaṅkaḷilum paraviṉatākat terikiṉṟatu. cālikku vaṭakkiḻakkil oru ciṟu cīvu maturā eṉṟum, vaṭamēṟkil oru peruntīvu cumaturā (Sumatra) eṉṟum, vaṭakkil oru māperun tīvu poruniyō (Borneo) eṉṟum, cumaturāviṟku vaṭakkiluḷḷa tīvakkuṟi maliyā (Malaya) eṉṟum, peyar peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.
poruni (tāmpara paraṇi) eṉpatu pāṇṭi nāṭṭu āṟṟup peyar. maliyam eṉpatu potiyalip peyar. cumaturā eṉpataṉ muṉṉoṭṭum ciṅkapuram (Singapore) eṉṉum tīvup peyarum, āriyac cārpāl ēṟpaṭṭaṉavākum. puram eṉṉum īṟu tamiḻ.
iṭiccoṟkaḷ
(1) olik kuṟippukkaḷ
	e-ṭu : tum, tup (tuntupi)
(2) viḷiyolikaḷ
	e-ṭu : ū, ē, ō - hū, hē, hō
(3) uṇarccik kuṟippolikaḷ
	e-ṭu : viyappu - ā - hā, ō, hāhā
	makiḻcci - hō
	irakkam - ā, ō
	nōvu - ā, ī, ā-hā, (um-) hum
	niṉivu - ē, ō
	kaḻivu varuttam - ā
	ikaḻcci = ē, (um-) hum
	uṭaṉpāṭu-(um-) hum  (va.va.)
itaḻvaki
alli akavitaḻ;
pulli puṟavitaḻ;
itaḻ ciṟiyatu;
maṭal periyatu.  (col : 67)
iteṉṉa mūṭṭi? viḷiyāṭṭu
pala piḷḷikaḷ kūṭiṉaviṭattu, oru periya piḷḷi piṟari yellām vaṭṭamāka irutti, avarkaḷ kikaḷi virittu nilattiṉmēṟ kuppuṟa vikkac ceytu, oru maraputtoṭarc coṟkaḷit taṉittaṉi colli ovvoru collālum ovvoru kiyic cuṭṭi, iṟutic collāṟ kuṟikkappaṭṭa piḷḷiyi uṅkaḷ appaṉ pēr eṉṉa! eṉṟu kēṭkum. ataṟku appiḷḷi muruṅkippū eṉṉum. piṉpu ap periya piḷḷi muruṅkippū tiṉṟavaṉē? (ḷē!) muḷḷān taṇṇīr kuṭittavaṉē! (ḷē!) pāmpukki paṭakkeṉṟu eṭuttuk koḷ eṉṟu collum. muruṅkippū..... kuṭittavaṉē! (ḷē) eṉṉum pakutiyāl, colluk koruvarāka nāṉku piḷḷikaḷ cuṭṭappeṟum. kuṭittavaṉē! (ḷē) eṉṟu muṭiyum piḷḷi, uṭaṉē oru kiyi eṭuttup piṉṉāl vittukkoḷḷa vēṇṭum. kuṭittavaṉē! (ḷē) eṉṟu iraṇṭām muṟi muṭiyum piḷḷi, iṉṉorukiyiyum eṭuttup piṉṉāl vittuk koḷḷavēṇṭum. iṅṅaṉam tirumpat tirumpac ceyyiṉ, iṟutiyil oru piḷḷi akappaṭṭuk koḷḷum.
appiḷḷi taṉ irukikaḷiyum maṭakki oṉṟaṉmēloṉ ṟāyk kīḻēvikka, maṟṟap piḷḷikaḷum tam kikaḷi avvāṟē avaṟṟiṉmēl aṭukki vippar.
periya piḷḷi kīḻē cāṇipōṭṭu meḻukalāmā? maṇ pōṭṭu meḻukalāmā? eṉṟu kēṭṭu, piṟar cāṇipōṭṭu meḻuku eṉṟāl, taṉ akaṅkiyāl aṭikkiyiṉ kīḻp pūcuvatupōl taṭava vēṇṭum; maṇpōṭṭu meḻuku eṉṟāl, puṟaṅkiyāl avvāṟu ceyyavēṇṭum.
piṉpu mīṇṭum, tīṭṭiṉa kattiyil veṭṭalāmā? tīṭṭāta kattiyil veṭṭalāmā? eṉṟu periya piḷḷi kēṭṭu, tīṭṭiṉa kattiyil eṉṟāl aintuviralum nerukki nīṭṭip pakkavāṭṭālum, tīṭṭāta kattiyil eṉṟāl maṇikkip pakkattālum veṭṭa vēṇṭum.
iṉi, veṭṭuñ ceyalpaṟṟi, muṟkūṟiya viṉāviṟkup patilāka, aṭiyil veṭṭaṭṭumā? nuṉiyil veṭṭaṭṭumā? eṉṟu kēṭṭu. aṭiyil veṭṭu eṉṟāl kuttukkiyaṭukkiṉ aṭiyilum, nuṉiyil veṭṭu eṉṟāl ataṉ nuṉiyilum veṭṭuvatumuṇṭu.
piṉpu mīṇṭum muṉmuṟippaṭi kikaḷi aṭukki vikka vēṇṭum. periya piḷḷi ovvoru kiyāyt toṭṭu iteṉṉa mūṭṭi? iteṉṉa mūṭṭi? eṉṟu kēṭkum. piṟar arici mūṭṭi, paruppu mūṭṭi, puḷi mūṭṭi. uppu mūṭṭi eṉa ovvoru carakkiṉ peyarāṟ kūṟuvar. ovvoru mūṭṭiyilum koñcaṅ koñcan tāruṅkaḷ eṉṟu periya piḷḷi kēṭkum. piṟar taramāṭṭōm eṉpar. ataṉāṟ periya piḷḷi, ellāriyum kaṇṇi mūṭik koṇṭu kuppuṟak kaviḻntukoḷḷac colvāḷ. avar aṅṅaṉañ ceytapiṉ, uṅkaḷ appā varukiṟārkaḷ, uṅkaḷ ammā varukiṟārkaḷ eṉṟu ēmāṟṟi yāriyēṉum eḻa vittu, appiḷḷikku oru kuṟippiṭṭa tokiyāṉa aṭi periyapiḷḷi koṭukkum. oru varum ēmāṟi eḻāviṭiṉ, viḷiyāṭṭup pōtum, eḻuntiruṅkaḷ eṉṟu colli, ellōrum eḻuntapiṉ ellārkkum aṭikoṭukkum. itōṭu āṭṭam muṭiyum.
intiya nākarikam āriyarateṉak kāṭṭak kiyāḷappaṭum vaḻikaḷ
1.	paḻantamiḻnāṭākiya kumarikkaṇṭa varalāṟṟi maṟittalum maṟuttalum.
2.	pāṇṭiyar niṟuviya muttamiḻk kaḻaka uṇmiyi maṟuttal.
3.	tamiḻnāṭṭu varalāṟṟi vaṭakkiṉiṉṟum vētak kālatti ṉiṉṟum toṭaṅkal.
4.	teṉṉāṭṭup paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷākiya tamiḻari vantēṟi kaḷākavum kalaviyiṉamākavuṅ kāṭṭal.
5.	tamiḻ muṉṉūlkaḷiyum mutaṉūlkaḷiyum piṉṉūlkaḷāka vum vaḻi nūlkaḷākavum kāṭṭal.
6.	kumarikkaṇṭa iṭappeyarkaḷiyum teyvap peyarkaḷi yum mūvēntar kuṭippeyarkaḷiyum āriyac collākak kāṭṭal.
7.	kaṭṭukkatikaḷiyum āriyac coṟkaḷiyum pukutti, iruperun tamiḻc camayaṅkaḷākiya civaneṟiyiyum tirumāl neṟiyiyum āriya vaṇṇamākkalum, tamiḻik kōyil vaḻi pāṭṭiṟkut takāta teṉṟu taḷḷalum.
8.	mūvēntar pētimiyāl āriyam vērūṉṟiya kaṭikkaḻakak kāla nūlkaḷiṉiṉṟu āriyac cārpāṉa cāṉṟu kāṭṭal.
9.	tamiḻ vaṭamoḻikkiḷiyeṉṟu ayalār karutumāṟu, aṭippaṭit tamiḻc coṟkaṭkellām valintum nalintum vaṭacoṉ mūlaṅ kāṭṭal.
10.	vaṭamoḻi tēvamoḻiyeṉṟum, pirāmaṇar nilattēvar (pūcurar) eṉṟum, vētakkāla āriyap pūcāriyarāl pukuttap paṭṭa ēmāṟṟuk karuttukkaḷiyum, piṟapput toṭarpāṉa kulappiriviṉiyiyum, toṭarntu pōṟṟal.
11.	camayaccārpāṉa coṟpoḻivukaḷāṟ potu makkaḷi aṟiyāmiyil amiḻttutal.
12.	uṇmi kūṟum tamiḻp pulavarkku aluvaṟpataviyillāvāṟu ceytal.



intiya nākarikam tamiḻaratē yātal
vēta āriyar mēlayāciyāviṉiṉṟu intiyāviṟkuṭ pukunta kālam ki.mu.2000-1500. aṉṟu avar kaṉṟu kālikaḷi ōṭṭit tiriyum nāṭōṭikaḷāyum, mulli nākarika niliyiṉarāyumē yiruntaṉar.
avarukku ilakkiyamumilli; eḻuttumilli. avar pēciya moḻi kirēkkattiṟku iṉamāyum paḻampāra cīkattiṟku mika neruṅkiyatāyum colvaḷamaṟṟa tāyum iruntatu intiyāviṟ kālūṉṟiya piṉṉarē, avar irukku eṉṉum tam mutal vētatti yāttaṉar. avvēta moḻi vaṭintiyap pirākirutat tiyum tiraviṭattiyum taḻuviyateṉpatu atil ekara okarak kuṟiliṉmiyālum pala tamiḻc coṟkaḷuṇmi yālum teriyavarukiṉṟatu.
tamiḻiṉ toṉmiyiyum muṉmiyiyum aṟiyāta varalāṟ ṟāciriyar, āriya vētakkālattiyē aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭu tamiḻnāṭṭu varalāṟṟit toṭaṅkum tavaṟāṉa vaḻakkam iṉṉum iruntu varukiṉṟatu. tamiḻ, vēta āriyattiṟku muntiya tāyiruppatōṭu vētap peyarkaḷē tamiḻc coṟkaḷiṉ tiripākavumirukkiṉṟaṉa.
	vētam :viḻittal pārttal, kāṇutal, aṟital. viḻi = aṟivu, ōti (ñāṉam). tēṟār viḻiyilā māntar (tirumantiram, 177)
			viḻi - L.Vide-Vise. Skt. Vid-Veda.
o.nō : kuḻal - kuṭal, oṭi - oci.
	curuti	:	ceviyuṟutal - kēṭṭal.
			ceviyuṟu-cru-cruti = kēḷvi.
			muṟkālattil eḻutappeṟātu kēṭṭēyaṟiyap paṭṭuvanta āriya maṟi.
	maṇṭalam	:	mul-muṉ-muṉi-vil.
			mul-mur-muri. murital = vaḷital.
			muri-mūri = vaḷivu.
			mur-muru-muruku = vaḷinta kātaṇi.
			muru-muṟu-muṟuku. muṟukutal = vaḷital; tirukutal.
			muṟuku-muṟukku = tirukal, tirukiya tiṉpaṇṭam.
			muṟukku-muṟukkam.
			muṟu-muṟṟu-muṟṟuki = nakaric cūḻki.
			muṟu-muṟi-miṟi = vaḷivu.
			muṟu-muṟi. muṟital = vaḷital.
			muṟi-maṟi. maṟital = vaḷital, maṭaṅkutal.
	mul-(muḷ)-muṇṭu 	= 	uruṭci, tiraṭci.
		muṇṭu-muṇṭi uruṇṭi, muṭṭi.
		muṇṭi viḷipaḻam (patiṟ. 60:6)
		muḷ-muṭṭu-muṭṭi.
	muḷ-(muṇ)-muṇam-muṇaṅku. 
	muṇaṅkutal = 	uḷ vaḷital.
			muṇam-muṭam vaḷivu.
			muṭam-muṭaṅku. muṭaṅkutal = vaḷital.
			muṭaṅku-maṭaṅku. muṭaṅku-muṭakku = maṭakku.
			muṭam-(muṭal)-muṭali = kuṟaṭu, uruṇṭi.
			muṇṭu-maṇṭu. maṇṭutal = vaḷital.
			maṇṭu-maṇṭi, maṇṭiyiṭal = muḻaṅkāli maṭakkutal.
			maṇṭu-maṇṭalam vaṭṭam, nāṭṭup pakuti, kālap pakuti, nūṟpakuti.
koṅku maṇṭalam, toṇṭi maṇṭalam eṉṉum vaḻakkuk kaḷi nōkkuka.
maṇṭalam-maṇṭali, maṇṭalittal = ceyyuḷiṉ ellā aṭikaḷum aḷavottu varutal. īṟu toṭaṅkiyil (antātiyil) mutalum īṟum oṉṟittu varutal.
maṇṭalam-maṇṭilam -	1.	vaṭṭam.
			2.	vaṭṭak kaṇṇāṭi.
			3.	vaṭṭamāṉa cuṭar.
	  				(ñāyiṟu, tiṅkaḷ)
			4.	nilappakuti.
maṇṭilat tarumiyum  (tol. akat.41)
			5.   aḷavotta aṭikaḷik koṇṭa ceyyuḷ.
a-i. alam-ilam.
o.nō. aṉam-iṉam. paṭṭaṉam - paṭṭiṉam.
intiya āriyar piṟkālattil tamiḻaroṭu toṭarpu koṇṭa piṉṉarē, camaṟkirutam eṉum aric ceyaṟkiyāṉa ilakkiya naṭi vaḻakki allatu pottaka moḻiyi amittu, atiṟpaṇṭit tamiḻ nūlkaḷiyellām moḻipeyarttuk koṇṭaṉar.
tamiḻar teṉṉāṭṭup paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷ, tamiḻ tōṉṟiyatu muḻukippōṉa kumarik kaṇṭattil 50,000 āṇṭukaṭkumuṉ. tamiḻ nākarikam tōṉṟiyatu 20,000 āṇṭukaṭkumuṉ. tamiḻilak kaṇa vilakkiyam tōṉṟiyatu ki.mu.10,000 āṇṭukaṭkumuṉ.
intiya nākarika aṭippaṭi tamiḻ nākarikamē. āyiṉum, iṉṟu atu aṟiyappaṭāmaliruppataṟkuk karaṇiyam (kāraṇam): āriya varukikku muṟpaṭṭa tamiḻilakkiyam aṉittum aḻintum aḻikkappaṭṭum pōṉamiyum, āriyak kulap piriviṉiyāl tamiḻar pala vakiyilum tāḻttappaṭṭiruppatum; ivaṟṟiṟku nērmāṟāka, intiyak kalikaḷum aṟiviyalkaḷum moḻi peyarttu eḻutappaṭṭuḷḷa camaṟkiruta nūlkaḷē iṉṟirup patum, avaṟṟiyellām mutaṉūlkaḷē yeṉṟu vaṭavar valippatum, kulavariciyilum kalvit tuṟiyilum āriyar uyarttappaṭṭiruppatumē.
oru tiruṭaṉ tāṉ tiruṭiya poruḷit taṉateṉṟē valippi ṉum, apporuḷiṉ varalāṟu atupiṟaṉatu eṉpati meyp pippatupōl, tamiḻ varalāṟu, tamiḻnāṭṭu varalāṟu ākiya mūṉṟum, intiya nākarikam tamiḻaratē eṉpati aiyantiripaṟa nāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa. āyiṉum, ivvaralāṟṟi mēliyar aṟiyār. tamiḻarum ituvari avarukku aṟivittilar. iṟṟit tamiḻil vaṭacoṟkaḷum tamiḻulakattil vaṭanūl muṟikaḷum āriyak karuttukkaḷum mikutiyākap pukuttappaṭṭiruppatum, kōyil vaḻipāṭu, camaṟkirutattil pirāmaṇap pūcāriyarāl naṭipeṟṟu varuvatum, tamiḻariṉ iyaṟpeyar perumpālum vaṭacollā yiruppatum, tamiḻik kāṭṭikkoṭukkum tannalattār talimip patavi tāṅkikkoṇṭu tamiḻappotu makkaṭkut teriyātapaṭi tamiḻ nākarikatti maṟippatum, vaṭamoḻi yāḷar kūṟṟi mēliyarkku meypōṟ kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉar.
poyyuṭi yoruvaṉ colvaṉ miyiṉāl
meypō lummē meypō lummē.
meyyuṭi yoruvaṉ colamāṭ ṭāmiyāl
poypō lummē poypō lummē.
eṉṉum veṟṟivēṟki veṇ centuṟikaṭkēṟpa, āriyar tamiḻar nilikaḷ niṟkiṉṟaṉa. āyiṉum potuviyalpiṟku vilakkāka kilpeṭṭu cilēṟṟar (Gilbert Slater) eṉṉum ceṉṉip palkalikkaḻaka mēṉāṭ poruḷiyal nūṟ pērāciriyar, tam intiya nākarikattil tiraviṭak kūṟu (Dravidian Element in Indian Culture) eṉṉum nūlil,
tiraviṭar iṅṅaṉam moḻiyil āriyap paṭuttappaṭṭapōtu, āriyar nākarikap paṇpāṭṭil tiraviṭappaṭutta peṟṟaṉar (“While the Dravidians were thus Aryanised in language the Aryans were Dravidised in Culture”) eṉṟu naṭunilit tīrppuk kūṟi yiruppatu, tamiḻarum tiraviṭarum makiḻntu pārāṭṭaṟkuriyatu.
intiya nākarikam maṭṭumaṉṟi ulaka nākarikamē intiya āriyara teṉṟu kāṭṭutaṟku, vētakālatti aḷaviṟantu muṉ taḷḷi vikkum muyaṟciyoṉṟu iṉṟu vaṭintiyāvil naṭi peṟṟu varukiṉṟatu. aṟiyappaṭṭa tampiyiṉ akavi (vayatu) uyarttik kūṟappaṭiṉ, aṇṇaṉiṉ akavi tāṉē uyartal kāṇka.
intiyā cariyāṉapaṭi teṟkiluḷḷatu
vaṭa intiyāvil camakirutattiyum ataṉ carittirattiyum paṭittu, intu nākarikattiṉ aṭippaṭik kūṟṟik kāṇa muyalvatāṉatu. ak kāriyatti mikak kēṭāṉatum mikac cikka lāṉatumāṉa iṭattil toṭaṅkuvatākum. vintiya malikkut teṟkiluḷḷa intiyat tīpakaṟpamē iṉṟum cariyāṉa intiyā vāka iruntuvarukiṉṟatu. iṅkuḷḷa makkaḷiṟ perumpālār āriyar varumuṉpu tāṅkaḷ koṇṭirunta kūṟupāṭukaḷiyum, moḻikaḷiyum, camutāya ēṟpāṭukaḷiyumē iṉṟum teḷi vākak koṇṭiruntu varukiṟārkaḷ.
iṅkēkūṭa, carittirāciriyaṉukkut taṉṉāṭṭup pāviṉiṉṟum ayalnāṭṭu ūṭiyi eḷitāyp piritteṭukka iyalāta vāṟu, āriyam mika naṉṟāy vērūṉṟiyuḷatu. āṉāl, eṅkē ṉum ōriṭattilum ati veṟṟipeṟap pirit teṭukka muṭiyumāṉāl atu teṟkiltāṉ. evvaḷavu teṟkē pōkiṟōmō avvaḷavu piritteṭukka vacati atikarikkum.
aṅṅaṉamāyiṉ, kalimuṟippaṭṭa intiya carittirāciriyaṉ taṉatu paṭippi, ituvari mika nīṭac ciṟantateṉṟu piṉpaṟṟiṉa muṟippaṭi kaṅkic camaveḷiyiṉiṉṟum toṭaṅkāmal kiruṣṇa kāvēri vikiyāṟṟuc camaveḷikaḷiṉiṉṟum toṭaṅka vēṇṭum.
iṅṅaṉam irupattintāṇṭu kaṭku muṉpē iruperuñ carittirap pulavar eḻutiyiruppavum. intiya carittirāciriyarkaḷ itic caṟṟum kavaṉiyātu, iṉṟum vaṭanāṭṭiyum āriyat tiyumē talimiyākavum aṭippaṭiyākavuṅ koṇṭu, intiya carittiratti nērmāṟāka eḻuti varuvatu peritum iraṅkattakka toṉṟām.
intiyāvil, vaṭapākattil āriya tirāviṭattip pirikka iyalāviṭiṉum teṉpākattil iyalvatākum. iṅkum iyalāteṉpatu ārāycciyillātār kūṟṟē. intiyāvil āriyar eṉpatu kīḻnāṭṭāriyareṉṟu moḻinūlār kūṟum camakiruta āriyari.
tamiḻnāṭṭu makkaḷuḷ āriyar yāvareṉiṉ pārppaṉareṉṉum oru kulattārē. piṟaruḷ, piṟkālattu vanta mēṉāṭṭārum. avaratu kalappuṟṟa intiyarāṉa caṭṭikkārarum, urutuvit tāymoḻiyākakkoṇṭa mukamatiyarum tavira, maṟṟavarel lām taṉittamiḻarē.
tamiḻnāṭṭiṉ vaṭapākattil lappiyeṉṟum, kīḻp pākattil marikkāyar eṉṟum, teṉpākattil rāvuttar eṉṟum, maliyāḷattil māppiḷḷi eṉṟum, nālvakiyākac collappaṭum mukamatiyarellām. tōṟṟattāl vēṟupaṭṭuk kāṇilum, piṟappāl tamiḻnāṭṭuk kiṟitavarippōlat tamiḻar allatu tirāviṭarēyāvar.
teluṅkit tāymoḻiyākak koṇṭuḷḷa nāyuṭu, reṭṭi, kōmuṭṭi, cakkiliyar mutaliya vakuppār. ikkāla moḻi muṟippaṭi tamiḻarākak karutappaṭāviṭiṉum. tamiḻarukku neruṅkiṉa iṉattārāvar. ivar vijayanakara āṭcik kālattil nāyakka maṉṉaruṭaṉ āntira nāṭṭiṉiṉṟum tamiḻnāṭṭiṟku vantavarāvar. maliyāḷam, kaṉṉaṭam, tuḷu mutaliya piṟa tirāviṭa moḻikaḷip pēcum appārppaṉarum tamiḻarukkut teluṅ karip pōl iṉattārāvar.
āriyarum tirāviṭamum vaṭanāṭṭiṟ pirikka muṭiyātavāṟu kalantu pōṉālum. aṅkum pirāmaṇar maṭṭum toṉṟu toṭṭut taṅkaḷ kulattik kūṭiyavari tūymiyāyk kāttuvantatākavē terikiṉṟatu.


intiyoḻippu
(1) intiyip pukuttiya cūḻnili
ellā vakiyilum nalampayappatum intiya ōrāṭcikkum taṉṉāṭcikkum vaḻikōliyatum īṭiṇiyaṟṟatumāṉa, āṅkilam intiyap potumoḻiyā yirukkumpōtu, potu moḻi viṉāviṟkē iṭamiṉmi.
tiṭumeṉṟu viṭutali (āṅkilarāṭcinīkkam) kiṭittatāl, tēcameṅkum maruṭciyum makiḻcciyum malintu, etirkkaṭci kaḷellām aṭaṅkiyoṭuṅki, pērāyattalivar etu ceyyiṉum etirppiṟ kiṭamillā tiruntami.
tamiḻarum tiraviṭarumāṉa teṉṉāṭṭup pērāyattārellām āriya aṭimittaṉattil āḻntuḷḷami.

(2) intiyip pukuttiyamuṟikēṭu
viṭutali peṟumuṉ intiyip paṟṟi muṭipu ceyyāmi.
araciyaṟ kaṭcittalivar kalvittuṟiyilum moḻittuṟi yilum aṭātu taliyiṭṭami.
kalviyatikārikaḷ moḻiyaṟiñar ākiyōriṉ karuttik kēḷāmi.
potumoḻit tiṭṭakkuḻuvil moḻikkoru paṭinika rāḷiyic (Representative) cērkkāmal, intikkup palavuṟup piṉaric cērttuk koṇṭami.
tamiḻukku maṟimaliyaṭikaḷiyēṉum cōmacuntara pāratiyi yēṉum cērkkāmi.
toṭarntu naṭipeṟṟa mukkūṭṭattilum āṅkilattiṟkum intikkum caricamak kuṭavōli kiṭittum, kuḻuttalivar naṭunili tiṟampit tam kuṭavōliyi intikkiṭṭami.

(3) intiyiṉ takutiyiṉmi
ikkāla aṟiviyalilakkiyattōṭu, vaṭamoḻiyilum tamiḻilu muḷḷa paṇṭi yilakkiyamumiṉmi.
colvaḷamillāc ciṟumoḻiyum, mikattirinta kocci moḻiyum, vaḷarccik kēṟṟa vērccol illāmoḻiyum vaṭintiya orunāṭṭu moḻiyum, teṉṉāṭṭārkku āṅkilam pōṉṟē ayaṉmoḻiyu māyiruttal.
(4) āṅkilam ayaṉmoḻiyaṉmi
intiyaruḷ oruvakuppārāṉa āṅkilō intiyariṉ tāy moḻiyā yiruttal.
nūṟṟiṟkeḻupatu kirēkka ilattīṉamum, pattu āṅkila cākacaṉiya mum, pattut tamiḻum, pattu ēṉi moḻikaḷumāka ēṟattāḻa ellā moḻic coṟkaḷiyum taṉṉakattuk koṇṭu ulakap potuk kalavi moḻiyā yiruttal.

tamiḻukku mika nerukkamāyiruttal.
(5) āṅkilattiṉ iṉṟiyamiyāmi
ikkāla aṟiviyal tōṉṟiyatum vaḷarntuvaruvatumāṉa moḻi yāyiruttal.
mūvilakkattiṟkumēl aiyilakkam vari matippuḷḷa coṟkaḷik koṇṭu, iṟṟimāntaṉ nuṇkaruttukkaḷiyellām terivikkum māperu vaḷamoḻiyāyiruttal.
iṉṟu ulakappotu moḻikaḷuḷ oṉṟāyum, etirkālattil ulakap potumoḻiyāy vaḻaṅkak kūṭiyatumāyiruttal.
intiyap palkalik kaḻakaṅkaḷiṉ toṭarpu moḻiyāyiruttal.
intiyavoṟṟumikkum intiyariṉ veḷic celaviṟkum uḷ nāṭṭilum veḷināṭṭilum piḻippiṟkum iṉṟiyamiyāta moḻiyāyiruttal.

(6) intiyāl varuṅkēṭu
iraṇṭāntarak kuṭivāṇar nili, intiyārkku ēṉiyar eṉṟum aṭimippaṭṭiruttal, māṇavarkku mummoḻik kalviccumi, tamiḻāṅkilam maṭṭuṅ kaṟṟavarkku vēliyiṉmi mutaliyaṉa.

(7) intiyār ēmāṟṟu
intiyit tiṇikka māṭṭōm eṉpatum āṅkilak kalvi tēviyilli eṉpatum, mummoḻit tiṭṭattiyē yāmuṅ kaṭippiṭippōm eṉpatum tuṇiccalāṉa mutaltara ēmāṟṟē.
intiyāriṉ mummoḻittiṭṭam piṟamoḻiyārkkē; tām intiyum āṅkilamum ākiya iru moḻittiṭṭamē mēṟ koḷvar. āṅkila aṟiviṉṟi ikkālam vāḻa iyalātātalāl, intiyi intiyā muḻuvatum pukuttumvari, āṅkilatti maṟimukamākavum ataṉpiṉ veḷippaṭiyākavuṅ kaṟpar.

(8) nēru uṟutimoḻiyum nīliyuṟutimoḻiyum oṉṟē
nēru uṟutimoḻi, intit tiṇippup pintinikaḻum eṉpatē yaṉṟi nīkkappaṭum eṉpataṉṟu. itu mummoḻittiṭṭatti mēṟ koṇṭa tiraviṭanāṭukaṭkē ēṟkum; irumoḻit tiṭṭattik koṇṭa tamiḻnāṭṭiṟku ēṟkavē ēṟkātu. ātalāl atu taṉittē uṭaṉaṭiyāka muḻuvalimiyuṭaṉ intit toṭarpi etirttoḻittal vēṇṭum.
tūkkut taṇṭaṉiyi nīkka vēṇṭum eṉṟu coṉṉavaṉukku, orukiḻami poṟuttut tūkkuvōm eṉpatu naṉmi payakkum viṭiyaṉṟu.
(9) tamiḻnāṭṭaraciṉ kaṭami
araciṉar aluvalakamāyiṉum taṉiyār aluvalakamāyiṉum, peyarp palakikaḷil tamiḻum āṅkilamumaṉṟi inticcol iruttal kūṭātu.
tamiḻnāṭṭil vaḻaṅkum añcaṟ paṭivaṅkaḷum aṭṭi uṟi muttirikaḷum tamiḻāṅkilac coṟkaḷiyē koṇṭiruttal vēṇṭum.
ceṉṉi mānakariluḷḷa teṉṉintiya intip parappaṟkaḻakam tamiḻukkut tīṅkuviḷittuvaralāl, uṭaṉē tamiḻnāṭṭiṉiṉṟu nīkkappaṭal vēṇṭum.
(10) iru moḻit tiṭṭam
kalvittuṟiyil aṭimutal muṭivari irumoḻit tiṭṭat tiyē kaṭippiṭittal vēṇṭum. kalvinēramallāta vēḷi yilum, kalvi niliyaṅkaḷil intikaṟpikkappaṭal kūṭātu.
irumoḻit tiṭṭatti etirppavarit tamiḻp pakivarākak koṇṭu, nāṭṭiraṇṭakak kuṟṟattiṟkuriya taṇṭaṉiyiṭal vēṇṭum.
tamiḻnāṭu pōṉṟē tiraviṭanāṭukaḷum irumoḻit tiṭṭatti mēṟkoḷḷat tūṇṭa vēṇṭum.

intiraṉ
intiraṉ eṉpatu vaṭintiyat tirāviṭar vēntaṉ eṉṉum maruta nilat tamiḻt teyvattiṟku vaḻaṅkiya peyar. itteyva mum iccollum mēliyāriṭattil illi eṉṟu mākkacu mullar kūṟiyiruppatu kavaṉikkattakkatu.  (ti.ma.piṉ.)

intu matattiṉiṉṟu tamiḻ matattip pirittal
intumatam eṉpatu oru taṉi matamaṉṟu. tamiḻar matam iraṇṭum āriyar paṭittukkoṇṭa pirama vaṇakkamuṅ kalanta kalaviyē intumatam.
civamatam (civaṉiyam) tirumālmatam (māliyar) iraṇṭum tūya tamiḻar mataṅkaḷ. ivi, muṟiyē, kuṟiñcinilac cēyōṉ vaṇakkattiyum mullinila māyōṉ vaṇakkattiyum mūlamākak koṇṭavi. camaṇamum puttamum allatu kiṟittava mum icalāmum pōla, ivviraṇṭum muṟṟum vēṟupaṭ ṭavi. tiruvāṉikkāc civaṉaṭiyār tiruvaraṅkañceṉṟu tirumāli yum, tiruvaraṅkat tirumālaṭiyār tiruvāṉikkā ceṉṟu civaṉiyum, toḻār. civaṉum tirumālum taṉittaṉi muttoḻi liṟivaṉ eṉpatē, ivviru matak koḷkiyum.
āriyap pūcāriyar tamiḻa mataṅkaḷi āriyappaṭuttum poruṭṭu, iṟivaṉ muttoḻilit taṉittaṉi pirittu, tām paṭittukkoṇṭa nāṉmukaṉip paṭipput tirumēṉi yeṉṟum, tirumālik kāpputtirumēṉi yeṉṟum, civaṉi aḻipput tirumēṉiyeṉṟum vakuttu, muttirumēṉik koḷkiyit tōṟṟu vittuviṭṭaṉar. āyiṉum, itu iṉṟum purāṇa ēṭṭaḷavil uḷḷatēyaṉṟi naṭimuṟiyiṟ kikkoḷḷappaṭavilli. tamiḻar nāṉmukaṉi eṅkēṉum vaṇaṅkuvatu milli.
āriyar intu matattil tam ciṟuteyva vaṇakkattiyum pala teyva vaṇakkattiyum vēḷvi vaḷarppiyum pukuttiyiruppa taṉālum, pirāmaṇap pūcāriyarik koṇṭu camaṟkirutatti lēyē vaḻipāṭṭi naṭattuvataṉālum, itaṉāl tamiḻukku iḻivum, tamiḻarkku iḻivōṭu piḻip piṉmiyum nērvata ṉālum, tamiḻar tam matattit tūymippaṭutti āriya aṭimit taṉittiṉiṉṟu vilakikkoḷvatē takkatām.
ātalāl, iṟivaṉic civaṉ eṉṉum peyarāl vaḻipaṭupavaṉ taṉṉic civaṉiyaṉ (civa matattāṉ) eṉṟum, tirumāl eṉṉum peyarāl vaḻipaṭupavaṉ māliyaṉ (tirumāl matattāṉ) eṉṟum, collal vēṇṭum. potuppaṭac colliṉ, teṉmatattāṉ allatu tamiḻa matattāṉ eṉṟē colla vēṇṭum. intu eṉpatu āriya aṭimit taṉattiyē kuṟikkum.

iyaṟki moḻi
uṇarcci yolikaḷ (Emotional sounds), viḷiyolikaḷ (Vocative sounds), kuṟippolikaḷ (Symbolic sounds), vāyviṉi yoli kaḷ, kuḻavivaḷarp polikaḷ (Nursury Sounds), cuṭṭolikaḷ (Deicite Sounds), eṉṉum eḻuvaki yolikaḷik koṇṭatu iyaṟkimoḻi (Natural Language) allatu muḻittal moḻi (Inarticulate Speech)  (ta.i.va.)
iraṭṭiyār
kampaḷar kāmpilic cīmiyiṉiṉṟum, iraṭṭiyār iraṭṭa pāṭiyiṉiṉṟum, oṭṭar oṭṭara allatu oricāc cīmiyi ṉiṉṟum vantavarāvar.  (col.27)
irattal vaki ītal vakikaḷ
ī eṉṟu irattal iḻintōṉ ceyal; tā eṉṟu kēṭṭal ottōṉ ceyal; koṭu eṉṟu kaṭṭaḷiyiṭutal uyarntōṉ ceyal;
īyeṉ kiḷavi iḻintōṉ kūṟṟē
tā eṉ kiḷavi oppōṉ kūṟṟē
koṭu eṉ kiḷavi uyarntōṉ kūṟṟē
eṉpaṉa tolkāppiyam. (eccaviyal, 49, 50, 51).
ātalāl, ītal eṉpatu iḻintōrk kaḷittal; tarutal eṉpatu ottōrk kaḷittal; koṭuttal eṉpatu uyarntōrkkaḷittal. aḷittal eṉpatu aṉpiṉāṟ koṭuttaliyum; iṭutal eṉpatu kīḻiṭṭuk koṭuttalākiya irappōrk kītaliyum; vaḻaṅkal eṉpatu eṭuttuk koṭuttaliyum kuṟikkum. aruḷutal eṉpatu aruḷik koṭuttal.  (col-56.)
irāku
irāku-rāhu
irāku karumpāmpu (piṅ.) eṉṟum karuṅkōḷ eṉṟum collappaṭum.
ir-iru-irumi=karumi. ir-irā-iravu. ir-iruḷ-iruṭṭu.
irā-irāku.
vaṭamoḻiyiṟ paṟṟuvatu (to seize, take hold of) eṉṟu poruḷ paṭum rap (rabh) eṉṉum colli mūlamākak kāṭṭuvatu, poruntap poyttal eṉṉum uttiyām.  (va.va : 89)
irukariyaṉ
āṟṟiṉ naṭuvil iruntukoṇṭu ataṉ irukarikaḷil etaṉi aṭivateṉṟu tuṇiyātu iṭarppaṭupavaṉippōla, oru kāriyattic ceyvatā tavirvatā eṉa irumaṉamāki iruppavaṉukku irukariyaṉ eṉṟu peyar.  (col-12.)
irutalimaṇiyaṉ
pāmpu vakikaḷuḷ maṅkuṇi (maḻuṅkuṇi) eṉpatoṉṟu. ataṟkut taliyum vālum vēṟupāṭillāmal irukaṭiyum ottiruppatālum, talippakkamum vāṟpakkamum atu cella muṭiyum ātalālum ataṟku irutali maṇiyaṉ eṉṟu peyar. pakinaṭpu ākiya īriṭattum ceṉṟu irucārārkkum nallava ṉāka naṭippavaṉ irutalimaṇiyaṉi ottiruttalāl avaṉi yum appeyarāl aḻippar.  (col.12)
irāmāyaṇakkālam
(tōrā. ki.mu.1300-1200)
cōḻaṉ vēntaṉāṉapiṉ vaṭanāṭṭil taṉ paṭinikarāḷiyāka amarttiya cōḻakkuṭiyiṉaṉ vaḻimarapē, camaṟkiruta ilakkiyat tiṟ cūriya vamicam eṉṟu collappaṭuvatu. avvaḻiyil vantavaṉē irāmaṉ.
kamparāmayaṇam, pālakāṇṭam, kulamuṟi kiḷattu paṭalattil, irāmaṉiṉ muṉṉōrākac collappaṭṭavar patiṉālvar. mt® bga®fŸ; kD, ãUJ (ntd‹ kf‹), ï£RthF, fF¤j‹ (fF¤[‹), gh‰flš filªJ mKjË¤jt‹ (ÃÄ?), māntātā, mucukuntaṉ, cipi, cākarar, pakīrataṉ, nūṟu kutiri vēḷvi (acuvamētam) ceytavaṉ, irāku, acaṉ, takarataṉ eṉpaṉa.
oru kālattil kaluḻaṉ viṇṇulaki yaṭintu, aṅkirunta amiḻtattik kavarntu ceṉṟāṉ. intiraṉ tāṉ tirumpi varumaḷavum viṇṇakarik kākka mucukuntac cōḻaṉi amartti, avaṉukkut tuṇiyāka oru pūtattiyum niṟutti viṭṭu, amiḻtatti mīṭkac ceṉṟu viṭṭāṉ. akkāli avuṇar tiraṇṭu vantu mucukuntaṉoṭu porutu tōṟṟōṭiṉar. piṉṉar oru vañcaṉiyāl avaṉi vellak karuti, avar mīṇṭum vantu, avaṉ akakkaṇṇum kāriruḷil mūḻkumāṟu oru pēriruḷ ampi viṭuttaṉar. avaṉ ceyvataṟiyātu mayaṅki niṟkumpōtu, avaṉ tuṇippūtam oru mantiratti utava ataṉāl teḷintu, avuṇarik koṉṟu kuvittāṉ. mīṇṭu vanta intiraṉ ceytiyaṟintu, mucukuntaṉukku niliyākavutavu māṟu appūtattiyum avaṉuṭaṉ aṉuppiṉāṉ. atu pukāril nāḷaṅkaṭiyil niṉṟu nāḷ toṟum paṭiya luṇṭu vantatu.
ikkatiyē cilappatikārattil,
kaṭuvici yavuṇar kaṇaṅkoṇ ṭīṇṭik
koṭuvari yūkkattuk kōnakar kātta
toṭukaḻal maṉṉaṟkut tolintaṉa rāki
neñciruḷ kūra nikarttumēl viṭṭa
vañcam peyartta māperum pūtam
tiruntuvēl aṇṇaṟkut tēvaṉ ēva
iruntupali yuṇṇum iṭaṉum  (6:7-13)
eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
cīṉa nāṭṭiṟku vāṉavar nāṭu eṉṟoru peyaruṇṭu. annāṭu pūtakkatikaṭkup peyarpōṉa teṉpatu alāṭiṉ katiyāl aṟiyappaṭum.
mucukuntaṉ cīṉanāṭṭaracaṉ oruvaṉoṭu naṭpuk koṇṭiruntu, avaṉ nakarukkuc ceṉṟirukkalām. aṉṟu kaluḻavēkaṉ eṉpāṉoruvaṉ amirtapati eṉpāḷoruttiyik kavarntu ceṉṟirukkalām. cīṉavaracaṉ mucukuntaṉit taṉ nakarik kākkumāṟu irutti viṭṭu amirtapatiyi mīṭkac ceṉṟapiṉ, ūṇar (Huns-A]z®) eṉṉum naṭu āciyavāṇar cīṉattali nakarit tākki mucukuntaṉāl muṟiyaṭikkap paṭṭirukkalām. cīṉa aracaṉ taṉakkut tuṇiyeṉṟu koṇṭirunta pūtappaṭi miyip pukārukku aṉuppiyirukkalām.
pulavar kuḻantiyār mucukuntaṉi mutukāntaṉ eṉpar.
mucuppōṟ kuntiyiruppavaṉ eṉṉum poruḷ koṇṭa tāyiṉ, mucukuntaṉ eṉpatu tūya teṉcollē. muṟpiṟappil mucukkaliyā yiruntāṉ eṉṉuṅ kati ikkālattiṟ koḷḷat takka taṉṟu.
cipi eṉpavaṉ, puṟāvit turattiya paruntiṟkut taṉ taciyi aṟuttuk koṭutta cempi allatu cempiyaṉ eṉṉum cōḻaṉē. avaṉ peyar vaṭamoḻiyiṟ cipi eṉat tirintuḷḷatu. itiyaṟiyāta (allatu aṟintē ēmāṟṟukiṉṟa) vaṭamoḻip pulavar, cōḻar cipi eṉṉum vaṭanāṭṭu āriya varacaṉiṉ vaḻiyiṉarākak kūṟi makiḻvar.
irāmaṉ kālattavaṉāṉa paracurāmaṉ, kārtta vīriyārccuṉaṉ makkaḷ eyta irupattōrampu paṭṭu irupattoru muṟi taṉ mārpil aṭittuk koṇṭiṟanta, taṉ tāyik koṉṟataṟkup paḻi vāṅkumāṟu, irupattoru talimuṟi aracarik karuvaṟukka vañciṉaṅ kūṟiṉāṉeṉṟum, ataṉ toṭarpākak kāntaṉ eṉṉum cōḻaṉik kollap pukār varumpōtu, accōḻaṉ taṉ aracap poṟiyit taṉ kātaṟ kaṇiki makaṉākiya kakantaṉiṭam oppuvittu, nāṉ akattiya muṉivar kaṭṭaḷippaṭi tirumpumaḷavum nī iti ēṟṟut tāṅku eṉṟu colli nāṭṭi viṭṭup pōṉatāka oru kati, maṇimēkali yiṟ kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
maṉmaruṅ kaṟutta maḻuvāḷ neṭiyōṉ
taṉmuṉ tōṉṟal takātoḷi nīyeṉak
kaṉṉi yēvaliṟ kānta maṉṉavaṉ
innakar kāppōr yāreṉa niṉii
nāvalan taṇpoḻil naṇṇār naṭukkuṟak
kāvaṟ kaṇiki taṉakkāṅ kātalaṉ
ikaḻntōr kāyiṉum eñcuta lillōṉ
kakanta ṉāmeṉak kātaliṟ kūuy
aracā ḷurimi niṉpā liṉmiyiṉ
paracu rāmaṉniṉ pālvan taṇukāṉ
amara muṉivaṉ akattiyaṉ taṉātu
tuyarnīṅku kiḷaviyiṉ yāṉtōṉ ṟaḷavum
kakantaṉ kāttal kākanti yeṉṟē
yiyinta nāmam ippatik kiṭṭīṅ
kuḷvarik koṇṭav vuravōṉ peyarnāḷ  (maṇi.22:25-39).
itaṉāl, irāmaṉ kālamum akattiyaṉ kālamum kāntaṉ kālamum oṉṟeṉpatum, pukāriṉ paḻamiyum, mūvēntar kuṭikaḷiṉ toṉmiyum aṟiyappaṭum.
akattiyar
vētak kālattil vaṭintiyāvil nikaḻnta iṉappōrkaḷel lām, āriyac cūḻcciyāl paḻaṅkuṭimakkaḷum vaṭatiraviṭaru māṉa orē iṉattiṟkuḷ nikaḻntaviyē. ulakamutal uyartaṉic cemmoḻi pēciyavarum, oppuyarvaṟṟa ilakkiya vilakkaṇa muṭiyavarum, paltuṟip paṇpāṭṭu nākarikarum, kūṟṟuvaṉi yum aṟikūvik kumikkum paṭiyiṉarum, kaṭal kaṭantu pala nāṭukaḷik kippaṟṟivarum, māpēriṉattārumāṉa paṇṭit tamiḻar; kalviyaṟi villātavarum, kiyōṅkiṉuṅ kātavaḻiyōṭu pavarum, kiyuṅkālumāka vantavarum, irantuṇṭē vāḻpa varum, viralviṭṭum eṇṇa vēṇṭāta ciṟu tokiyiṉaru māṉa, āriyap pūcārikaṭku aṭimippaṭṭup pōṉatiyum; ivvirupatām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ piṟpakutiyilum āriya vayappaṭṭa pērāyat tamiḻar, urimi vēṇṭum nayaṉmik kaṭcit tamiḻarip pakittu veṉṟatiyum iṉṟum etirppatiyum; nōkku miṭattu, vaṭanāṭṭup paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷ āriyap pūcārikaṭku aṭaṅkippōṉatu oruciṟitum viyappaṉṟu. tamiḻarik koṇṭē tamiḻari aṭakki yoṭukki varuvatu pōl, nāṭṭu makkaḷik koṇṭē nāṭṭumakkaḷi veṉṟirukkiṉṟaṉar vaṭakkilum.
āriyap pūcārikaḷ cintu veḷiyiṉiṉṟu kaṅki veḷikku vantapiṉ, vēta mantiraṅkaḷiṉ poruḷi viḷakkum pirāmaṇam eṉṉum urinaṭinūlkaḷ eḻuntaṉa. pal vēḷviccāli mutukuṭumip peruvaḻutiyum aracavēḷvi (rāja sūya) vēṭṭa perunaṟkiḷḷiyum pal yāṉic celkuḻu kuṭṭuvaṉum pōṉṟa aracarit tamiḻakattil tuṇikkoṇṭatu pōṉṟē, vitēka nāṭṭu mātavaṉ pōṉṟa aracari vaṭanāṭṭil tuṇik koṇṭu āriya vēḷvi matattip parappiyirukkiṉṟaṉar eṉpatu, catapata pirāmaṇattāl teriya varukiṉṟatu (1, 4 : 1 : 10).
vēḷvi ellām vallatum aḷavilā āṟṟaluṭiyatumāku meṉṟum, vēḷviyāciriyaṉē makkaḷuṭ periyava ṉeṉṟum, avaṉ vēḷvi vaḷarppa taṉālēyē maḻi peytu payir viḷintu ā curantu ulakam naṭipeṟu kiṟateṉṟum, karuttuk kaḷ parappappaṭṭaṉa. āriyap pūcāri periyavaṉ eṉṉuṅ karut til piramaṉ (brahman) eṉappaṭṭāṉ. pirāmaṉ = pirāmaṇaṉ
piramaṉ mutal nālvaruṇattu  (tiruvāṉik. kōcceṅ. 69).
	piramakatti	=	pirāmaṇak koli.
	piramakattiyi ṉīṅkip piṟaṅkuvāṉ  (cētupu.taṉuk. 57).

	pirama kāyattiri	=	pirāmaṇar nāṭōṟum ōtum mantiram.
	pirama kulam	=	pirāmaṇa vakuppu.
	pirama carppam	=	pirāmaṇap pāmpu.
	pirama cūttiram	=	pūṇūl (pirāmaṇaṉ aṇivatu).
	pirama tāyam	=	pirāmaṇarkku viṭappaṭṭa iṟiyili
			nilam.
	pirama tīrttam	=	nīril taruppiyit tōyttu
			uṭampil taṭavuki.
pirama tēyam	=	pirāmaṇarkkut tāṉamākak koṭukkap
		paṭṭa ūr.
	pirama mārāyaṉ,	=	pirāmaṇa amiccaṉ paṭṭappeyar.
			pirama rāyaṉ
pirama muṭi(muṭiccu)	=	pirāmaṇiṉ pūṇūliluḷḷa oru vaki
		muṭiccu.
	pirama muṉi (riṣi)	=	pirāmaṇa muṉivaṉ.
	pirama rākkataṉ	=	tīccā vaṭinta pirāmaṇap pēy.
	pirama yōṉi	=	pirāmaṇap piṟappu.
	pirama vintu	=	pirāmaṇaṉukkup piṟantavaṉ.
peru-peruku-va. pruh. peruṅkati, peruvuṭiyār eṉṉum teṉcoṟ kaḷiṉ moḻi peyarppē, pruhat katā, pruha tīvara eṉṉum vaṭacoṟkaḷ.
perumaṉ-va. prahmaṉ-piramaṉ. piramaṉuk kuriyatu pirāmaṇam. pirāmaṇattiṟkuriyavaṉ pirāmaṇaṉ.
pirāmaṇar teṉṉāṭṭut tamiḻa nākarikac ciṟappip paṟṟik kēḷvippaṭṭu, iṅkum tam mēmpāṭṭi niṟuvumāṟu, nimica aṭaviyil aṭikkaṭi mānāṭu kūṭic cūḻntatākat terikiṉṟatu.
oru nimi (noṭi) nērattil oru perumpaṭi kollappaṭṭa iṭam, nimicam eṉṟu peyar peṟṟatākac collappaṭu kiṉṟatu. imi-nimi-va. nimi-nimiṣa-nimiṣa-nimica.
nimicāṭavi mānāṭṭut tīrmāṉattiṉpaṭi, akattiyar teṉṉāṭu nōkkip puṟappaṭṭār. avar kāciyiṉiṉṟu vinta maliyaṭintu, aṅkiruntu taṇṭaka aṭavi vantu taṅki, ataṉpiṉ kāñciyaṭintu, piṉ kāviri tōṉṟum ciyam eṉṉum kuṭakumali ceṉṟu, kuṭamali vaḻiyākap potiya mali pōyc cērntatākak kāñcippurāṇaṅ kūṟukiṉṟatu.
vintamali kaṭakka muṭiyāta teṉṟu āriyar neṭunāḷākak karutik koṇṭiruntataṉāl, akattiyar atik kaṭantu vanta pōtu ataṉ cerukki yaṭakkiṉatākak kūṟiṉar.
yōkamuṟu pēruyirkaḷ tāmulivu ṟāmal
ēkuneṟi yāteṉami tittaṭiyi ṉēṟi
mēkaneṭu malitavaḻ vintameṉum viṇṭōy
nākamatu nākamuṟa nākameṉa niṉṟāṉ.
eṉpatu kamparāmāyaṇam (āraṇi.akat.39).
irāmaṉ taṉ maṉiviyoṭum tampiyoṭum kāṭṭiṟku vanta taṇṭaka aṭaviyil akattiyarik kaṇṭapōtu, ammuṉivar irāmaṉi aṅkēyē yiruntu arakkarik kollac coṉṉā reṉṟum; ataṟku irāmaṉ tāṉ ataṟkākavē vantiruppatāl, iṉṉum caṟṟut teṟkē taḷḷip pōy arakkar varum vaḻiyiṟ kāttirukka vēṇṭumeṉṟu coṉṉāṉeṉṟum; ataṟku akattiyar icintu, villum vāḷum ampum puṭṭiluṅ koṭuttup pañcavaṭiyiṟ pōyirukkac ceṉṉāreṉṟum; kamparāmāyaṇaṅ kūṟukiṉṟatu.
viḻumiyatu coṟṟaṉiyiv villitivaṇ mēṉāḷ
muḻumutalvaṉ vittuḷatu mūvulakum yāṉum
vaḻipaṭavi ruppatitu taṉṉivaṭi vāḷik
kuḻuvaḻuvil puṭṭiloṭu kōṭiyeṉa nalki.
ippuvaṉa muṟṟumoru taṭṭiṉiṭi yiṭṭāl
oppuvara viṟṟeṉavu rippariya vāḷum
veppuruvu peṟṟavaraṉ mēruvari villā
muppurame rittataṉi moykkaṇiyu nalkā.
eṉpaṉa akattiyar irāmaṉukkup paṭikkalam vaḻaṅkiyatu paṟṟiyaṉa (kampa.akat.55,56).
akattiyar kāciyiṟ puṟappaṭu muṉṉarē civaperumāṉiṭan tamiḻ kaṟṟār eṉṉuṅ katiyum, akattiyarukku aṭuttē oru  pirāmaṇakkūṭṭam vantu taṇṭaka aṭaviyil taṅkiyiruntatum, akattiyar irāmaṉukku villum vāḷum vaḻaṅkiyatum, nōkku miṭattu, ōr āriyak kuṭiyēṟṟak kūṭṭattiṉ tali varākavē akattiyar tiṭṭamiṭṭu vantatākat terikiṉṟatu.
avar tamiḻakam vantu muttamiḻuṅ kaṟṟu, oru muttamiḻilakkaṇam iyaṟṟi, ataṟku akattiyam eṉap peyariṭṭār. tamiḻa muṉivar pōṟpotiya maliyil taṅki, āriyarum tamiḻarumāṉa māṇavar palarukkut tamiḻ kaṟpittār. āriya māṇavaruḷ oruvaṉāṉa intiraṉ eṉpavaṉ, iyaṟṟami ḻilakkaṇattit taḻuvi, poruḷ āriyattiṟ kēṟkāmiyāl ati nīkki, eḻuttum collum paṟṟi mutal āriya ilakkaṇa nūli iyaṟṟi, ataṟku aintiram eṉap peyariṭṭāṉ. atip piṉpaṟṟiyē pirāticākkiyaṅkaḷ eṉṉum āriya vētac ciṟṟi lakkaṇaṅkaḷ tōṉṟiyiruttal vēṇṭum. intiraṉ tamiḻeḻuttiṉiṉṟu kiranta eḻutti vakuttē nūliyaṟṟi yiruttal vēṇṭum.
kāṉcuttāntiyacu pecukki (Constantius Beschi) eṉṉum vīramāmuṉivar, ittāliyā nāṭṭiṉiṉṟu 18-ām nūṟṟāṇ ṭil iṅkuvantu, vēmpāy (Bombay) maṇṭalattic cērnta kōvāvil tamiḻkaṟṟu, tamiḻnāṭṭiṟ potumakkaḷoṭu toṭarpu koṇṭu camayappaṇiyāṟṟi, 3615 maṇṭilam (viruttam) koṇṭa tēmpāvaṇi eṉṉum ciṟanta pāviyatti yum, 370 nūṟpā koṇṭa aintilakkaṇat toṉṉūl viḷakkam eṉṉum ariya ilakkaṇa nūliyum, iyaṟṟiyati nōkkiṉ, akattiyar muttamiḻilakkaṇam iyaṟṟiyatu attuṇi viyappiṟ kiṭamāṉataṉṟu.
akattiyam āriyarāl aḻiyuṇṭapiṉ, piṟa nūlkaḷiluḷḷa akattiya mēṟkōḷkaḷellām tokukkappaṭṭaṉa. mutaṟkaṇ tokutta ciṟu tokuppu ciṟṟakattiyam eṉṟum, piṉṉart tokutta perun tokuppu pērakattiyam eṉṟum, peyar peṟṟa tākat terikiṉṟatu. piṟkālattil aviyum iṟantoḻintaṉa.
civaperumāṉ tirumaṇattiṟku vanta patiṉeṇ kaṇattāriyum tāṅkātu paṉimali yamiḻntataṉāl, ñālattiṉ vaṭatici tāḻntu teṉṟici yuyara, tēvar vēṇṭukōṭkiṇaṅki akatti yar teṟkil vantu potiya malimēl niṉṟapiṉ, iru ticiyum camanilippaṭṭaṉa eṉṉuṅ kati, oru kālattiṟ paṉimali kaṭalaṭi yiruntatiyum, teṟkiṟ paṉimali pōl uyarntirunta kumarimali muḻukiṉatiyumē, kuṟikkum.
iṉi, aṇivakiyiṟ poruḷ koḷḷiṉ, ilakkiyat tuṟiyi lum poruḷiyal kumukāyaviyal araciyal mutaliya vāḻkkit tuṟikaḷilum, vaṭakkil tāḻntiruntāriyam akattiyar teṟkē vantapiṉ uyarnta teṉṟum, teṟkil avaṟṟil uyarn tirunta tamiḻam tāḻntateṉṟum, koḷḷalām. ivvakiyi lēyē,
maṉṉiya ciṟappiṉ vāṉōr vēṇṭat
teṉmali yirunta cīrcāl muṉivaraṉ
eṉṉum puṟapporuḷ veṇpāmālic ciṟappup pāyiram poruḷpaṭu vatākum.
akattiyari yaṭuttē nāratarum teṉṉāṭu vantu, icit tamiḻkaṟṟup pañcapāratīyam eṉṉum icittamiḻ nūli iyaṟṟiyiruttal vēṇṭum.
iṉi icittamiḻnūlākiya perunāri peruṅkurukum, piṟavum, tēvaviruṭi nārataṉ ceyta pañcapāratīya mutalā uḷḷa toṉṉūlkaḷ iṟantaṉa. nāṭakattamiḻ nūlākiya paratam akattiyam mutalākavuḷḷa toṉṉūlkaḷum iṟantaṉa. eṉṟu 14-ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉarāṉa aṭiyārkku nallār tam cilappatikāra urippāyirattuḷ varintiruttal kāṇka.
nārataṉ vīṇi nayanteri pāṭalum
....................................................
maṅkala miḻappa vīṇi maṇmicit
taṅkuka ivaḷeṉa  (cilap.6:18-23)
eṉpataṉālum, nārataṉiṉ tamiḻakat toṭarpum icippulami yum aṟiyappaṭum. vīṇi eṉpatu tamiḻar narappukkaruvi. narampu viṇṇeṉa icippatu vīṇi. nōyiṉāl narampu valikkum pōtu, narampu viṇviṇ eṉat teṟikkiṉṟatu eṉṟu kūṟum vaḻakki nōkkuka.
viṇ-vīṇ-vīṇi=va.vīṇā. mūṅkilik kuṟikkum vēṇu eṉṉum vaṭacolliṉiṉṟu, vīṇi allatu vīṇā eṉṉum collit tirippatu poruntātu. vētakkālattil āriyarukku narappuk karuviyumilli; icippulamiyumilli. orunarampil orē yici (curam) icippatu yāḻ eṉṟum, oru narampil melivu camaṉ valivu eṉṉum munnilikaḷuḷ oṉṟum palavum icippatu vīṇi eṉṟum, vēṟupāṭaṟika. ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻum cakōṭa yāḻum vīṇi vakikaḷē. ivaṟṟuḷ muṉṉatu meṭṭuḷḷatu; piṉṉatu kōṭṭiyam (kōṭṭu vāttiyam) pōl meṭṭillatu.
nāṭakattamiḻ nūlākiya paratam eṉṟu aṭiyārkku nallār kuṟittiruppatāl, tamiḻp paratanūlē vaṭamoḻi naṭa nūlākiya parata cāttirattiṟku mutaṉūleṉa aṟika. mutaṉūliṉ peyariyē vaḻinūṟkum iṭuvatu ōr āriya valakkāram. mutaṉūli aḻittu viṭṭu vaḻinūli mutaṉūleṉpatu avar vaḻakkam. ātivāyi lār veṇpāvil iyaṟṟiya parata cēṉāpatiyam eṉṉum nāṭakattamiḻ nūl iṟantupaṭṭatu. iṉṟu vaḻaṅkum parata cēṉāpatiyam vēṟu.
irāmāyaṇam eṉpatu, katiyi nōkkiṉum, pāviyatti (kāviyatti) nōkkiṉum, pāratampōl attuṇi varalāṟṟu muṟippaṭṭatum teḷivum tiṭṭamumāṉa ceytikaḷik koṇṭatum aṉṟu.
vaṭanāṭṭil nāṟpatuvaki irāmāyaṇak katikaḷ vaḻaṅku kiṉṟaṉaveṉṟum, avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟil irāmaṉ taṉ taṅkiyāṉa cītiyi maṇantāṉ eṉṟum, iṉṉoṉṟil irāmaṉ irāvaṇaṉiṉ maṉiviyāṉa cītiyik kavarntu koṇṭāṉ eṉṟum, iruppatākac colkiṉṟaṉar.
irāmaṉ kōtāvarikkut teṟkil varavilli yeṉṟum, cītiyik kavarnta arakkaṉ vāḻnta ilaṅki kōtāvari yāṟṟiṭittiṭṭēyeṉṟum, tamiḻ nāṭṭilum ilaṅkit tīvilum nikaḻntaṉavākac collappaṭum ceytikaḷellām piṟkālattuk kaṭṭukkatiyeṉṟum, ārāycciyāḷar cilar karutukiṉṟaṉar.
itaṟku ētukkaḷāvaṉa-
 (1)	irāmaṉ taṉ maṉiviyuṭaṉ tampiyuṭaṉum, kōtā varik kariyiluḷḷatum aintu ālamaraṅkaḷ cērntirun tatumāṉa pañcavaṭiyil taṅkiṉami.
 (2)	irāvaṇaṉ cītiyip pañcavaṭiyiṟ kavarntami.
 (3)	ilaṅki eṉṉum coṟku āṟṟiṭik kuṟi eṉṉum poruḷuṇmiyum, kōtāvari naṭuvil ōr āṟṟiṭikkuṟiyuṇmi yum.
 (4)	ki.mu. 3-ām nūṟṟāṇṭi mōriyarkkumuṉ, vaṭa nāṭṭār oruvarum tamiḻakattiṉmēṟ paṭiyeṭuk kāmi.
 (5)	irāmaṉ paṭiyik kurakkuppaṭiyeṉṟatum, kuraṅku kaṭku uyartiṇit taṉmi kūṟiyatum, aṉu māṉ eṉṉum kuraṅkip paṉmoḻip pulavaṉeṉṟum toṇṇilakkaṇap (nava viyākaraṇa) paṇṭitaṉeṉṟum pārāṭṭiyatum.
 (6) cītiyit tēṭumāṟu irāmaṉ kuraṅkukaḷi viṭutta pōtu, kōtāvarikku vaṭakkiluḷḷa vintamali, narumatiyāṟu, ēmakūṭam, taṇṭaka aṭavi, muṇṭat tuṟic cōli, pāṇṭumali ākiya iṭaṅkaḷip pārkkac coṉṉatuṭaṉ, kōtāvariyum aṭiyuṅkaḷ eṉṟami.
 (7) erumiyūrkku (micūrkku) vaṭakkiluḷḷa kiṭkinti mali, aṉṟu cēranāṭṭic cērntu māvali (makāpali) vēntaṉ āṭcikkuṭ paṭṭiruntami.
 (8)	irāmavilakkumaṇar veḷḷakkaṇakkāṉa kurakkup paṭiyuṭaṉ cōḻanāṭu vaḻiyākap pāṇṭināṭu vantu, kaṭalukkaṇi kaṭṭi ilaṅki pukuntu, irāvaṇa ṉoṭu patiṉeṇmātam porutu veṉṟu arakkar kulatti vēraṟuttaṉar eṉṉum ceyti, cōḻaṉukkum pāṇṭiyaṉukkum teriyātu pōṉami.
 (9)	irāmaṉ vintamalikkut teṟkil, muṉivar eṉṉum āriyap pūcārikaḷum arakkarum caṭāyu eṉṉum kaḻukum kuraṅkukaḷum tavira, tamiḻariyum vēṟu makkaḷiyuṅ kāṇāmi.
 (10)	pīṭumaṉ, irāmaṉ kālattavaṉāṉa paracurāmaṉiṭam viṟkali payiṉṟavaṉāyiṉum, irāmaṉiyō irāmāyaṇattiyō aṟiyā tiruntami.
 (11)	irāmāyaṇak kati iṭattiṟ kiṭamum, irāmāyaṇap pāviyam āciriyarkkāciriyarum, ciṟitum peritum vēṟupaṭṭiruttal.
 (12)	irāmāyaṇap pāviyam, mēṉmēlum uyarvunaviṟci yum iṭiccerukalum piṟcērkkiyum kati māṟṟamuṅkoṇṭu vantuḷḷami.
irāmāyaṇap paṉuval mutaṉmutal vāṉmīkiyārāl ciṟṟaḷa vākap pāṭappaṭṭirukkalām. pāratak kālattiṟkup piṉ patiṉ tōṟṟaravu (tacāvatāram) vakukkappaṭṭapōtu, atu viri vaṭiyat toṭaṅki yirukkalām.
paruti cūḻntavip payaṅkeḻu mānilam
orupakal eḻuvar eyti yaṟṟē
viyamun tavamun tūkkiṉ tavattuk
kiyavi yaṉittum āṟṟā tākaliṉ
kiviṭ ṭaṉarē kātalar ataṉāl
viṭṭōri viṭāaḷ tiruvē
viṭāa tōrivaḷ viṭappaṭ ṭōrē
eṉṉum puṟappāṭṭu (358) vāṉmīkiyār eṉṉum pulava roruvar pāṭiyatākavuḷḷatu. itu aracu niliyāmiyiyum tavattiṉ mēṉmiyiyum aḻakāka eṭuttuk kūṟuvatu. āciriyar peyariyum pāṭṭiṉ poruḷiyum nōkkumiṭattu, ivar irāmāyaṇa vāṉmīkiyār tāmō, aṉṟi avar vaḻiyiṉarō eṉṉum eṇṇam miṉpōl tōṉṟi maṟikiṉṟatu.
akattiyar vaḻikāṭṭiyapiṉ tamiḻakam vanta pirāmaṇar, teṉṉāṭṭārum vaṭanāṭṭār pōṉṟē matappittarā yiruntatu kaṇṭu, tām nilattēvareṉṟum tam moḻi tēva moḻiyeṉṟum ēmāṟṟi, kumarimutal paṉimalivari intiyap paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷ aṉivariyum, ulakil makkaḷuḷḷa kāla mellām tamakkum tam vaḻiyiṉarkkum aṭimippaṭuttutaṟ poruṭṭu, kumukāyat tuṟiyil oṉṟum camayattuṟiyil oṉṟumāka īraṉittintiyat tiṭṭaṅkaḷi vakuttuviṭṭaṉar.

vaḷaṅkuṉṟiya nāṭu, ceyappaṭu poruḷ kuṉṟiya viṉi eṉṉum toṭarp poruḷkaḷiyum oppunōkki yuṇarka.
il = illi. il, illi eṉpaṉa, uḷ uṇṭu eṉṉum viṉikaṭku etirmaṟiyām. uḷ eṉṉum viṉi maliyāḷap paḻamoḻikaḷil iṉṟum vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu.

āṇ makap pēṟillāta peṟṟōr, tam marukaṉit tam illatti lēyē iruttik koḷvar. iv ēṟpāṭṭi illattam eṉpar kaṉṉaṭar.
ilakkaṇam
ilakkaṇam - lakṣaṇa
ilakku-ilakkaṇam = ciṟanta naṭikku eṭuttukkāṭṭāka allatu kaṟṟōr piṉpaṟṟum ilakkāka, kūṟappeṟum moḻi yamiti (Grammar).
vaṭamoḻiyil kuṟi, kuṟikkōḷ, iyal, iyalviḷakkam (defini-tion), viḷakkak kāṭṭu (illustration) eṉṉum poruḷkaḷilēyē lakṣṇa eṉṉum colli āḷvar.

ilakkam-lakṣa
ilakku-ilakkam = kuṟi.
ilakka muṭampiṭumpikku  (kuṟaḷ.627)  (va.va.90)
ilakkam2 : ilakku = ili, eyyuṅkuṟi, kuṟi, kuṟikkōḷ, kuṟitta iṭam. oru kuṟitta iṭatti ilakku eṉpatu pāṇṭi nāṭṭu vaḻakku. ïy¡F-yº(t.),
ilakku-ilakkam-lakṣa (va.)  (ti.ma : 738)
ilakkaṇam, ilakkiyam
ilakkaṇam, ilakkiyam eṉṟa iru coṟkaḷum teṉ collā eṉṟu ārāyumuṉ. avaṟṟāl kuṟikkappeṟum iraṇ ṭum teṉmoḻiyiluṇṭā eṉṟu kāṇalvēṇṭum. ilak kaṇamum ilakkiyamum toṉṟu toṭṭut tamiḻukku iruntu varuvatu maṭṭumaṉṟi. mutaṉ mutalākavum avi nāvalan tēyattil eḻunta moḻi, teṉmoḻiyē eṉpatu mikiyaṉṟu. ilakkaṇamum ilakkiyamum toṉṟutoṭṭut tamiḻukku iruntiruppiṉ. avaṟṟik kuṟikkac collum tamiḻil irun tiruttal vēṇṭum. itaṟku ilakkaṇam, ilakkiyam eṉṟa irucol tavira vēṟu teṉcolluṇṭā veṉiṉ etuvu milli.
cilar, ilakkiyattiṟkuc ceyyuḷ eṉpatum, ilakkaṇattiṟku nūl eṉpatum tamiḻppeyar eṉpar. ceyyuḷ eṉpatu yāppu vaṭiviṟkup peyar eṉpatit tolkāppiyar yāppilakkaṇa viyalukkuc ceyyuḷiyal eṉṟu peyariṭṭatiyum. avviyaliṉ mutal nūṟpāvē māttiri mutaliya 26 uṟuppukaḷiyum. ammi mutaliya eṭṭoṭu kūṭṭi.
nallicip pulavar ceyyuḷ uṟuppeṉa
vallitiṟ kūṟi vakutturit taṉarē
eṉṟu kūṟuvatiyum, 127ām, 128ām nūṟpākkaḷ ceviyuṟi yum aṅkatamum paṟṟivarum ceyyuḷkaḷi muṟiyē ceviyuṟic ceyyuḷ eṉṟum, aṅkatac ceyyuḷ eṉṟum kūṟuvatiyum.

pāṭṭuri nūlē vāymoḻi piciyē

yāppiṉ vaḻiyatu eṉmaṉār pulavar (78)
eṉa yāppu eṉṟu muṉṉark kūṟiyatiyē.
eḻunilat teḻunta ceyyuḷ teriyiṉ
eṉac ceyyuḷ eṉṟu piṉṉar āciriyar peyar māṟṟik kūṟiyati yum. nūlukkuri ceyyuḷ urinaṭi ākiya iru vaṭivilum paṇṭik kālattiruntatiṉāl avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟāṉa ceyyuḷ vaṭivuriyē eḻunilattuḷ oṉṟākak kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷa teṉpa tiyum. ceyyuḷ urinaṭi ākiya iraṇṭiyum taḻuviyatē ilakkiyam eṉpatiyum nōkkik kāṇpārāka. kāviyam (Epic) tamiḻil toṭarnilic ceyyuḷ eṉappaṭṭatiyum kāṇka.
ceyyuḷik kuṟittaṟku yāppu, pā, pāṭṭu mutaliya piṟa peyarkaḷumuṇṭēyeṉiṉ. aviyellām parupporuṅ kaḷil oru poruṭ collāyiṉum nuṇporuḷil vēṟu paṭṭavi eṉpati aṟital vēṇṭum. av vēṟupāṭāvaṉa:
yāppu	:	metrical composition.
ceyyuḷ	:	poem, poetry.
pā			: 	variety  of metre.
pāṭṭu	:	ode
tūkku	:	metrical rhythm.
ceyyuḷ eṉpatu taṉippaṭṭa ceyyuḷiyum (Poem), ceyyuṭ ṭokutiyiyum (Poetry), ceyyuḷ naṭiyiyum kuṟikkum.
ceyyuḷ eṉpatu ceyyappaṭuvatu eṉṉum poruḷiyuṭiyatu. cey. pakuti : uḷ. vikuti.

eṉṟār pavaṇantiyār.
poem allatu poetry eṉṉum āṅkilaccollum itē poruḷik koṇṭiruppatu viyakkattakkatu.
E.poem, (from) F.poeme, (from) L.poema, (from).
Gk.poema - poiema - poieo = make.
Gk. poiesis - poesis = making, poetry, E.poesy.
Gk. poietes - poetes = maker, poet, E.poet.
iṉi, nūl eṉṉuñ colli ārāyvām. nūl eṉpatu, oru moḻiyiṉ allatu kaliyiṉ allatu vēṟētēṉumoru poruḷiṉ oḻuṅki allatu iyalpi eṭuttuk kūṟum puttakattiyum, ataṉ piṉpu ākupeyarp poruḷil atu paṟṟiya kaliyiyuṅ kuṟikkumē yaṉṟi. avvoḻuṅki allatu iyalpiyē kuṟikkātu. eṭuttukkāṭṭāka, oru moḻiyiṉ oḻuṅkippaṟṟi viṉaviṉāl, ataṉ ilakkaṇameṉṉa eṉṟu kēṭpatu muṟiyākumēyaṉṟi, ataṉ nūl eṉṉa eṉṟu kēṭpatu muṟiyākākatu. atu vaḻakku maṉṟu. ilakkaṇam eṉpatu grammar allatu definition eṉṟum, nūl eṉpatu treatise eṉṟum poruḷ paṭum. ivaṟṟuḷ, muṉṉatu ilakkaṇa nūliyum, piṉṉatu oru nūlāl allatu nūṟṟokutiyāl uṇarttappeṟum aṟivuk kaliyiyum (Science) kuṟikkumpōtu ākupeyarām. logos eṉṉum kirēkkaccol, mutalāvatu colliyē kuṟittup piṉṉar āṅkilattil uriyāṭṭiyum nūliyum kaliyi yum kuṟittami kāṇka. āyiṉ, ip piṟporuḷkaḷil atu logue eṉṟum logg eṉṟum tirivatuṭaṉ, tokic coṟkaḷiṉ īṟākavum (suffix) māṟiviṭum. ilakkaṇam eṉṉum col nūlik kuṟikkumpōtu moḻiyilakkaṇa muṭpaṭṭa ellāk kaliyilakkaṇaṅkaḷiyum kūṟum puttakaṅkaṭkellām potup peyar eṉṟum vēṟupāṭu uṇartal vēṇṭum.
iṉi, orucilar pulam, iyal eṉṟa coṟkaḷ tamiḻil ilak kaṇattik kuṟikkum eṉpar.
pulam eṉṉum colliṉ iyaṟporuḷ aṟivu eṉpatē. iccolliṉ aṭip piṟanta pulami pulavar eṉṟa coṟkaḷum, aṟivuṭimi aṟiñar eṉṟē poruḷpaṭum. ittakiya potuccol moḻiyilakkaṇattic ciṟappākac cuṭṭutaṟkup poruntātu.
pulantokut tōṉē pōkkaṟu paṉuval
eṉṟu paṉampāraṉār kūṟiyiruppiṉum, atu piṟarāl ēṟṟuk koḷḷappaṭṭatum peruvaḻakkāka vaḻaṅkiyatum illi. aṟivunūl kaḷellāvaṟṟiyum potuvākap pulam eṉpatu ceyyuḷ vaḻakkam. pulam puriyantaṇar eṉṉum paripāṭaṟ ṟoṭaril (6 : 45) maṟinūl pulam eṉappaṭṭatu. iḵtu ōr ākupeyar. vētam eṉṉum vaṭacollukkum aṟivu eṉpatē iyaṟporuḷākum. (É¤(=m¿.)-ntj«). paṉampāraṉār kūṟṟil uḷḷa pulam eṉṉuñ col, potuvāka aṟivu allatu aṟivunūl ellāvaṟṟiyum kuṟippatāyiṉum, iṭam nōkkic ciṟappāka ilakkaṇatti allatu ilakkaṇa nūlik kuṟippa tākak koḷḷappaṭum. āṉāl, itu āṭcipaṟṟiyatēyaṉṟi iyalpupaṟṟiyataṉṟu. itaṉāl pulam eṉpatu ilakkaṇap peyarākātu.
iṉi, iyal eṉṟa collōveṉiṉ, atu ellāp poru ḷukkum uriya taṉmiyi allatu iyalpip potuppaṭi yākac cuṭṭuvataṉṟi vēṟoṉṟum kuṟiyāmiyiṉ. ilakkaṇap peyarātaṟku eḷḷaḷavum ēṟṟataṉṟām. iyal = taṉmi. iyaṉmoḻi vāḻttu= oruvaṉuṭiya ciṟanta taṉmikaḷi eṭuttuk kūṟip pukaḻvatu. iyal = iyalpu. iyaṟki.
piṟa moḻikaḷil ilakkaṇam tōṉṟu muṉpum, āriyar varumuṉpum toṉṟutoṭṭum, tamiḻil ilakkaṇa ilakkiyam iruntu varuvatuṭaṉ. iṉṟum, tamiḻiltāṉ, piṟa moḻikaḷuk kellāmillāta poruḷilakkaṇamum iruntu varukiṉṟatu. itaṉālēyē,
kaṇṇu taṟperuṅ kaṭavuḷuṅ kaḻakamō ṭamarntu
paṇṇu ṟatterin tāynta ip pacuntamiḻ ēṉi
maṇṇi ṭiccila ilakkaṇa varampilā moḻipōl
eṇṇi ṭippaṭak kiṭantatā eṇṇavum paṭumō
eṉṟu parinturittār parañcōti muṉivar.
iṉi, ilakkaṇam ilakkiyam eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷiṉ vaṭamoḻi vaṭivāṉa lakṣaṇam, lakṣyam eṉvaṟṟiṟku vaṭamoḻiyil, grammar, literature  eṉṉum poruḷuṇṭāveṉiṉ, atuvu milli. vaṭamoḻiyil avaṟṟiṉ poruḷāvaṉa:
lakṣaṇa = kuṟi, aṭiyāḷam, iyalpu, taṉmi, kuṇam, aḻaku, (mutaliyaṉa).
lakṣyam = kuṟikkappaṭṭatu. variyaṟukkappaṭṭatu, varaṇikkap paṭṭatu. kuṟikkōḷ, nōkku, kuṟikkōḷuṭiyatu (mutaliyaṉa).
ivviru coṟkaḷiṉ aṭippaṭik karuttum kuṟi eṉpatē yākum. āṉāl, ivaṟṟiṉ mūlam rañc (ranj) eṉpataṉ tirivāṉa rakta  (rakta) eṉṟum, lak (lag) eṉṟum, iruvēṟu vakiyākac collappaṭṭukiṉṟatu.
rañc = varaṇam pūcap peṟu, niṟaṅkoḷ, viḷaṅku, oḷir.
iccol raṅ (rang) eṉpataṉ tiripākum.
lak = ici, iṇi, kaṭṭu, oṭṭu.
iccol, lego eṉṉum kirēkkac collukkum,  ligne eṉṉum ilattīṉ collukkum iṉac collākum.
lakṣaṇa, lakṣya eṉṉum coṟkaḷuṭaṉ, lakṣam (nūṟāyiram). lakṣmaṉ allatu lakṣmaṇaṉ, lakṣmī mutaliya coṟkaḷum, vaṭamoḻiyil toṭarpuṟuttappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. lakṣmaṉ, lakṣmaṇaṉ = ciṟanta kuṟikaḷi allatu nallilakkaṇaṅkaḷi  yuṭiyavaṉ. lakṣmī = naṟkuṟiyi allatu atirṣṭatti yuṭivaḷ; atirṣṭacāli allatu teyvam.
laṣmī eṉṉum collukkuc cilar lak (lag) eṉpatiyum, cilar lañc (lanj) eṉpatiyum, mūlamākak kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉar. lañc = oḷir, viḷaṅku, tikaḻ.
laṣaṇam, lakṣyam eṉṉum irucoṟkaḷum, vaṭamoḻiyil ilakkaṇam ilakkiyam eṉṟu poruḷpaṭātatōṭu, ilak kaṇam, ilakkiyam eṉṉum tamiḻc coṟkaḷippōla, kuṟip poruḷiyē aṭippaṭiyākavum, viḷaṅkutaṟ poruḷ tarum ilaṅku eṉṉum colliyē mūlamākavum koṇṭuḷḷaṉa. itu mikak kavaṉikkattakkatu.
vaṭamoḻiyil ilakkaṇattik kuṟikka vyākaraṇam eṉṉum collum, ilakkiyattik kuṟikkac cākityam eṉṉum col lum uḷḷaṉa. tamiḻilō ilakkaṇam, ilakkiyam  eṉṉum coṟkaḷit tavira vēṟetuvumilli. aḵtōṭu, avviru coṟkaḷukkum vēr tamiḻiltāṉ uḷḷatu. aḵteṅṅaṉa meṉiṉ, kāṭṭuval:
eḻuccip poruḷiyuṇarttum ekara veḻutti aṭiyākak koṇṭa el eṉṉuñcol, eḻukiṉṟa allatu utikkiṉṟa katiravaṉiyum ataṉ oḷiyiyum kuṟikkum.
ellē yilakkam eṉpatu tolkāppiyam  (iṭi 21)
el = katiravaṉ, veyil, oḷi, pakal
el - eṉṟu katiravaṉ.
eṉṟu - eṉṟavaṉ = katiravaṉ
eṉṟu - eṉṟūḻ = katiravaṉ mutircci, kōṭi.
ekaram allatu ēkāram eḻuccip poru ḷuṇarttuvati, eḻu, empu, evvu, ekku, eṭu, ēṇ, ēṇi, ēṇi, ēttu, (ēṟṟic col), ēntu, ēl, ēṟu, erect, edify, cructate mutaliya coṟkaḷiṟ kāṇka. uyarccip poruḷum eḻuccip poruḷōṭokkum.
el - elku - elaku - ilaku = viḷaṅku.
coṟkaḷiṉ mōṉittiripil ikaram ekaramākavum ekaram ikaramākavum tirivatu iyalpē.
ilaku - ilaṅku = viḷaṅku.
ilaṅku = ēri, kuḷam.
teḷinta nīr paḷiṅkupōl viḷaṅkutalāl. ēri ilaṅku eṉappaṭṭatu pōlum!
mīṉka ṇaṟṟē ataṉcuṉi  eṉṟu kapilar paṟampumalic cuṉiyi nilāviṟku oppiṭṭuk kūṟiyatu kavaṉikkattakkatu.
ilaṅku - ilañcu - ilañci = ēri, kuḷam.
lañcu (lanj), raṅku (rang) eṉṉum vaṭacoṟkaṭku ilaṅku eṉṉum teṉcollē mūlamākum.
ilaṅku - laṅku - raṅku (va)
laṅku - lañcu - rañcu - rakta (va)
ilaṅku - ilaṅki = āṟṟiṭikkuṟi, tīvu, irāvaṇaṉ āṇṭa nakaram allatu tīvu.
perunīriṭippaṭṭa ciṟunilam taṉittu viḷaṅkuvatāl ilaṅki eṉappaṭṭatu pōlum!
ilaṅku-ilakku = viḷaṅku, viḷakkam.
ilakkutal = colippittal, viḷaṅkavittal, varital, eḻututal, kuṟittal. irīraki yilakkuka (civaca. potu.274).
varaṇantīṭṭip paṭam varivatum eḻutteḻutuvatum viḷaṅka vittalākum. mutaṟkālattil paṭaveḻuttē (Linoglyph)  vaḻakki liruntatu. iṉṟum eḻututal eṉṉuñ col. varaṇappaṭam varitaliyum eḻutteḻututaliyuṅ kuṟittal kāṇka. kaṇṇukku maṟintuḷḷa poruḷkaḷi  viḷakku viḷakkutal pōla, eḻuttum kaṇṇukku maṟintuḷḷa karuttukaḷi viḷakkuvatākum, eḻutalum kuṟittalum oṉṟē.
ilakku - ilakki = eḻutu.
ivvuruvu neñceṉṉuṅ kiḻiyiṉmē liruntilakkittu  (cīvaka.180)
ilakku allatu ilakki eṉṉun teṉcollē vaṭamoḻiyil lik eṉat tiriyum, itu mikamikak kavaṉikkattakkatu.
lik=eḻutu, likita= eḻutappaṭṭatu. lipi=eḻuttu, lēkak= eḻuttāḷaṉ, lēka=eḻutappaṭṭatu, lēkaṉī= eḻutukōl- ivvāṟu ilakku eṉṉuñ col vaṭamoḻiyil palavāṟākat tiricoṟkaḷi uṇṭu paṇṇiyuḷḷatu.
ilakku = kuṟipporuḷ, ampeyyuṅ kuṟi, aṭiyāḷam, kuṟitta iṭam. kuṟitta camiyam, kuṟitta allatu nāṭiya poruḷ, porakkuṟitta etiri. kuṟitta aḷavu.
ilakku vittal = kuṟivittal, ilakkuppārttal = kuṟi pārttal,
camiyam pārttal - ivi iṉṟum tamiḻ vaḻakkām.
ilakka muṭampiṭumpik keṉṟu kalakkattik
kiyāṟāk koḷḷātām mēl   (kuṟaḷ.627)
eṉṟār tiruvaḷḷuvar.
oru kuṟitta iṭatti iṉṟum ilakku eṉpar nelli nāṭṭār. ilakku eṉṉum collē, ilattīṉil locus eṉṟu tirintu, āṅkilattil, local, locate/ locative mutaliya coṟkaḷip piṟappikkum. kuṟitta iṭam eṉṉum ciṟappup poruḷit tarum tamiḻccol, ilattīṉil taṉ ciṟappup poruḷiyiḻantu, iṭam eṉṉum potupporuḷiyē tarutal kāṇka. iṅṅaṉamē, oppōṉ kēṭṭalik kuṟikkum tā eṉṉum tamiḻc col, ilattīṉil kēṭṭali maṭṭum kuṟikkum do eṉṉum ticiccollākat tirintuḷḷatu.
eyvataṟkuk koṇṭa kuṟiyaṉṟi, eṭuttukkāṭṭuvataṟkuk koṇṭa kuṟiyum, ilakku eṉṟē collappaṭum.
uṭaṉpiṟappaṉpiṟku ilakkāṉavar iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ  eṉṉum coṟṟoṭaril, ilakkāṉavar eṉpatu eṭuttukkāṭ ṭāṉavar eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum. ipporuḷil ilakku eṉṉum col ilakkiyam eṉṉum vaṭivam eytum, oru karutti allatu kūṟṟi ilaṅka (viḷaṅka) vikkum poruḷ ilakku eṉappaṭṭatu. āṅkilattiluḷḷa illustrate eṉṉum collum ipporuḷatē. L. illustro = to light up, to illuminate, il = in, lustro = to make light. 
ilakku - ilakkam = oḷi, kuṟipporuḷ, eṇkuṟi, eṇṇiṭam, eṇ, pēreṇṇākiya nūṟāyiram.
ilakkamiṭutal = kaṇakkiṭutal.
mutalāvatu nūṟāyirattiṟkum nūṟu nūṟāyirattiṟkum tamiḻil taṉiccol illi. piṉṉar, pērilakkamākiya nūṟāyiram ilakka meṉṟum, kaṭici yilakkamākiya nūṟu nūṟāyiram kōṭi yeṉṟum collappaṭṭaṉa.
ilakkam - lakṣa(va) - lāk(u)- lac (E) 
kōṭi - kōṭṭi (va) - (kuṭōṭu (u)  crore (E) 
ilakku - ilakkar, ilakkāram = āṭi
āṭi āḷkaḷi viḷaṅkac ceyvatāl ilakkar eṉappaṭṭatu.
āṭi yillātavaṉ arimaṉitaṉ
ilakku - ilakki = āṭi, campaḷam.
campaḷam oru kuṟiyetirppātalāl ilakki yeṉap peyar peṟṟatu.
ilaṅku - ilañcu - ilañciṉi - ilāñciṉi = kuṟi, aṭi yāḷam, muttiri, ilañciṉi - ilacciṉi = kuṟi, muttiri, ilāñciṉi - lāñcaṉa (va).
eḻuttik kuṟikkum ilakku eṉṉum col, ākupeyarāyc colliyum moḻiyiyum uṇartti, aṇam vikuti peṟṟu moḻiyiṉ nūṟṟokutiyiyuṅ kuṟikkum.
Logos eṉṉum kirēkkac col colliyum, kaṭṭuriyiyum nūliyuṅ kuṟittal kāṇka. ac collum ilakkam eṉṉum teṉcollukku iṉamāṉatē.
GK. gramma = a letter; gramma - grammar (E.)
L. litera = a letter; litera - literature (E.)
iṅṅaṉam, āṅkilattiluḷḷa ilakkaṇavilakkiyap peyar kaḷum, eḻuttik kuṟikkuñ colliṉiṉṟē piṟantuḷḷami kavaṉikkattakkatu.
ilakkaṇam eṉṉum teṉcolliṉiṉṟē, lakṣaṇā eṉṉum vaṭacollum tirikkappaṭṭu, piṉpu tamiḻil ilakkaṇi eṉa vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu.
lakṣaṇā = kuṟippup poruḷ.
viṭṭa vilakkaṇi, viṭāta vilakkaṇi, viṭṭum viṭāta vilak kaṇi ākiya mūṉṟum kuṟippāyp poruḷuṇarttuvaṉavē. ilakkaṇikkiṉamāṉa ākupeyar kuṟippāyp poruḷuṇarttu vaṉavaṟṟōṭu (pavaṇantiyārāl) cērkkap peṟṟiruttal kāṇka.
tamiḻil, ilakkaṇam ilakkiyam eṉṉum coṟkaḷ, vērp poruḷit tāṅki niṉṟu, eḻuttiyuṅ kuṟittu,  Grammar, literature eṉṉum poruḷit tarukiṉṟaṉa. vaṭamoḻiyilō, avaṟṟukku nērāṉa lakṣaṇam lakṣyam eṉṉum coṟkaḷ vērccol aṟṟu, eḻuttik kuṟikkumpōtu lik eṉa vēṟoru vaṭivam pūṇṭu, ilakkaṇa vilakkiyattik kuṟikkātu kuṇaṅkuṟip poruḷiyē tāṅki niṟkiṉṟaṉa. ivvēṟu pāṭṭik kaṇṭu koḷka.
itukāṟum kūṟiyavaṟṟāl, ilakkaṇam, ilakkiyam eṉṉum colliraṇṭum teṉcollē yeṉṟu teṟṟeṉat teḷika.
ilakkaṇam, ilakkiyam eṉṉum iraṇṭiyuṅ kuṟikkat tamiḻil  aṇaṅkam aṇaṅkiyam eṉa vēṟiru coṟkaḷum uḷa. añcaṇaṅkam - aintilakkaṇam, añcaṇaṅkiyam - aintilakkiyam (yāḻppāṇa akarāti), aṇaṅkam - ilakkaṇam (potiki nikaṇṭu). aṇaṅkutal - olittal. pukiyaṇaṅka (puṟapporuḷ veṇpāmali 10, 18). ivi ceṉṉip palkalik kaḻaka akarātiyiṉ iṇippu maṭalattilum kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
aṇaṅku=oli, eḻuttu, aṇaṅku - aṇaṅkam = ilakkaṇam.
aṇaṅku - aṇaṅkiyam = ilakkiyam.
aṇaṅku eṉṉum viṉiccol tūya taṉittamiḻc collāta lil, aṇaṅkam, aṇaṅkiyam eṉṉum peyarkaḷi vaṭacol leṉat taliciṟanta tamiḻp pakivarum vāytiṟavār eṉpatu teḷitarutēṟṟam.  - centamiḻc celvi   ēppiral 1948

ilakkiyam
ilakkiyam - lakṣya
ilakku - ilakkam = ciṟanta vāḻkkiyilakki viḷakkik kūṟum vaṉappu (kāviyam)
vaṭamoḻiyil lakṣya eṉṟa coṟkum ilakkāṉa poruḷ eṉṟē poruḷ kūṟuvar.  (va.va : 90)
ilakkiyam, ilakkaṇam
vaṭamoḻiyum teṉmoḻiyum eṉatu uṉatu eṉṟu, eṅkun toṭarn tetirvaḻakkāṭaṟ kiṭamāṉa eṇṇiṟanta coṟkaḷuḷ, ilakkiyam. ilakkaṇam eṉpaṉa iraṇṭām.
ulakil aṭuttaṭuttu vāḻum makkaḷ tammuṭ koṇṭuṅ koṭuttum poruḷkaḷi māṟṟik koḷvatu pōlum, aṭut taṭuttu vāḻum vakuppār allatu nāṭṭār tammuṭ paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷi māṟṟik koḷvatu pōlum, aṭuttaṭuttu vaḻaṅkum moḻikaḷum tammuṭ koṇṭuṅ koṭuttum coṟ kaḷi māṟṟik koḷkiṉṟaṉa.
iṉi, ulakamoḻikaḷellām orutāy vayiṟṟiṉaveṉṟu moḻi nūl vallār kaṇṭa muṭiviṟkēṟpa. aṭuttu vaḻaṅkātu neṭṭiṭiyiṭṭu vaḻaṅkum moḻikaṭkum āyirakkaṇakkāṉa coṟkaḷ potuvāyiruttali ārāycciyāṟ kāṇalām. āṅkilam ēṟattāḻa 6,000 milkaṭkappāl vaḻaṅkiṉum āyirak kaṇakkāṉa teṉcoṟkaḷit taṉṉakattuk koṇṭuḷḷatu. utāraṇamākac cila coṟkaḷi īṇṭuk kāṭṭutum.
Abba -  appaṉ.	Abbot, the father of an abbey.
