iccoṟkaḷ ellām āṅkilattiṉiṉṟum tamiḻukku vantavi yalla. āṅkilam varumuṉṉarē ivi tamiḻil uḷḷaṉa. āṟā yiram kalliṭiyiṭṭa āṅkilattiṟkum tamiḻukkum ittuṇip potuc coṟkaḷirukkumāyiṉ. ēṟattāḻa 5,000 āṇṭukaḷāka aṭuttaṭuttu vaḻaṅkum vaṭamoḻikkum teṉmoḻikkum ettuṇip potuc coṟkaḷiruttal vēṇṭum? ippotuc coṟkaḷellām moḻi nūṉ muṟiyil kūrntu nōkku vārkkut tūya teṉcoṟkaḷeṉṟē tōṉṟā niṟkum, vaṭa moḻikkum teṉmoḻikkum potuvāyuḷḷa coṟkaḷi, vaṭacolleṉpārum teṉ colleṉpārumām irucārār moḻi nūlaṟiñar. vaṭamoḻiyip pōṉṟē teṉmoḻiyum ciṟantatoru tāymoḻiyātaliṉ, vaṭamoḻiyiṉiṉṟum teṉmoḻikku vantuḷḷa coṟkaḷip pōlavē teṉmoḻi yiṉiṉṟum vaṭamoḻikkuc ceṉṟuḷḷa coṟkaḷum paṉṉūṟ ṟukkaṇakkiṉa. avaṟṟuḷ iraṇṭu ilakkiyam, ilakkaṇam eṉpaṉa.  (tamiḻppoḻil viṭi 1934)
ilakkaṇavuri vaḻukkaḷ
1.	oṟṟaḷapeṭi
ṅañaṇa namaṉa  eṉṉum naṉṉūṟ cūttirattiṟku.
cūttirattuk kūṟappaṭṭa pattoṟṟum āytam oṉṟumākap patiṉō reḻuttum, kuṟiṟkīḻiṭi, kuṟiliṇik kīḻiṭi, kuṟiṟ kīḻikkaṭi, kuṟiliṇik kīḻkkaṭi eṉṉum nāṉkiṭattum aḷapeṭukka oṟṟaḷa peṭi mottam nāṟpattu nāṉkām. avaṟṟuḷ āyta māṉatu iyalpīṟāka vēṉum vitiyīṟāka vēṉum colluk kīṟṟil vārāmiyāl kuṟiṟ kīḻkkaṭi kuṟilik kīḻkkaṭi eṉṉum iraṇṭiṭattiyuṅ kaḻikka oṟṟaḷapeṭi nāṟpat tiraṇṭātal kāṇka eṉṟē muṉṉip piṉṉi uriyāciriya rellām urittār.
kuṟṟiyalukaram kuṟiṟkīḻaṉṟik kuṟiliṇikkīḻ vārāteṉpatu.
kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉa rāytap puḷḷi
uyiroṭu puṇarnta vallāṟaṉ micittē
eṉṉum cūttirattāṟ ṟeḷivām. ataṉ uriyilum.
vilaḵḵki vīṅkiru ḷōṭṭumē māta
rilaḵḵku mutti ṉiṉam
eṉa ivvāṟu kuṟiliṇik kīḻum āytam varumālōveṉiṉ, aṉṉa iyalpākavē āytamāy niṟpaṉavalla, ōci niṟittaṟ poruṭṭu oṟṟilvaḻi oṟṟākkutaliṉ, īṇṭu āytamum oṟṟāy varumeṉak koḷka eṉṟār mayilinātar. ākavē kuṟiliṇikkīḻ āytam vārāteṉpatu peṟappaṭṭatu.
iṉi,  al + tiṇi = aḵṟiṇi, muḷ + tītu = muḵṭītu. āv + kaṭiya = aḵkaṭiya eṉa vitiyīṟāy āytam varutaliṉ īṟṟil vārāteṉpatum pōliyuriyeṉa maṟukkappaṭum. ākavē, āytam kuṟiṟkīḻiṭi kuṟiṟkīḻkkaṭi eṉṉum īriṭattum vantu aḷapeḻum eṉṟum, kuṟiliṇik kīḻiṭi kuṟiliṇik kīḻkkaṭi eṉṉumīriṭattum vārāteṉṟum, av viraṇṭiṭat tiyumē kaḻikka oṟṟaḷa peṭi mottam nāṟpattiraṇṭām eṉṟum kaṇṭukoḷka.
2.	ēvalorumi
naṭavā maṭicī eṉṉum ēvalviṉic cūttira uriyil cey eṉṉum pakutiyoṭu āy vikuti puṇarntu ceyyāy eṉa niṉṟu. piṉpu avvikuti keṭṭu mīṇṭum cey eṉa niṟpatē ēvalorumi yeṉac cilar urittār.
vikuti puṇarntu keṭuvatu iṅkilīcu camaṟkirutam pōṉṟa āriyamoḻikaṭ kiyalpēyaṉṟit tamiḻukkiyalpākātu. Cut, set  eṉṉum viṉikaḷ iṟanta kālattil vikuti peṟṟiḻantu mīṇṭum pakutiyaḷavākavēyirukkum.
vikuti puṇarntu keṭuvatāyiṉ, aṅṅaṉam keṭāta cila viṉikaḷumiruttal vēṇṭum. aṅṅaṉa miṉmiyiṉ cey eṉṉum vāypāṭē ēvalorumi yeṉṟu tuṇiyappaṭum.
mēlum, ceyyāy eṉpatu muṉṉili etirmaṟi orumi etirkāla viṉimuṟṟuc col. atu ōci vēṟupāṭṭāṉ vēṇṭu kōḷāṅkāl ceyyāyā eṉa viṉāpporuḷ paṭṭup piṉpu avviṉāvē cey eṉa uṭaṉpāṭṭup poruḷ tantu niṟkum.
ēvalorumi vikuti puṇarntu keṭuvatāyiṉ ēvaṟpaṉmiyum vikuti puṇarntu keṭal vēṇṭum. aṅṅaṉam keṭāmiyiṉ, orumi vikuti maṭṭum puṇarntu keṭumeṉṟal poruntātu.
iṉṉōraṉṉa iṭaṅkaḷilellām vaṭamoḻiyiyal vēṟu teṉmoḻi yiyal vēṟu eṉpatit teṟṟeṉa vuṇartal vēṇṭum. aṅṅaṉam uṇarātārē.
ceyeṉ ēval viṉippakāp patamē
eṉa veḷḷiṭiyāy āciriyar kūṟiṉum ataṉiyum uṇarātu ivvāṟu mayaṅkak kūṟuvar.
3.	toḻiṟpeyar vikuti
pāṭu eṉpatu oru toḻiṟ peyar vikuti yeṉṟum, kōṭpāṭu eṉpatu ataṟkutāraṇa meṉṟum māṇavarkkup pāṭamāyuḷḷa ilakkaṇaṅkaḷellām mayaṅkik kūṟāniṟkum.
kōḷ eṉpatu oru mutaṉili tirinta toḻiṟpeyar. piṉpu paṭu eṉṉum tuṇiviṉi cērntu atuvum mutaṉili tirintu pāṭu eṉṟāyiṟṟu. pāṭu eṉpatu vikutiyāyiṉ kōḷ eṉpatu pakuti yātal vēṇṭum. aṅṅaṉamākāmiyiṉ aḵturi yaṉmi yaṟika. puṟappaṭu, kaṭappaṭu eṉpavi puṟappāṭu kaṭappāṭu eṉṟā ṉāṟpōlak kōṭpaṭu eṉpatum kōṭpāṭu eṉṟāyiṟṟu.
vāypāṭu eṉpatilō veṉiṉ atilum paṭu eṉṉum tuṇi viṉiyē mutaṉili tirintatātal vēṇṭum.
4.	eḻuvāyurupu
avaṟṟuḷ,
eḻuvā yurupu tiripil peyarē
eṉṉum cūttira vuriyil, tamakkeṉa vārāycciyiṉṟic cuvāmināta tēcikar pōṉṟa piṟar kūṟṟukaḷiyē koṇṭu kūṟum orucār āciriyar.
eḻuvāy vēṟṟumikku ai mutalāṉavi pōṉṟa urupu illātirun tālum, uriyiṟkōṭal eṉkiṟa uttiyāl āṉavaṉ, ākiṉṟavaṉ, āvāṉ; eṉṟavaṉ, eṉkiṉṟavaṉ, eṉpavaṉ; avaṉ mutalāka aimpālilum varukiṟa col lurupukaḷ uṇṭeṉak koḷka  eṉṟu vaḻuppaṭavurittaṉar. avar tamiḻilakkaṇattiyē aṟiyār.
eḻuvāykku iyaṟki vaṭivaṉṟi vēṟurupilli yeṉṟu tolkāppiyamum naṉṉūlum tulaṅkak kūṟum.
āṉavaṉ mutaliya mūṉṟum, eṉṟavaṉ mutaliya mūṉṟum muṟiyē ā (āku), eṉ eṉṟa viṉippakutikaḷiṉiṉṟum piṟanta viṉiyā laṇiyum peyarkaḷ. vēṟṟumi urupellām peyarkku īṟāyaṉṟit tamittu vārā. iṭiccollātaliṉ tamittu varuvatum peyarccollāyiruppatum, aimpāl kāṭṭuvatum avaṟṟukku ilakkaṇa mākā. oru peyarkku maṟṟoru peyar urupāy varumeṉṟal eṭṭuṇiyum poruntāta toṉṟām.
koṇṭu, nimittam mutaliya viṉiyum peyarum piṟa vēṟṟumi kaḷukkuc collurupāy varumē yeṉiṉ, aviyum avvav vēṟṟumip poruḷpaṭa varumēyaṉṟi avitāṉē vēṟṟumi yurupaṉmiyum, iṭiccol lākāmiyum aṟintu koḷka.
āṉavaṉ mutaliya āṟu peyarkaḷum, cāttaṉ, cātti eṉṟa peyarkaḷip pōlavē āṉavaṉi, āṉavaṉāl, āṉavaṉukku eṉa eṇ vēṟṟumippaṭutaliṉ, avi peyarkaḷēyaṉṟi vēṟṟumiyurupākāmi yaṟika. peyarukku vēṟṟumi yaṉṟi vēṟṟumiyurupiṟku vēṟṟumi yiṉṟu.
cāttaṉ, cātti mutaliya peyarkaḷ mikak kuṟuki yiruttaliṉ, kaṭṭuri vakiyāṉ ōci nīṭaṟporuṭṭu cāttaṉāṉavaṉ, cāttiyāṉavaḷ eṉṟu cilarāṟ kūṟappaṭuvataṉṟip piṟa vēṟṟumi yurupukaḷip pōla evviṭattum ellārāṉum kūṟappaṭuva talla.
ākavē mutal vēṟṟumikku urupuṇṭeṉpatu pōliyuri yeṉa maṟukka.

5.	eḻuvāyc col
eḻuvāy peyarccollākavē yirukkumeṉṟu itukāṟum ellāc ciṟṟilakkaṇaṅkaḷuḷḷum kūṟappaṭṭatu. atu ārāycciyiṉṟi vaḻivaḻi vantatōr kūṟṟām.
eḻuvāy nikaḻkāla viṉiyeccamākavum irukkum.
e-ṭu : avaṉukkup pāṭat teriyum = avaṉukkup pāṭṭut teriyum.
pāṭa eṉpatu eḻuvāy.
āṅkilattil  infinitive mood (nikaḻkāla viṉiyeccam) eḻuvāyāy varutali oppunōkkuka. iṉṉōraṉṉa iṭaṅkaḷi lellām ottup pārvi ilakkaṇamē (Comparative Grammar) payaṉpaṭu meṉka.
iṉic ceyappaṭuporuḷum eḻuvāy pōla nikaḻkāla viṉiyeccamā yirukkumeṉṟaṟika.
e-ṭu : avaṉ pāṭat terintu koṇṭāṉ = avaṉ pāṭṭit terintu koṇṭāṉ.
pāṭa ceyappaṭuporuḷ.
6.	uric col  (Poetic Diction)
uriccoṟ kiḷavi virikkuṅ kāli
........................................................
eccol lāyiṉum poruḷvēṟu kiḷattal   (tolcol 782)
eṉṉum tolkāppiyar cūttirattiṟkuc cēṉāvariyar
tamakkiyalpillā viṭiccoṟ pōlātu ici kuṟippup paṇpeṉṉum poruṭkut tāmē yuriyavātaliṉ, uric col lāyiṟṟu. perumpāṉmiyuñ ceyyuṭkuriyavāy varutaliṉ uriccollāyiṟṟeṉpāru muḷar.
ici kuṟippup paṇpeṉṉum poruḷavāyp peyar viṉi. pōṉṟum avaṟṟiṟku mutaṉiliyāyun taṭumāṟi oru col poruṭkurittātalē yaṉṟi orucoṟ palaporuṭkum pala col oru poruṭkum uriyavāy varuvaṉa uriccolleṉ ṟum. avi peyarum viṉiyum pōla īṟupaṟṟip poru ḷuṇartta lākāmiyiṉ veḷippaṭāta vaṟṟi veḷippaṭṭa vaṟṟōṭu cārttit tammi yeṭuttōtiyē apporu ḷuṇarttap paṭumeṉṟum, uriccoṟkup potuvilakkaṇamum avaṟṟiṟkup poruḷuṇarttu muṟimiyu muṇarttiyavāṟu.
meytaṭumāṟalum orucoṟ pala poruṭ kurimiyum palacol oruporuṭkurimiyum uriccoṟku uṇmiyāṉ ōtiṉā rēṉum, uriccoṟku ilakkaṇamāvatu ici kuṟippup paṇ peṉṉum poruṭkuriyavāy varutalē yām eṉṟu uri varintār.
nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum ivvāṟē uri kūṟiṉār.
ellāc coṟkaḷam perumpālum ici kuṟippup paṇpu paṟṟiyē tōṉṟiyiruttaliṉ, ici kuṟippup  paṇpiṟkuriyaṉa uriccolleṉpatu poruntā; poruntumāyiṉ ici kuṟippup paṇpu paṟṟiya coṟkaḷellām uric collātal vēṇṭum. aṅṅaṉamākāmiyum, iciyuṅ kuṟippum paṇpi laṭaṅkutalum, paṇpup peyar aṟuvakip peyaruḷ oṉṟā talum, orucār toḻilum paṇpiṉpāṟpaṭṭut toḻiṟ paṇpeṉappaṭutalum, viṉic coṟkaḷiṟ perumpālaṉa iciyuṅ kuṟippum paṟṟi varutalum, ilakkaṇa muṇarntārk  kellām eḷitu pulaṉām.
meytaṭumāṟal coṟkaḷukkellām potuviyalpātaliṉ atuvum oru ciṟappilakkaṇamaṉṟu.
oru coṟ palaporuṭ kurimiyum palacol oruporuṭ kurimiyum peyar viṉi yiṭi yuri nāṉkiṟkum potuvāṉa tiricol ilakkaṇamātali,
oru poruḷ kuṟitta vēṟucol lākiyum
vēṟuporuḷ kuṟitta oru col lākiyum
irupāṟ ṟeṉpa tiricoṟ kiḷavi   (tol. 882)
eṉpataṉāṟ kaṇṭukoḷka.
peyarkkum viṉikkum urimi pūṇṭu niṟpaṉa uriccol leṉiṉ, iṭiccollum aṅṅaṉam urimi pūṇṭu niṟṟaliṉ atuvum ilakkaṇamaṉmi yaṟika.
iṉi, uriccol ilakkaṇantāṉ eṉṉiyō veṉiṟ kūṟutum:
colleṉap paṭupa peyarē viṉiyeṉ
ṟāyiraṇ ṭeṉpa aṟintici ṉōrē   (tol. 643)
eṉpatāl ilakkaṇa vakiyiṟ coṟkaḷ peyar viṉi eṉa iraṇṭēyeṉpatum, iṭiccollāvaṉa ap peyariṭi yum viṉiyiṭiyum varum uṟuppukaḷeṉpatum, avvuṟuppu kaḷiṉṟip peyarp pakupataṅkaḷum viṉippakupataṅkaḷumilli yeṉpatum, taṉittuvarum iṭiccoṟ kaḷellām peyar viṉi yoṉṟaṉuḷ aṭaṅku meṉpatum, uriccol leṉpatōr ilakkaṇavakic collaṉ ṟeṉpatum, atu peyarākavum viṉiyākavu mirukkumeṉpatum, ceyyuṭkuriya tiricoṟkaṭ kellām poruḷuṇarttuvatē uriyiyaleṉpa tum, ceyyuṭkuriya coṟkaḷē uriccol leṉappaṭṭaṉa veṉpatum aṟiyappaṭum.
naṉṉūlārum.
palvakip paṇpum pakarpeya rāki
orukuṇam palakuṇan taḻuvip peyarviṉi
oruvā ceyyuṭ kuriyaṉa vuriccol  (naṉ.442)
eṉat teḷḷitiṟ ṟerittār.
atuvumaṉṟi,
iṉṉa tiṉṉuḻi iṉṉaṇa miyalum
eṉṟici nūluṭ kuṇakuṇip peyarkaḷ
collām parattaliṟ piṅkala mutalā
nallō ruriccolil nayantaṉar koḷalē.  (naṉ. 460)
eṉavum puṟaṉaṭi yōtiṉār.
itil piṅkala mutaliya nikaṇṭukaḷi yellām uriccol leṉṟamiyum, avi ceyyuṭkuriya palavaki yaruñcoṟ kaḷukkum poruḷ kūṟutaliyum, avaṟṟuḷ kāṅkēyar nikaṇṭu uriccolleṉṟē peyar peṟṟamiyum kaṇṭukoḷka. tamiḻaṟivum tiricol vaḻakkum mikkirunta kālattil ceyyuṭ coṟkaḷellām iyaṟcoṟpōl eḷitāyp poruḷ viḷakkuvaṉavāyiṉa. piṉpu avi kuṉṟiya kālattu avaṟṟit taṉittaṉi akarātiyiyalpil eṭuttōtip poruḷkūṟa vēṇṭiyatāyiṟṟu.
peyarum viṉiyumpōla īṟupaṟṟip poruḷuṇartta lākāmiyiṉ, tammi yeṭuttōtiyē ap poruḷuṇarttap paṭutalāṉum eṉṟār cēṉāvariyarum. ovvorucolli yum taṉittaṉiyeṭuttōtip poruḷ kūṟuvateṉṟāl, atu ceyyuṭ collāy ulaka vaḻakki lillātateṉpatu eḷitiṟ pulaṉām, uriccoṟkaṭkellām poruḷ kūṟuvatit tolkāppiya uriyiyaluṭ kaṇṭukoḷka. uriyiyaleṉpatē ceyyuṭkuriya aruñcoṟkaṭ kellām poruḷuṇarttum akarātit toṭakkam. piṉpu atu virivāṉa nūlāy nīṇṭu nikaṇṭu eṉappaṭṭatu. nikaḷ-nīḷ. akalam nikaḷam eṉpatōr paṇṭi vaḻakku. nikaḷam - caṅkili;  nīḷamāyiruppatu. nikaḷam nīḷameṉa maruviṟṟu.
poruṭkup poruṭeriyi ṉatuvaram piṉṟē  (tol. col. 874)
moḻipporuṭ kāraṇam viḻippat tōṉṟā  (tol.col. 877)
eṉat tolkāppiyar uriyiyaluṭ potuppaṭak kūṟuvatum itaṉi valiyuṟuttum.
peyarum viṉiyumē mutaṉmutaluṇṭāṉa coṟkaḷ. avaṟṟiṉiṉṟum piṟkālattu iṭiccol vakukkappaṭṭatu. ataṟkum piṉpu uriccoṟ pākupāṭuṇṭāyiṟṟu.



7. iṭukuṟip peyar
a,ā eṉṟaṟ ṟoṭakkattaṉa, iṭukuṟic ciṟappup peyar  eṉpatu naṉṉūṟ paḻiyavuri.
a, ā eṉpavi eḻuttukaḷē yaṉṟi avaṟṟiṉ peyarkaḷākā. eḻuttāvatu oli, oru poruḷ vēṟu, ataṉ peyar vēṟu, kuṭam eṉpatu oru poruḷ, apporuḷ vēṟu; atik kuṟikkum kuṭam eṉṉum col vēṟu. a, ā eṉpavi eḻuttukaḷākiya poruḷkaḷ, uyir, kuṟil, neṭil eṉpaṉavē avaṟṟiṉ peyarkaḷ, oru poruḷ peyariṉṟiyum yātāṉu mōr pulaṉākac cuṭṭappaṭum. a,ā eṉpavi peyarkaḷāyiṉ avaṟṟāṟ kuṟikkappaṭum poruḷkaḷ aviyaṉṟi vēṟātal vēṇṭum. aṅṅaṉamiṉmiyiṉ aḵturi yaṉmi yaṟika.
aṉṟiyum iṭukuṟiyeṉṟōr peyar yāṇṭumilatu.
ellāc collum poruḷkuṟit taṉavē
moḻipporuṭ kāraṇam viḻippat tōṉṟā
eṉṟār āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār.
aṟṟēl, makkaṭkiṭum iyaṟpeyarkaḷōveṉiṉ, aviyum mutalāvatu iṭappaṭumpōtu kāraṇap peyarkaḷākavē yiruntiruttal vēṇṭum. (e-ṭu: murukaṉ = iḷiñaṉ.  Victor -  veṟṟi peṟṟavaṉ) piṉṉarum avi, teyvappaṟṟu, atikāri māṭṭaṉpu. iṉamuṟi, iṉṉōci, viḻumiya poruḷ, uyarnōkkam eṉpavaṟṟuḷ oṉṟu paṟṟiyē makkaṭkiṭappaṭṭu varukiṉṟaṉa.
makkaṭkiṭappaṭum iyaṟpeyarellām mutalāvatu poruḷ kuṟittaṉaveṉṟum, piṉpu makkaṭpaṉmiyāl makavu tōṟum putup peyariṭutalarumi paṟṟip paḻampeyarkaḷē mīṇṭum mīṇṭum piṟarkkiṭappaṭukiṉṟaṉa veṉṟum peyiṉ (Bain) eṉṉum āṅkilap pulavar tam ilakkaṇattiṟ kūṟiyuḷḷār.
iṭukuṟippeya ruṇṭeṉṉum iḻukkuṇarcciyē cilar tavaṟ ṟārāyccikkum kāraṇamāyiruntu varukiṉṟatu.
tāvaraṅkaḷuḷ iṭukuṟip peyaruṭiyaviyellām tamiḻ nāṭṭiṟkuriya veṉṟum, kāraṇap peyaruṭiyavi yellām ayalnāṭṭiṟkuriyaveṉṟum, veṟṟili (veṟumi +  ili) miḷakāy (miḷaku + kāy) mutaliyavi kāraṇap peyaruṭimi yāl ayalnāṭṭiṉiṉṟu vantaviyeṉṟum, vāḻi, vēmpu mutaliyavi iṭukuṟip peyaruṭimiyāl tamiḻnāṭṭiṟkuriya veṉṟum, iyaṟkip poruṭ kaṭṭuri  eṉṉum nūliṟ kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
ovvoru poruḷukkum taṉimoḻiyāyum toṭar moḻiyā yum oṉṟum palavum peyarirukkumeṉṟum. coṟkaḷ tōṉṟi ūḻikkaṇakkāṉa kālaṅkaḷākiyiruttaliṉ avaṟṟuṭ palavaṟ ṟiṉ poruḷkaḷ iṟantum maṟintum irukkiṉṟaṉa veṉṟum, veṟṟilikku aṭi eṉṟu maṟupeyar paṇṭi nūlkaḷiṟ kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷateṉṟum, avariyiṉattic cērnta kāy kottavari yeṉṉappaṭṭāṟpōl, miḷakiṉattic cērnta kāy miḷakā yeṉṉappaṭṭateṉṟum, vaḻavaḻa veṉṟiruppatu vāḻiyeṉṟum, vemmiyāṉa kālattil taḻippatu vēmpu eṉṟum annūlāciriyar aṟintilar pōlum.
ikkālattiṟ cilar āṅkilamuṇarnta māttiriyāṉē tamiḻu muṇarntōmeṉṟu tamiḻārāyat toṭaṅkukiṉṟaṉar. atu nālvar kaṇṇilār nālvāy kaṇṭa tokkum.
itukāṟum kūṟiyavaṟṟi oṉṟiṉa muṭittal taṉṉiṉa muṭittal eṉṉum uttiyāṟ koḷka.

8. pōli (naṉ. 122, uri)
mutaṟpōli iṭippōlikaḷiṉpiṉ muṟiyē colla vēṇṭiya ivviṟutip pōliyi muṉṉē coṉṉataṉāl. karumpu curumpar, vaṇṭu - vaṇṭar, ciṟaku - ciṟakar, iṭakku - iṭakkar eṉac cilar kuṟṟiyalukarap peyariṉiṟuti ukarattukku ar eṉpatu pōliyāka varutalum.... koḷka  eṉpatu.
kaṭip pōliyiṉ ciṟappup paṟṟiyum ataṉ peruvaḻakkup paṟṟiyumē atu mutaliṟ kūṟappaṭṭatu. atu orukāl oṉṟik kuṟippiṉum. pantal - pantar, kuṭal - kuṭar pōṉṟa pōlikaḷē koḷḷappaṭumallatu vaṇṭu - vaṇṭar, ciṟaku - ciṟakar eṉpavi koḷḷappaṭā.
kuṟṟiyalukaraṅkaḷ mikak kuṟukiya ōciyuṭiyaṉavāyc colluk kīṟṟiliruppatu uccarippiṟ keḷitāyirāmi paṟṟi avaṟṟi (nīṭṭi) eḷitākkuvataṟku am, al, ar, i mutaliya cāriyikaḷ varuvatuṇṭu. avi pōliyākā. ar eṉpatu pōliyāyiṉ ēṉiyavum pōliyātal vēṇṭum. avi cāriyiyātaliṉ aḵtu pōliyuriyeṉa maṟukka.
e-ku: 	kuṉṟu - kuṉṟam	cukku - cukkal
	neñcu - neñcam	kuccu - kucci
	iṭakku - iṭakkar	kuñcu - kuñci
ar eṉpatu cāriyiyāka nūlkaḷiṟ kūṟappaṭavilli. āyiṉum, atu cāriyi yeṉṟē koḷḷaṟpāṟṟu. al eṉpataṉ īṟṟup pōli eṉiṉum amiyum.
iṉi, pittu - piccu, vitta - vicca eṉat takarattukkuc cakaram pōliyāka varutalum aṭaṅkum vaḻiyaṟintu amittuk koḷka  eṉpatu mēṟcūttira muṭivuriyākum.
piccu, vicca eṉpavi ceyyuḷ vikāramēyaṉṟip pōliyākā, avi pōliyāyiṉ kocci  (Barbarism)  yeṉpatoṉ ṟiṉṟik kallāmākkaḷ pēcuvatellām pōliyuḷaṭaṅku meṉṟum.
ācceṉṟā lāyiram pāṭṭā kātā
āppiṭṭukkoṇ ṭaṭipaṭṭa cokkēcaruk kācippaṭṭu
eṉṉum ceyyuḷaṭikaḷiluḷḷa āccu. āppiṭṭu  eṉpavum ulaka vaḻakkil tiruntiya moḻikaḷākumeṉṟum maṟukka.

9. pataviyal
naṉṉūṟ pataviya lurimukattil, iv viyalukku ātāram perumpālum vaṭamoḻiyātalāl atu tōṉṟa moḻi yiyal eṉap peyar kuṟiyāmal pataviyal eṉap peyar kuṟittār pōlum  eṉṟu āciriyar karutti oruvāṟu ūṭuruvi yaṟin tāṟpōṟ kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
tolkāppiyattiṟ pataviya leṉṟōr iyalil pakupata uṟuppukaḷellām tokuttuk kūṟappaṭāviṭiṉum eḻuttati kāram collatikārameṉṉu mīratikāraṅkaḷilum āṅkāṅku vēṟuvēṟu kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
pakutiyum vikutiyum peyariyal viṉiyiyal iṭiyiyalkaḷuḷ, cāriyiyum vikāramum puṇariyalkaḷuḷḷum, iṭinili yoṉṟum puṇariyal iṭiyiyalkaḷuḷḷum tolkāppiyat tiṟ kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷami kāṇka.
tolkāppiyak kālattu makkaḷ nuṇṇaṟiviṉarātaliṉ, avarkkup palaviyalkaḷuḷḷum vēṟu vēṟu kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷa pakupata vuṟuppukaḷi mīṇṭumōriyalil tokuttuk kūṟa vēṇṭātāyiṟṟu. naṉṉūṟ kālattil makkaḷaṟivu kuṟin tamiyiṉ avaṟṟit tokuttuk kūṟavum vēṇṭiyatāyiṟṟu. tolkāppiyattiṟ kūṟappaṭāta pakupata vilakkaṇatti vaṭa moḻi taḻuvip putitāyc ceytār pavaṇantiyāreṉiṉ, avar kālam variyum tamiḻāṉatu āṅkilam pōlap pataviyaṉ moḻi yāyiruntila teṉavum, avaratu naṉṉūlālēyē vaṭamoḻi pōlap pataviyaṉ moḻiyāyiṟṟeṉavum iyaṟkikkum moḻi nūlukkum muraṇpaṭumaṉṟō? ātalāl tolkāppiyar kālat tilēyē pakupata vilakkaṇa miruntateṉṟum, atu tol kāppiyattil āṅkāṅkuk kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷateṉṟum, ati maṟuttumōriyalil tokuttuk kūṟuvatu aṟṟi muṟippaṭi kūṟiyatu kūṟaleṉṉum kuṟṟamākumeṉṟum uyttuṇarntu koḷka.

10.	ticiccol
ticic collāṉatu paṉṉiru koṭuntamiḻ nāṭṭiṉiṉṟum vantu centamiḻil vaḻaṅkum koṭuntamiḻc coṟkaḷēyaṉṟip piṟamoḻic coṟkaḷākā.
centamiḻ cērnta paṉṉiru nilattum
taṅkuṟip piṉavē ticiccoṟ kiḷavi  (tol. col. 883)
eṉṟār āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār. ati yuṇarātu,
centamiḻ nilañcēr paṉṉiru nilattiṉum
oṉpatiṟ ṟiraṇṭiṉil tamimoḻi nilattiṉum
taṅkuṟip piṉavē ticiccoṟ kiḷavi  (naṉ. 273)
eṉṟu patiṉeṇmoḻi nilaṅkaḷiyum kūṭṭiyurittār pavaṇantiyār.
cēṉāvariyar. nacciṉārkkiṉiyar mutaliya uriyāciriya rellām koṭuntamiḻc coṟkaḷiyē ticiccoṟku utāraṇaṅ kāṭṭiṉar. teluṅku, kaṉṉaṭam, maliyāḷam mutaliya moḻikaḷellām paṇṭik koṭuntamiḻkaḷātaliṉ ticic coṟ kutāraṇaṅ kāṭṭappaṭṭaṉa. vaṭamoḻi yoṉṟē tolkāppiyar kālattu intu tēcattil vaḻaṅkiṉa piṟamoḻiyākum. atu ticiccolliṉiṉṟum pirikkappaṭṭu vaṭacolleṉa vitantu kūṟappaṭṭatu. itaṉāl koṭuntamiḻc coṟkaḷē ticic coṟkaḷeṉṟum piṟa moḻikaḷellām vaṭacoṟpōla avvam moḻip peyarālēyē kuṟikkappaṭu meṉṟum, avi ticic colluḷaṭaṅkāveṉṟum peṟappaṭu māṟu kāṇka, iṅkilīcu. intuttāṉi, pōrttukkī mutaliya piṟamoḻic coṟkaḷiyellām ticic collāka ivaṟṟic ciṟṟilakkaṇaṅkaḷ kūṟāniṟkum. avaṟṟi āṅkilac col, intuttāṉiccol, pōrttukkīciyac col eṉa avvam moḻip peyarālēyē kuṟittal vēṇṭum. ivi akkālat tiṉmiyiṉ tolkāppiyattiṟ kūṟappaṭṭila. piṟamoḻic coṟkaḷiyellām tārāḷamāyt tamiḻil vaḻaṅkumāṟu ticic collāk koḷḷa ilakkaṇikaḷ iṭan tantārāyiṉ. ērāḷamāṉa piṟamoḻic coṟkaḷ yātum varampiṉṟit tamiḻiṟ kalantu ataṉ iṉimiyiyum taṉimiyiyum kulittuviṭutal tiṇṇam. atōṭu avi tamiḻukku iṉṟiyamiyātavi yeṉṟum, aviyiṉṟit tamiḻil muṟṟap poruḷaṟivuṟuttalamiyāteṉṟum, eṇṇap paṭum, ātaliṉ ilakkaṇikaḷ iṭantantilar. iṅkilīcu pōṉṟa moḻikaṭkē ticic coṟkaḷ iṉṟiyamiyātaviyākum. tamiḻilirunta ettuṇiyō coṟkaḷ vaḻakkiṉṟi iṟan toḻintaṉa. āyiṉum iṟṟik kālattum, piṟacoṟ kalavātu karuttellāvaṟṟiyum uṇarttavallatu tamiḻ oṉṟēyākum. āṅkilatti vaṭamoḻi yutaviyiṉṟit tamiḻil moḻi peyarkka muṭiyāteṉṟu cilar karutukiṉṟaṉar. mūlappakuti kaḷiṉiṉṟum iyaṟcoṟkaḷiṉiṉṟum tiricoṟkaḷum toṭarccoṟkaḷum ākkumāṟu avaraṟintārāyiṉ aṅṅaṉam karutār. ovvoru moḻiyiṉum piṟamoḻiyic cevviyāy moḻi peyarppatu aritākum. āyiṉum, tamiḻil ēṉiyavaṟ ṟiṟpōl attuṇi aritaṉṟu, itupōtu nūl vaḻakkiṉum ulaka vaḻakkilumuḷḷa tamiḻc coṟkaḷiyē tamiḻ makkaḷ aṟintilar.
tamiḻukku vaṭamoḻi yutavi vēṇṭātirukka, vaṭacolli yēṉ tamiḻilakkaṇaṅ kūṟa vēṇṭumeṉiṉ, vaṭamoḻi tamiḻukku iṭattāl aṇittātaliṉ pōkkuvaravupaṟṟiyum moḻi peyarppup paṟṟiyum vaṭamoḻiyiluḷḷa nūṟpeyar kaḷum āṭpeyarkaḷumāyt tamiḻil vantu vaḻaṅkiya ciṟappup peyarkaḷē vaṭacolleṉap paṭṭaṉaveṉṟum, moḻi vaḻak kiṟkum karuttaṟivippiṟkum vēṇṭiya piṟa potuc coṟkaḷ tamiḻil vantu vaḻaṅkumāṟu kūṟappaṭṭila veṉṟum paṇṭi nūlkaḷellām vaṭamoḻi viravāta centamiḻ nūlkaḷeṉṟum kūṟiviṭukka.
centamiḻ cērnta paṉṉiru nilattum

11. etirmaṟiyummi
cāttaṉ varutaṟkumuriyaṉeṉpatuvārāmikkumuriyaṉeṉṉumetirmaṟiyioḻipākuṭittāyniṟṟaliṉ.etirmaṟi, iḵtu aḵṟiṇiÉravupbpayaÇyalpumāUḷavē vṉapgṇpugṟṟiyumtrum.ïJ ãṟitōrbpāruḷitjḻuvātuxrubpāruḷiṉÉṉiyikṟuttuÃṟṟaliṉvccattiṉnvaṟāyiṟṟu.nkš Máriyarvtirmaṟie¢rm eṉwimayiṉiḵtu vccatâṉkūṟāku«eṉṟuÉrittu¡kūṟiṉā®nacciṉahrkkiṉiyar.
itēcjhuz¤ij ēṉic ciṟṟilakkaṇattārellārum vaḻivaḻi kāṭṭi vantaṉar.
cāttaṉ varutaṟku muriyaṉ eṉpatu varāmikku muriyaṉeṉ patit taḻuvi niṟṟaliṉ, atu ēṉiyatu taḻīiya eccavummi yaṉṟi etirmaṟiyummi yākātu. atu piṟitoru poruḷit taḻuvāviṭiṉum piṟatoruviṉiyit taḻuvi niṟkumāṟaṟika. cāttaṉ varutaṟku muriyaṉ eṉpatu etirmaṟi yummiyāyiṉ, cāttaṉ varāmikku muriyaṉ eṉpatu uṭaṉpāṭṭummiyātal vēṇṭum. uṭaṉpāṭṭi oḻipāyuṭittāy  niṟṟaliṉ, avvāṟu uṭaṉpāṭṭummi cūttirattiṟ kūṟappaṭāmiyiṉ aḵturiyaṉmi yaṟika. piṉṉi yātōtāṉ etirmaṟiyummi yeṉiṉ; nāḷ tavaṟiṉum nāttavaṟāṉ eṉpuḻi, nāḷ; tavaṟiṉum eṉpatu tavaṟātu eṉṉum oḻipi uṭittāy niṟṟaliṉ atuvē etirmaṟiyummiyeṉka. kaṭal vaṟṟiṉuṅ kaṅki vaṟṟātu. teyvam tīmi ceyyiṉum koṟṟaṉ tīmi ceyyāṉ eṉpaṉavum ivi pōṉṟa piṟavumataṟkutāraṇam.  (centamiḻc celvi cuṟavam 1932)
ilikkāmpu vaki
aṭitāḷ tōki ivaṟṟiṉ kāmpu;
kāmpu iliyiṉ kāmpu;
maṭṭi ōliyiṉ kāmpu
ili narampu
narampu kāmpiṉ toṭarcciyāka iliyiṉ nuṉivari celvatu;  narampu narampiṉ kiḷi  (col: 66)
ilippuri kiḷittal
iliviḻuntu mēvik kiṭakkum ciṟu kuḻikaḷiyellām kiṇṭik kiḷittu oru poruḷit tēṭip pārttalukku ilip puri kiḷittal eṉṟu peyar. ivvaḻakku oruvaṉi ellā iṭamum turuvit tēṭip pārttaliyum kuṟikkum. (col 11, 12.)
ilimaṟikāy
ilikkuḷ maṟintu kiṭantu cilarkaṇṇukkut tōṉṟātu cilar kaṇṇukku maṭṭum tōṉṟum kāy, ilimaṟi kāy, muntiya āciriyarkkut tōṉṟātu pintiṉa āciriyarkkut tōṉṟum moḻiyiyalpukaḷi ilimaṟikāy eṉpatu ilakkaṇa āciriyar vaḻakkam.  (col. 14)
ili vaki
ili - puḷi, vēmpu mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ ili: 
tāḷ - nel, pul mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ ili;
tōki - cōḷam karumpu mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ ili;
ōli - teṉṉi, paṉi mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ ili;  (col : 66.)
iḻipu
iḻipu eṉpatu orunūl vaki. atu, tolkāppiyattil, 
oṟṟoṭu puṇarnta valleḻut taṭaṅkātu
kuṟaḷaṭi mutalā aintaṭi oppittu
ōṅkiya moḻiyāṉ āṅkaḷam oḻukiṉ
iḻipiṉ ilakkaṇam iyinta tākum.  (poruḷ 557)
eṉak kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. iḻipu eṉṉum peyar nūli allatu iḻittalik kuṟikkum iḻi eṉṉum col aṭiyāyp piṟantatākum.  (col.7)

nilamē nīrē tīyē
iyi eṉṉum colliṉ tiripāṉa ēy eṉṉum coṟkup poruntal allatu kūṭutaṟ poruḷuṇmiyāl, ataṉ kaṭikkuṟiyāṉa ēkāra viṭiccol, eṇṇup poruḷil vantateṉa vaṟika.
itukāṟum kūṟiyavaṟṟāl, iḷ eṉṉum vērccolliṉ aṭippaṭip poruḷ poruntutal eṉpatu uṇarappaṭum.
iḷam payirvaki 
nāṟṟu nel kattari mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ iḷanili; 
kaṉṟu mā puḷi vāḻi mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ iḷanili:
karuntu vāḻiyiṉ iḷanili;
piḷḷi teṉṉiyiṉ iḷanili;
kuṭṭi viḷāviṉ iḷanili;
maṭal allatu vaṭali paṉiyiṉ iḷanili:
piṅkūḻ nel cōḷam mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ pacum payir.   (col : 71)
iḷavaṭṭak kācu
tirumaṇattaṉṟu maṇamakkaḷ ūrvalam varuvatum, cila ciṟṟūr kaḷil ayalūr maṇamakaṉ vantu oru peṇṇi maṇantu mīḷumpōtu, avvūr iḷiñar iḷavaṭṭakkācu eṉṉum kinīṭṭam kēṭpatum, paṇṭik kālattil oru peṇṇi nōkkip pala iḷiñar pōṭṭiyiṭṭup porutatik kuṟippāy uṇarttum.  (ta.ti : 5)
iṟakup peyar
tūvu allatu tūvi paṟaviyiṉ uṭampiluḷḷa ciṟu tūvu; iṟaku ciṟakilum vālilumuḷḷa peruntūvu.  (col : 52.)
iṟappu 
makkaḷ piṟappu kaṇviḻippum, avaratu iṟappu kaṇṇaṭippum pōṉṟatu, vāḻkki muḻuvatum oru pakal naṭavaṭikki pōṉṟatē. peṟṟōr tam piḷḷikaḷit tam kaṇṇuḷḷapōtē kariyēṟṟa vēṇṭum eṉṟu colvatu vaḻakkam. pakal muṭintu iravu vanta piṉ kaṇ mūṭit tūṅkuvatu pōl vāḻkki muṭinta pōtum makkaḷ tam kaṇ mūṭi viḻiyāt tuyil koḷvar.
uṟaṅkuvatu pōlum cākkā ṭuṟaṅki
viḻippatu pōlum piṟappu  (kuṟaḷ 239)
tuñciṉār cettāriṉ vēṟallar (kuṟaḷ 926)
eṉṟār tiruvaḷḷuvar  (col. 9)
iṟi 
iṟi allatu iṟivaṉ eṉpatu, aracaṉukkum kaṭavuṭkum potup peyar. kaṭavuḷ eṅkum taṅkiyiruppatu pōla araca ṉuṭiya āṇi, avaṉuṭiya nāṭeṅkum taṅkiyirukkiṟatu eṉpatu karuttu. iṟuttal - taṅkutal. col.24.
iṟivaṉ tāḷ - tamiḻaka maruntukaḷ perumpālum taḻiyum taṇṭumāy iruttaliṉ piṟavippiṇikku maruntākum kuṟippup paṭa iṟivaṉ tiruvaṭikaḷ naṟṟāḷām.  (ti.ma.37)
iṉpam 
nāṭaka naṭippu, aḻakiya iyaṟkik kāṭci, tiruviḻā, ici yaraṅku, coṟpoḻivu, nālvaki aṟucuvi uṇṭi, naṉkāṟṟu, iṉiya illaṟa vāḻvu, taṭpaveppac camanili, nili viḷi yāṭṭu, nīr viḷiyāṭṭu, vēlivāyppu, varippaḷu iṉmi, ceṅkōlāṭci mutaliyaṉa.  (kuṟaḷ 738)
iṉpattuppāliṉ irutiṟam
cila āriya nūlkaḷik kaṟṟa tuṇiyāṉē tammic ciṟanta aṟiñarākak karutik koḷḷum maṭattalivarum pōlittuṟavi yarum, tirukkuṟaḷ iṉpattuppālik koṇṭiruppatāl tuṟaviyar kaṟkat takka nūlaṉṟeṉṟu tuṇintu kūṟuvar.  iṟiva ṉukkum ātaṉukku miṭippaṭṭa aṉpi uvami vāyilāka viḷakkutaṟku, kaṇavaṉ maṉiviyariṭippaṭṭa kātaliṉuñ ciṟantatu vēṟiṉmi yāṉēyē, civaṉaṭiyāruṭ ciṟanta māṇikka vācakar ciṟṟampalak kōviyip pāṭiṉāreṉṟum, ataṉ veḷipporuḷ ulakaneṟiyum uṭporuḷ vīṭṭuneṟiyum paṟṟiṉaveṉṟum, aṟintu koḷka.
āraṇaṅkā ṇeṉpa rantaṇar yōkiyar ākamattiṉ
kāraṇaṅkā ṇeṉpar kāmukar kāmanaṉ ṉūlateṉpar
ēraṇaṅkā ṇeṉpa reṇṇa reḻutteṉpa riṉpulavōr
cīraṇaṅ kākiya ciṟṟam palakkōvi ceppiṭiṉē.  (taṉiyaṉ)
ittakiyatē tirukkuṟa ḷiṉpattup pālumeṉcu.
iṉṉutal
iṉṉutal iṉittal allatu iṉpamātal.
iṉ-iṉpu-iṉpam . iṉ-iṉi. iṉṉā, iṉiya   eṉpataṉ etir maṟiyāṉa palaviṉpāl viṉiyālaṇiyum peyar.  (ti.ma.178)
īkārac cuṭṭu
aṇmi, piṉmi, iḻuttal
1.	aṇmi
muṉmiyik kuṟittaṟku muṉṉōkkik kuvinta vāyitaḻ, muṉmikkup piṟpaṭṭa aṇmiyik kuṟittataṟkup piṉ ṉōkku mukattāl īkārattit tōṟṟuvittatu. atu aṇmic cuṭṭāyiṟṟu.
aṇmic cuṭṭu
peyar : ītu - itu.
	īṉ = ivviṭam, ivvulakam; īṉōr = ivar,
	ivvulakattār.
	il - iḵtu - ittu
	iv - iva - ivi.
	ivaṉ, ivaḷ, ivar.
	ikam = ivvulakam, ivviṭam, ñālam.  (mu.tā)
peyareccam: ī - i.
		īṉ - iṉ - iṉṉa.
		īṉ - īṉa - inta.
viṉiyeccam:	īṅku - iṅku - iṅkā - iṅki.
		īṅku - īṅkaṇ - īṅkaṉ - īṅkaṉam
		īṅkaṉ-iṅkaṉ-iṅṅaṉam
		iṅkaṉ - iṅṅaṉ - iṅṅaṉam
		īṇṭu: 
ivaṇ.
im - impu - impar.
viṉiyeccamum
iṭiccollum :	iṉ - iṉṟu. iṉ - iṉṉē:
		iṉ - iṉṉum = ituvariyum:
		iṉ - iṉi. iṉṉiṉi (aṭukku):
		iṉ - iṉṉā
		ita - itā - itō - itōḷ - 
		itōḷi.  (mu.tā)
		intā - intō.
2. piṉmi
muṉmikkup piṉmiyāṉa aṇmiyik kuṟittataṟku vāyitaḻ piṟpaṭuvatāl, īkārac cuṭṭu aṇmikkaṭuttup piṉmi yuṇarttiṟṟu.
1.	piṉmi (kālamum iṭamum)
	iṉ - iṉi = iṉimēl, piṉpu.
	iṉ - iṉṉum = itaṟku mēlum.
	piṉ - piṉṉam - piṉṉar. piṉ- piṉṉē.
	piṉ - piṉpu. piṉ-piṉṟu- pintu. piṉ-piṟku
	piṉ-piṟpāṭu (piṉ+pāṭu).
	piṉ - piṟa- piṟavu - piṟaku - piṟakku. piṟakkiṭutal= piṉṉukkuc cellutal.
piṟakku - piṟaṅku - piṟaṅkaṭi = piṉṉāl varum urimiyāḷaṉ (vāricu).
piṉ - piṉam - piṟam - piṟampu:  piṟampattaṅkāl - piṉṉaṅkāl.
piṟam - piṭam - piṭar - piṭari = taliyiṉ piṉpuṟam.
piṭam - piṭṭam = piṉpuṟam, piṉpuṟattiṉ kaṭi allatu aṭippakuti (kuṇṭi).
piṭṭam - puṭṭam, piṭṭam - piṭṭi-puṭṭi= paṟaviyiṉ piṉpuṟam. 
piṟam - puṟam - puṟaṉ. puṟakkaṭi = vīṭṭiṉ piṉ pakkam.
puṟaṅkūṟutal = piṉṉāṟ paḻittal, puṟaṉ - (puraṉi) - puṟaṇi = puṟaṅkūṟṟu.
puṟaṉē = piṉpu, pinti : puṟaṉaṇṭi = piṉpuṟam. piṟaku - puṟaku = piṉpu.
II  maṟunili
iṉ - iṉṉum = tirumpavum, mēlum, vēṟum:
iṉṉoṉṟu = vēṟoṉṟu. iṉṉoru = vēṟoru, maṟṟa.
iṉ - ēṉ. ēṉōr = maṟṟōr. ēṉ- ēṉi= maṟṟa.
piṉ = tirumpa, vēṟu. piṉṉum = tirumpavum.
piṉṉum piṉṉum = tirumpat tirumpa.
piṉṉoṉṟu = vēṟoṉṟu. piṉṉē yār? = vēṟu yār?
piṉa - piṟa- piṟitu. piṟa- piṟaku= vēṟu. piṟaku- piṟakku = vēṟāka.
piṟam - piṟaṉ + vēṟoruvaṉ, maṟṟavaṉ, ayalāṉ, 
piṟam - puṟam - puṟampu = maṟṟi.
III  mutuku 
mutuku piṉpuṟamiruppatāl piṉmip peyar mutukik kuṟittaṟkāyiṟṟu.
piṉ- piṟku. piṟam- piṟavu-piṟaku= mutuku. =piṟaku - piṟakku = mutuku.
piṟaku - puṟaku. piṟakku - puṟakku. puṟakkiṭutal= puṟaṅkāṭṭutal.
piṟam - puṟam - puṟaṉ, puṟam =mutuku. puṟam- puṟampu = mutuku.
piṟam- piṟaṉ- verinta- ven-veṉ=mutuku.
piṟam- piṭam-piṭṭam- mutuku.
IV  mēṟpuṟam
vilaṅkukaṭkum paṟavikaṭkum kuppuṟappaṭuttirukkum māntaṉuk kum mutuku mēṟpuṟamāyiruppatāl, mutukiṉ peyar mēṟ puṟattik kuṟittatu.
puṟam= mēṟpakkam, pakkam.
puṟam- puṟaṉ-(puṟaṉi)-puṟaṇi= mēṟpuṟam.
V  veḷippuṟam
maṭikkappaṭum allatu karuṭṭappaṭum poruḷkaṭku muṉ pakkam uṭpuṟamum piṉpakkam veḷippuṟamum pōṉṟirutta lālum, uyiraṟṟa kaṉapporuḷkaṭku mēṟpuṟam muḻutum veḷippuṟamāyiruntalālum, mēṟpuṟattik kuṟikkum puṟam eṉṉum col veḷippuṟattiyuṅ kuṟittatu:
puṟam= veḷi, veḷippakkam, puṟap poruḷ.
puṟam- puṟavu- puṟaku= veḷippuṟam, puṟampāṉavaṉ. puṟaku- puṟakku.
puṟam- puṟampu= veḷippuṟam.
puṟampu- puṟampar. puṟappaṭu- puṟappāṭu, puṟappaṭutal= veḷi varutal. 
uḷḷum puṟampum  akamum puṟamum eṉṉun toṭarkaḷāl, puṟam eṉṉum col uṭpakkattiṟku etirāṉa veḷip pakkattik kuṟippatu teḷivām. mūṭiṉa kiyiṉ uṭpuṟamā yiruppatu uḷḷaṅki allatu akaṅki eṉṟum, ataṉ veḷippuṟamāyiruppatu puṟaṅki eṉṟum kūṟappaṭutal kāṇka.
puṟam = maruta nilattiṟkup puṟampāṉa mulli, mulliyaṭutta kuṟiñci.
puṟam- puṟavu= mulli, kuṟiñci. puṟam- puṟampu- puṟampaṇi= mulli, kuṟiñci.
puṟam- puṟa= mulli niṟap paṟavi vaki, puṟa-puṟā- puṟavu- puṟavam.
piṭṭam- pīṭṭaṉ = puṟampāṉavaṉ. 
VI  piṉṉiṭal 
iḷ- iḷittal= vāyitaḻip piṉṉukkiḻuttal. vāyit tiṟantu pallik kāṭṭal.
iḷ-iri, irital= piṉṉukkōṭutal, tōṟṟōṭutal, ōṭutal,
iḷ-iṭa, iṭattal= ili pū mutaliyavaṟṟip piṉṉukkiḻut toṭittal.
iḷ-(iṇ)- iṇuṅku, iṇuṅkutal= tirukip piṉṉuk kiḻuttal, piṉṉukkiḻut toṭittal.
iṭa-iṭakku= piṟcelavu. kutiri iṭakkup paṇṇukiṟatu eṉṉum vaḻakkik kāṇka.
iṭa-iṭi. iṭital= piṉvāṅkutal.
iṭi- iṭiñcal = piṉvāṅkac ceyyum taṭi.
iṭa-iṭaṟu. iṭaṟutal= piṉṉukkut taḷḷutal.
iḻuttal
iyalpāka iḻuttaleṉpatu, muṉṉāluḷḷa poruḷip piṉṉukku valintu koṇartalātaliṉ, piṉmik karuttil iḻuttaṟ karuttut tōṉṟiṟṟu.

II  iḻuppunōy

icittal= narampiḻuttal.  (mu.tā.)
īkārac cuṭṭuk karuttu vaḷarcci
īkārac cuṭṭu, mutalāvatu aṇmiyik kuṟikkum. aṇmi muṉmikkup piṉmiyātalālum, īkārattiyolikkum pōtu vāy piṉṉukkiḻuppatālum, aṇmik kaṭutta īkārac cuṭṭuk karuttu piṉmiyākum.
piṉmik karuttil tōṉṟuvatu piṟpaṭaṟ karuttu.
māntaṉ niṉṟa niliyil iyaṟkiyāy oṉṟi iḻuppatu muṉṉiṉṟu piṉṉātaliṉ, piṟpaṭaṟ karuttiṉiṉṟu piṟpaṭuttaṟ karuttākiya iḻuttaṟ karuttup piṟakkum.
mēṉōkkic ceṉṟa poruḷ piṟpaṭutal iṟaṅkalātaliṉ, piṟpaṭaṟ karuttiṉiṉṟu iṟaṅkal allatu kīḻuṟaṟ karuttut tōṉṟum.
īkārac cuṭṭāluṇarttappeṟum peruṅ karuttukkaḷ, aṇmi piṉmi kīḻuṟal eṉṉum mūṉṟē. mutukiṟkup piṉ nikaḻvatu oruvarukkun teriyāmiyāṉum, muṉṉōkkik koṇṭu piṉṉukkuc celvatu māntaṉukkum maṟṟavuyirikaṭkum. iyaṟki yaṉmiyāṉum, piṉmik karuttil vēṟoṉṟun tōṉṟaṟ killi. mēṉiṉṟiṟaṅkum oru poruḷ nilattil allatu nīril viḻuntu ataṟkuḷ pukiṉum kīḻuṟal allatu iṟaṅkutalākiya aḷavē īkārac cuṭṭuk karuttellikkuṭ paṭṭatām. oru poruḷukkuṭ pukuvatellām tuḷittaliṉ pāṟpaṭṭatāka liṉ. atu ūkārac cuṭṭiṉ āṭcikkuṭpaṭṭateṉa aṟika.

īḻavar

acittal vilaṅku pōl aciyiṭṭut tiṉṉutal, atukkutal cūṭāṉa uṇavi vāyiṉ irupuṟattilum māṟi māṟi otukkutal; arittal pūcci puḻuppōlac ciṟitu ciṟitāyk kaṭittal; aruntu tal ciṟitu ciṟitāyt tiṉṉutal allatu kuṭittal; ārtal vayiṟu nirampavuṇṇutal; uṇṇutal etiyum uṭkoḷḷutal; utapputal (kutapputal) vāyiliruntu veḷivarumpaṭi mikutiyāyc cavittal; uṟiñcutal oṉṟiluḷḷa nīri vāyāl uḷḷiḻuttal; otukkutal oru kaṉṉattil aṭakkutal; kaṭittal kaṭiṉamāṉa tip pallāl uṭittal; karumputal oru poruḷiṉ ōrattil ciṟitu ciṟitāyk kaṭittal; kaṟittal mellak kaṭittal; kuṭittal kalattiluḷḷa nīrip potuvakiyil vāyiliṭṭu uṭkoḷḷu tal kutaṭṭutal kālnaṭipōl aciyiṭṭu vāykku veḷiyē taḷḷutal; koṟittal ovvoru kūlamaṇiyāyp palliṭi vittu umiyip pōkkutal; capputal cavittu oṉṟaṉ cāṟṟi uṭkoḷḷutal;
cavittal veṟṟili, pukiyili mutaliyavaṟṟi mellutal; cāppiṭutal cōṟuṇṇutal; cuvittal oṉṟaṉ cuviyi nukartal; cūpputal kaṭiṉamāṉatic capputal; tiṉṉutal meṉṟu uṭkoḷḷutal; nakkutal nāviṉāl toṭutal; parukutal kiyiṉāl āvalōṭu aḷḷik kuṭittal; māntutal orē viṭukkil allatu perumaṭakkāyk kuṭittal; mukkutal allatu mokkutal vāy niṟiya oṉṟiyiṭṭut tiṉṉutal; mellutal pallāl arittal; mēytal mēlākap pullit tiṉṉutal; viḻuṅkutal mellāmalum palliṟ paṭāmalum virintu uṭ koḷḷutal; micittal miccil uṇṇutal. uṭampu
uyirillāta poruḷkaḷ ettaṉiyō vēṟupaṭṭa vaṇṇam vaṭivu aḷavu cuvi mutaliya taṉmikaḷiyuṭiyavaṉāy iruntālum avaṟṟi ellām uṭampoṭu toṭarpu paṭutti oṉṟākak koṇṭu avaṟṟukku uṭampu allatu mey eṉa orē peyariṭṭataṟkuk kāraṇam aṇṭatti ottatu piṇṭam eṉṟum, uyiraṟṟa poruḷaḷittum aimpūtac cērkki allatu vēṟupāṭu eṉṟum muṉṉit tamiḻar aṟintiruntamiyē.  (col.31.)
uṭal
uṭal, uṭampu, kuṭampi, kūṭu, mey, yākki, muṭi, kaṭṭi eṉa uṭampiṟku vaḻaṅkum peyarkaḷiṉ kāraṇatti nōkkiṉ appeyarkaḷi iṭṭa makkaḷiṉ uyariya aṟivu pulaṉām.
uṭal -	uyiruṭaṉ cērntē iyaṅkuvatu allatu uyiruṭaṉ iruppatu, uṭal allatu uṭampu.
kūṭu -	kuñcu poritta piṉ toṭarpu nīṅkum muṭṭik kūṭu pōla uyiriviṭṭu nīṅkuvatu kuṭampi allatu kūṭu. (kuṟaḷ. 333)
mey - 	uyiri mēlākap potintippatu mey.
yākki - tōl narampu elumpu taci arattam mutaliya eḻuvakit tātukkaḷāl yākkappeṟṟiruppatu allatu  muṭiyappaṭṭiruppatu yākki allatu muṭi.
kaṭṭi -	uyirnīṅkiya piṉ kaṭṭi pōlakkiṭantu erivatu allatu maṇṇōṭu maṇṇāyp pōvatu kaṭṭi  (col. 31)
uṭal nalap poruttam
maṇamakkaḷ iṉpan tuyttaṟkum illaṟam naṭattaṟkum iṉṟiyamiyātatu uṭal nalam. cila nōykaḷ maṟivāyirukkum, allatu maṟikkappaṭṭirukkum. ātalāl, takka maruttuvap paṇṭitarik koṇṭu maṇamakkaḷi nōṭṭañ ceytal vēṇṭum. allākkāl oruvarāl iṉṉoruvar nōyppaṭuva tuṭaṉ, avarkkup piṟakkum piḷḷikaḷum vaḻivaḻi nōyp paṭṭu varunta vēṇṭiyirukkum.  (ta.ti. 43,44.)
uṭal valiyāl tīmi
oruvarkku uṭal pōṉṟatē uḷḷamum valimiyaṭital vēṇṭum. uḷḷa valimikku aṭippaṭi aṟivē. itaṉāl, aṟivi uraṉ eṉṟār tiruvaḷḷuvar. uraṉ-valimi.

 varaṉ eṉṉum vippiṟkōṟ vittu   (kuṟaḷ-24)
eṉpatu kuṟaḷ.
aṟivē āṟṟal (Knowledge is power) eṉpar āṅkilarum.
uḷavalimiyōṭu kūṭiya uṭalvalimi kūṭat tīmi viḷikkum eṉpatu, tuṟaviyar karuttu. itaṉālēyē avar nōṉpālum tavattālum tam uṭali oṭukkuvar. uḷavalimiyōṭu kūṭāta uṭal valimiyō. piṟarkkup peruntīṅku viḷippatu tiṇṇam.
uṭal paruttavaṉik kuṇṭaṉ eṉṟum taṭiyaṉ eṉṟum kūṟuvatu ulaka vaḻakku. ippeyarkaḷ tīyavaṉ allatu koṭiya vaṉ eṉṉum poruḷip peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. tēvāra āciriyarum tivviyappaṉuval āciriyaruṅkūṭac camaṇarik kuṇṭar eṉṟu kūṟuvar.
uṭal taṭittavaṉ kuṟumpu allatu tīṅku ceyvāṉ eṉpatu, mēṟkūṟiya vaṟṟāl viḷaṅkum.  (col: 111)

uṇmiyiṉ iyalpu
uṇmi eṉpatu colli maṭṭum paṟṟiya aṟamaṉṟu. uḷḷum puṟamum ottiruppatē uṇmi. uḷḷam vāy mey ākiya mukkaraṇaṅkaḷum, oruṅkē uṇmiyil ottiruttal vēṇṭum. ivviyalpiyaṟintē, uṇmi, vāymi, meymmi eṉa uṇmikku muppeyariṭṭaṉar muṉṉōr. uḷḷattip paṟṟiyatu uṇmi. vāyip paṟṟiyatu vāymi; meymip paṟṟiyatu meymmi. (meymmi eṉṉuñ colli mey eṉṉum uṭampiṉ peyaraṭip piṟantatākak koḷḷutalāl poyyāṉa vuṭampi meyyeṉpatu maṅkala vaḻakku eṉa, ilakkaṇa uriyāciriyar kūṟuvatu poruntātu.)
uṇmi colli maṭṭum paṟṟiyateṉṉum tappukkoḷki yālēyē, pollātavar poyyāṇiyiṭuvatum piṟar ati nampi oppukkoḷvatum, nērkiṉṟaṉa.
uḷattoṭu poruntātu colloṭum meyyoṭum maṭṭum poruntuvatu orupōtum uṇmiyākātu. uḷattoṭu poruntiya uṇmiyurippavar tiruntiya voḻukka muṭiya varāyiruttal tiṇṇam eṉṉum karuttē,

uṇavu
uṇpaṉa tiṉpaṉa parukuvaṉa nakkuvaṉa eṉa uṇavu nālvaki.
cōṟum kaḷiyumākac camikkappeṟum nel, pul (kampu) mutaliyaṉa uṇpaṉa.
kāykaṟikaḷ tiṉpaṉa.
pālum pataṉīrum (teḷivum) parukuvaṉa.
tēṉum nekiḻnilip payiṉum nakkuvaṉa.  (ti.ma.43)
uttaram, takkaṇam emmoḻic coṟkaḷ?
tamiḻaṉ piṟantakamākiya kumari nāṭṭil. mutaṟkaṇ tōṉṟiya nāṟṟicip peyarkaḷ, teṉ, vaṭam, kīḻ mēl eṉpaṉa. avi 4ām vēṟṟumikkuriya kuv vurupēṟṟut teṟku, vaṭakku, kiḻakku (kīḻkku), mēṟku eṉṟāki. piṉṉar āṭcipaṟṟi mutal vēṟṟumiyākavē vaḻakkūṉṟi viṭṭaṉa.
avaṟṟuḷ, muṉṉiraṇṭum nilittiṇic ciṟappum, piṉ ṉiraṇṭum nila maṭṭamum paṟṟiyaṉa.
teṉ eṉpatu teṉṉi. teṉṉutal = kōṇutal. perum pālun teṉṉik koṇṭiruppatāl teṉṉi appeyar peṟṟatu. teṉ - teṉṉi, teṉ - teṉku - teṟku.
teṉṉi iyaṟkiyākat tōṉṟiyatu kumarikkaṇṭattil. ikkālattil teṉṉiyiṉ pala vakikaḷ intō maliyāviṉ iyaṟkiyāka vaḷarvatāl an nilappakutiyē teṉṉiyiṉ piṟantakamāyirukkalāmeṉṟu karutappaṭukiṉṟatu. āyiṉ ap pakutiyum kumarik kaṇṭattic cērntatēyeṉpati aṟital vēṇṭum. paḵṟuḷi yāṟṟiṟkum kumariyāṟṟiṟkum iṭippaṭṭa eḻunūṟṟuk kāvata nilapparappiliruntu palvēṟu nāṭukaḷik kuṟikkumiṭattu, aṭiyārkku nallār ēḻteṅka nāṭṭiyē muṟpaṭak kuṟittiruttalāl. an nāṭukaḷ kumarikkaṇṭattiṉ teṉkōṭiyaṭuttiruntami peṟappaṭum. teṉkōṭiyil teṉṉi ciṟappāka vaḷarntataṉālēyē, teṉṟici appeyar peṟṟiruttal vēṇṭum.
iṅṅaṉamē nāvalan tēyattiṉ vaṭakōṭiyilum vaṭamaram (ālamaram) ciṟantu vaḷarntataṉāl. vaṭatici appeyar peṟṟatu. vaṭanāvalap pakutiyāṉa vaṅkattil atu ciṟappāka vaḷarvatālēyē. mēlip payirnūlārum ataṟku vaṅkāḷa atti (Ficushem-galensis) eṉa ilattīṉap peyariṭṭiruk kiṉṟaṉar.
ālamaram cūḻa viḻutuviṭṭu vaṭṭamāka akaṉṟu vaḷarvatāl. vaṭam eṉap peyarpeṟṟatu. val-vaḷ-vaṭṭu-vaṭṭam-vaṭam.
o.nō : paṭṭam-paṭam = tuṇi māṉiyar villiyamcu camaṟkiruta - āṅkila akaramutaliyum, vaṭa (vata) eṉṉum ālamarap peyarukku vaṭṭam eṉpatiyē poruṭ karaṇiyamākak kūṟukiṉṟatu.
vaṭṭam - va vaṭa (vaṭṭa)
vaṭamoḻiyeṉṉum camaṟkirutattil, vaṭṭam eṉpatu vrutta eṉṟē yirukkumeṉpatu kavaṉikkattakkatu.
vaṭṭam (tamiḻ) - vaṭṭa (pirākirutam) - vrut (camaṟkirutam)
nila maṭṭam iṟṟit tamiḻ nāṭṭiṟ pōṉṟē kumarik kaṇṭat tilum, mēṟkil uyarntum kiḻakkil tāḻntum iruntataṉāl, avviru ticikaḷum muṟiyē mēl kīḻ eṉappeyar peṟṟaṉa.
ulakil uyarnta paṉimali. oru kālattil kaṭaṟkariyāy iruntatu. aṉṟu vaṭatici kaṭalāl tāḻntum, teṉtici kumarimaliyāl uyarntum, iruntaṉa. piṉṉar paṉimali yeḻuntapiṉ iruticiyum camamāyiṉa. ataṉpiṉ kumarimali mūḻkiviṭavē, vaṭaticiyuyarntum teṉṟici tāḻntum pōyiṉa. ātalāl, vaṭaticikku uttaram eṉṉum peyarum teṉṟicikkut takkaṇam eṉṉum peyarum tōṉṟiṉa. avaṟṟoṭu ēṉiyiru ticikkum, kīḻ mēl eṉṉum paḻiya karaṇiyam paṟṟiyē kuṇam kuṭam eṉṉum iru peyarkaḷum uṭaṉtōṉṟiṉa.
kuḷ-kuḻi. kuḷ-kuṇṭu = paḷḷam. kuḷ-kuṇ-kuṇam = paḷḷam. kuṇṭuṅ kuḻiyumāyirukkiṟatu eṉṉum vaḻakki nōkkuka.
kuṭu-kuṭumi = ucci, māṭavucci, taliyucci, uccikkoṇṭi, uccimuṭi, makuṭam, maliyucci, koṭumuṭi. kuṭu-kuṭam = uyarcci, mēṭu, kuṭanāṭu = uyarnta malināṭāṉa cēranāṭu, mēlnāṭu.
kiḻakku mēṟku eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷiyottu. kuṇam kuṭam eṉpaṉavum kuṇakku kuṭakku eṉak kuvvurupēṟṟaṉa.
tamiḻ kumarināṭṭil tōṉṟiya ulaka mutaṉmoḻiyātalāl, cēymi aṇmi muṉmi ākiya mūviṭattiyuñ cuṭṭum ā (a) ī (i) ū (u) eṉṉum muccuṭṭum mutaṉmutal māntaṉ vāyil tōṉṟiya cuṭṭolikaḷāka maṭṭumiṉṟi uyiroli kaḷākavum irukkiṉṟaṉa. itaṉālēyē, avi tamiḻ neṭuṅ kaṇakkil mutalil vikkappaṭṭiruppatuṭaṉ, iṉṟum āriyamoḻikaḷiṟpōl maṟiyātum tiriyātum iyalpākavē tamiḻil vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟaṉa. cuṭṭuvakiyilum ataṉ vaḻippaṭṭa mūviṭap peyar vakiyilum, tamiḻ (tiraviṭam) āriyattiṟku (camaṟkiruttiṟku) muntiya teṉṟum, ulaka mutaṉ moḻiyōṭu neruṅkiya toṭarpuṭiya teṉṟum, kālṭuvelār tam oppiyalilakkaṇattil āṅkāṅku mikaveṭutturittuc cellutal kāṇka.
muṉṉilip peyar: ūṉ-uṉ (orumi) ūm-um (paṉmi)
uvaṉ, uvaḷ, uvar, utu, uvi, uṅku eṉpaṉa muṉṉilip poruḷkaḷiyum iṭattiyumē kuṟittalāl, ukara allatu ūkāraccuṭṭu mutaṉmic cuṭṭē. muṉmi cēymikkum aṇ mikkum iṭippaṭṭa tātalāl, iṭimic cuṭṭeṉpatu orumaruṅku poruntum. āyiṉ, cēymikku varampilli yātalāl, muṉmi iṭimiyākalām. iṭimi eṉṟum muṉmiyākātu.
muṉmi aṇmikkuc caṟṟuc cēymiyātalāl muṉmic cuṭṭu, tamiḻiṟ ciṟupāṉmi ciṟu cēymiyiyum vaṭanāṭṭu moḻikaḷiṟ cēymiyiyum uṇarttum.
e-ṭu:	uṅku = nī (nīṅkaḷ) irukkumiṭattu taṉayarik kaṇṭirō vuṅkaṇeṉṉa (cētupu 6:39)
utōḷ - utar (inti) = aṅku.
muṉmik karuttiṉiṉṟu muṟcelavuk karuttut tōṉṟum.
e-ṭu:	uy = muṉcel. muṟceluttu, untu = muṉtaḷḷu uki = muṟceluttu.
nilamaṭṭattiṟkum nīr maṭṭattiṟkum mēṟpaṭṭa muṟcelavu pakkavāṭṭilum mēṉōkkiyumirukkumātalāl muṉmiccuṭṭu uyarccik karuttiyun taḻuvum.
e-ṭu:	ukkam = kaṭṭit tūkkuṅ kayiṟu. ukappu = uyarvu ukaḷtal = kutittal ukaḷittal = kutittal. ukittal = eḻutal. uccam = uyarnili ucci = uyariṭam.
uttaram = 1)	mukaṭṭuk kuṟukkup perumaram  allatu iruppuviṭṭam.
2)	mēṉmaṭaṅkal = kūṟṟuvaṉ, kūṟṟuvaṉ pōl aḻikkun tī uttaramantiri = talimi yamiccar.
upputal = parutteḻutal
umpal = uyarnta yāṉi
umpar = mēl, mēlulakam, tēvar
umpaṉ = uyarntōṉ
uyartal = mēleḻutal, mēṟpaṭutal
uvaṇ = mēliṭam, uvaṇam = uyarap paṟakkum kaluḻaṉ
uvaṉi = tēvarulakam.
uṟi = uyarak kaṭṭit tūkkuṅ kayiṟu allatu toṭari.
uṉṉutal = uyarak kutittal
ūrtal = ēṟic cellutal
ūṅku = uyarvu, mēṉmi.
uttaram = 1. paṉimaliyāl uyarnta ticiyāṉa vaṭakku (tivā)
Uttara, mfn cp.fr.1.ud; opposed to adbara. upper, higher, superior (e.g. uttare dantas, the upper teeth), RV, AV, TS. CHUP; Ragh, and c, northern (becuse the northern part of India is high), AV; Mn; Susr.; pancat & c. eṉṟu māṉiyar villiyamcu camaṟkiruta - āṅkila akaramutali kūṟutal kāṇka.
2. vaṭakkil tōṉṟum ūḻittī (piṅ).
uttara maṭaṅkal = vaṭavaṉal eṉṉum ūḻittī (ti.vā.)
kumarināṭṭuk  kālattil tamiḻar (neytalnilap paravar) cuṟṟuk kaṭalōṭikaḷā yiruntataṉāl, vaṭamuṉiyil avvappōtu tōṉṟum oru palvaṇṇa voḷiyik kaṇṭu, ataṟku vaṭavi allatu vaṭavaṉal eṉṟu peyariṭṭaṉar. atiyē ūḻi yiṟutiyil ulakiyaḻikkum tīyākavum karutiṉar.
vaṭavā vaṉalu maṭanaṭip piṭiyum
arumip peyaruṅ kutiriyum vaṭali   (piṅ.986)
vaṭavik kaṉalip piḻinteṭuttu   (antakak.taṉip.)
veḷḷat tiṭivāḻ vaṭavaṉali   (kamparā. tilamā.86)
kaṭukiya vaṭavaṉalat tīṉivittatu 
aṉal-aṉalam  (kaliṅ.402)
akkaṭaliṉ mītuvaṭa vaṉalniṟka villiyō  (tāyu.paripūra.9)
mēliyar vaṭavaṉali, Aurora (down) Borealis (northern) eṉṟu ilattīṉilum northern light eṉṟu āṅkilattilum, vaḻaṅkuvatu muṟṟum poruḷottiruttal kāṇka.
vaṭamoḻiyāḷar vaṭavi eṉṉum teṉcolli, vaṭavā, vaṭavā mukam, vaṭavāmukākkiṉi, paṭapā, paṭapāmukam, paṭapā mukākkaṉi eṉṟu tirittum virittum, peṭṭikkutiri mukattil tōṉṟiya neruppeṉṟu, poruntāp poytta lākap poruḷkūṟuvar. atit tamiḻar nampiya piṉṉarē. vaṭavik kaṉaṉmāvum (takkayākap.694) eṉṟu oṭṭak kūttarum pāṭanērntatu. itaṉāl, tamiḻccol tirikkappaṭṭa toṭu tamiḻar matiyum tirikkappaṭṭami kāṇka.
j¡F = jhœî bj., ka.takku (taggu) avarkkut takkut toṇṭi avar takkilē pāṭukiṟār. eṉṉum vaḻakkukaḷi nōkkuka.
takku - takkaṇam = nilamaṭṭattil tāḻnta teṉṟici. teṉṉāṭu (deccan) 
takkaṇam - va.takṣiṇam
vaṭamoḻiyāḷar, takṣiṇam eṉṉum collit teṟku eṉṉum poruḷil, takṣiṇākkiṉi, takṣiṇāyaṉam, takṣiṇā calam (potiyam), takṣiṇa pāñcālam, takṣiṇāmūrtti eṉa āḷuvar āyiṉ, takṣiṇam eṉṉum colliṟkut takṣ eṉpati mūlamākakkoṇṭu ataṟkut tiṟamiyāyiruttal allatu valamiyōṭiruttal eṉṟu poruḷkūṟi, kiḻakku nōkkum pōtu teṉṟici valappuṟamum vaṭatici iṭappuṟamum iruppatāl takṣiṇam eṉṉum coṟku valam eṉṟum uttaram eṉṉum coṟku iṭam eṉṟum poruḷ tōṉṟiṉaveṉṟu karaṇiyaṅ kāṭṭuvar.
takṣiṇam pari = valam vā. valañcuṟṟu.
pratakṣiṇa = valam varuki, valañ cuṟṟuki.
takutiyeṉṉuñ coṟku vallamiyeṉṟum poruḷiruppatāl takṣ eṉṉum vaṭacol taku eṉṉun teṉcolliṉ tiripākavumirukkalām.
o.nō. : L.dexter = of or on the right-hand side.
E. dexterous = deft, adroit, clever, using right hand by preference
E, ME. deft = dexterous, skilful.
E, daft. ME.daffte = OE ged & fte (Goth gadaban to be fit) orig. Sense, fitting, suitable.
takṣ eṉṉum vaṭacoṟkum. (dexter) eṉṉum ilattīṉac coṟkum. mūlam etuvāyiṉum, uttaram, takkaṇam eṉṉum iru ticiccollum teṉcollēyeṉpataṟkuc cāṉṟu kaḷāvaṉa:-
1. uttaram, takkaṇam eṉpaṉavaṟṟōṭu cērntu vaḻaṅkappeṟum kuṇam kuṭam eṉṉum ticippeyarkaḷ tūya teṉcol. avi vaṭamoḻiyil illi. avaṟṟōṭu cērnta ēṉi yiraṇṭum teṉcollākavēyiruttal vēṇṭum.
2. uyarntōṉ tāḻntōṉ eṉpaṉa pōla, uttaram takkaṇam eṉṉum iraṇṭum oṉṟiṟkoṉṟu etirāṉa uṟaviyaṟ kuṟiyīṭukaḷ (relative terms.)
camaṟkirutattil. uttaram eṉpataṟku nilavuyarcciyiyum takṣiṇam eṉpataṟku valappuṟattiyum, karaṇiyamākak kāṭṭuvatu poruntātu.
3. uttaram eṉṉum colliṟku aṭiyāṉa ukarac cuṭṭum. takkaṇam eṉṉum colliṟku mūlamāṉa takku eṉṉum mutaṉiliyum tūya tamiḻ.
4. kumarināṭṭil tōṉṟiya tamiḻ camaṟkirutattiṟku muntiyatu.
5. uttiram takkaṇam eṉṉum nāṭṭuppeyarkaḷ allatu ticippeyarkaḷ, tamiḻarum avar vaḻiyiṉarāṉa tiraviṭarum toṉṟutoṭṭu vāḻum nāvalan tēyattilaṉṟi, vēṟev vāriya nāṭṭilum eṉṟēṉum vaḻaṅkiyatilli.
kaṭavuḷ vaḻipāṭiṉṟik katiravaṉ vaṇakkattiyē ciṟappākak koṇṭa vētāriyar. intiyāviṟkuṭ pukuntapiṉṉarē, kālik katirava vaṇakkattiṉ pōtu takkaṇam valappuṟamum uttaram iṭappuṟamum iruttal kaṇṭu. avviruticip peyar kaṭkum muṟiyē valam iṭam eṉap poruḷ koṇṭiruttal vēṇṭum.
kiḻakku mutaliya muṉṟōṉṟiya nāṟṟicip peyarum ulaka vaḻakkil āḻa ūṉṟiviṭṭataṉāl, kuṇakku (kuṇam) mutaliya piṟṟōṉṟiya nāṟṟicip peyarum ceyyuḷ vaḻakkeṉṉum ilakkiya vaḻakkilēyē iṭampeṟalāyiṉa.
ulaka vaḻakkiliṉmiyālum poyyuṭi vaṭavar col vaṉmiyiṉālumē, uttaram takkaṇam eṉṉum iru teṉ coṟkaḷum vaṭacolleṉum mayakkattiṟku iṭamāyiṉaveṉa, ikkaṭṭuriyiṉiṉṟum teṟṟeṉat terintu koḷka.
mutaltaram, kaṭittaram, mēltaram, kīḻttaram, naṭuttaram, iṭittaram, ciṟutaram, peruntaram, eṉpaṉa pōṉṟatē uttaram (uyarnta nili) eṉṉum vaḻakkum eṉka. vaṭamoḻiyāḷar ivvaṭivi uṟaḻtaram (comparative degree) ākkik koṇṭaṉar. ataṉāl ut+tara (ud+tara) eṉṟu pakuppar. atukaṇṭu mayaṅkaṟka.
muraṭṭuk kuṟukkup perumaram uttaram eṉṟu peyarpeṟ ṟiruttaliyum nōkkuka.  - maturi eḻuttāḷar maṉṟam 16ām āṇṭuviḻāc ciṟappumalar 1974.

neṭuñcēṇ veḷināṭṭārāṉa kālṭu velār tolkāppiya mum kaṭik kaḻakap paṉuvalkaḷum tamiḻp perum pulavarkkut teriyātu maṟintum, tamiḻaṉ piṟantakam kumari nāṭeṉṉuṅ karuttu oruvaruḷḷattilum tōṉṟa vaḻiyillātum, irunta kālattil, maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ pōlum vaḻikāṭṭiyār oruvaru miṉṟit tāmē tamiyarāyt tamiḻik kaṟṟāyntārēṉum, naṭu niliyāka āyntataṉāl, tamiḻiṉ ulaka mutaṉmiyik kaṇṭu, tiraviṭac cuṭṭukaḷ camaṟkirutattiṉiṉṟu kaṭaṉkoḷḷappaṭṭa viyalla. ataṟku māṟāka, avi, camaṟkirutac cuṭṭukaṭkum ata ṉoṭu cērnta vēṟupala intiya-airōppiyak kuṭumpa moḻic cuṭṭukaṭkum mūlamāṉa, yāppēttiyac (Japhetic) cuṭṭaṭikaḷi ottuḷḷaṉa. (tiraviṭa moḻikaḷiṉ oppilakkaṇam, ceṉṉip patippu, pak.422) eṉṟu tiṇṇamuṟak kūṟiyuḷḷār.
camaṟkirutattiṉ taṉiyuṭimiyallāta nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉa aṭippaṭic coṟkaḷ, ataṟkum tamiḻukkum potuvāka vuḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟuṭ perumpālāṉa tamiḻ eṉpatu, aṟiviyaṟ colliyalāl iṉṟu veṭṭa veḷiyākiṉṟatu. vaṭamoḻi yāḷar kuṟikkōṭ colliyal (Tendentious Etymology) koṇṭē, avaṟṟiyellām vaṭacol leṉṟē ituvari ēmāṟṟi vantirukkiṉṟaṉar.
āriya moḻic cuṭṭukaṭkellām tamiḻc cuṭṭukaḷē aṭip paṭiyātalāl, vēṟu poruṭ coṟkaḷi vaṭamoḻiyāḷar uṭaṉaṭiyāy oppukkoḷḷāviṭiṉum, cuṭṭupporuṭ coṟkaḷit tamiḻc colleṉṟu oppuk koḷḷāmalirukka muṭiyātu.
tamiḻc cuṭṭukaḷ, mūvēṟiṭattit tappātu cuṭṭum mūvēṟu taṉiyeḻuttolikaḷ allatu ōreḻuttuc coṟkaḷ; āriyac cuṭṭukaḷō perumpālum iṭamāṟik kuṟippaṉavum, īriṭat tiṟkup potuvāṉavum, aṭi tirintaṉavumāṉa palaveḻuttuc coṟkaḷ. iḵtoṉṟē tamiḻccuṭṭiṉ muṉmi kāṭṭac cālum.
āriyamoḻikaḷiṟ ceṉṟu vaḻaṅkum tamiḻc cuṭṭuc coṟkaḷ palaviruppiṉum, iṅku umpar eṉṉuñ collē eṭuttuk koḷḷappaṭṭatu.
eṇpeyar muṟipiṟap puruvamāttiri mutalī ṟiṭinili pōli yeṉṉum pattu akattilakkaṇattiṟkum, kiḷavi puṇarcci yeṉṉum iru puṟattilakkaṇattiṟkum, iṭamāṉatum; uyirmey uyirmey eṉṉum pākupāṭum uyir meyt taṉivaṭivum uṭiyatum, mutaṉmutal māntaṉ vāyil tōṉṟiya ā ī ū (a i u) eṉṉum muccuṭṭukaḷiyē mutaṟkaṇ koṇṭatumāṉa neṭuṅkaṇakku, ulakil mutaṉ mutal tōṉṟiyatu tamiḻilēyē tamiḻneṭuṅ kaṇakkiṉ vaḷarcciyē vaṭamoḻi vaṇṇamāli. itip piṟaḻa vuṇarntār kālṭuvelār.
ā ī ū eṉṉum mūṉṟum, muṟiyē, cēymic cuṭṭum aṇmiccuṭṭum ivviraṇṭiṟkum iṭippaṭṭa muṉmic cuṭṭum ākum. ivaṟṟuḷ ētēṉum oṉṟi olikkum niliyil, ēṉiyiraṇṭiyum olikka viyalātu. ātalāl, ivi viralpōṟ cuṭṭikaḷ (Pointers) āyiṉa. itu tamiḻiṉ allatu tirāviṭattiṉ ciṟappiyalpu.
māntaṉ iyakkam allatu celavu nilattiṉmēlum maliyiṉmēlum nikaḻvatāl, muṉmiccuṭṭu iṭam nōkki uyirccic cuṭṭumākum.
kālamuṉ iṭamuṉ eṉa muṉ iruvakippaṭum oṉṟōṭoṉṟu mayaṅkuvatāl, muṉmiccuṭṭu piṉmic cuṭṭākavum ilakkiyattil aruki vaḻaṅkum.

teṉkiḻak kirōppāvi yaṭutta mēli yāciyāviṉiṉṟu vantatākat terikiṉṟa kīḻiyāriyam, īrāṉiyam, intiyam eṉa iru kiḷikaḷuḷḷatu. īrāṉiyattiṉ mutunili centu (zend); putunili pāracīkam (persian). intiyattiṉ mutunili vētamoḻi; putunili camaṟkirutam.
umpar-uppiri (centu), uppari (vētamoḻi)
ittāliyac coṟpōl cakara meymmutal peṟāmalum, kirēkkac coṟpōl hakaramutalākat tiriyātum, ukara mutalatākavē tamiḻccoṟ pōlum orucār cerumāṉiyatti yottum, kīḻiyāriyac coṟkaḷ vaḻaṅkuvatu kavaṉikkat takkatu.
āriya valliṉam tamiḻ valliṉatti nōkkak kaṭiyaṉavātalāl, upari, upiri eṉa oṟṟip pakarattoṭu kuṟikkappaṭum āriyac coṟkaḷ tamiḻ oliyiyaṟkēṟpa iraṭṭip pakarattoṭu kuṟippaṭṭaṉa, eṉa aṟika.
kumari nilattamiḻiṉiṉṟu tiraviṭamum, tiraviṭattiṉiṉṟu pirākirutamum, pirākirutattiṉiṉṟu āriyamum tirintiruttalāl, vētamoḻi tirimoḻiyēyaṉṟit tamiḻpōl iyaṉmoḻi yaṉṟu.
ātalāl, uppari eṉṉum vētamoḻic col umpar eṉṉum teṉcoṟṟiripē yeṉpatu teritaru tēṟṟam.
upari ya, to go upwards.
Upari silam gam, to go over the mountin.
Sahasram satani upari casiau, 1000 and 800 in addition.
upari payah pibet, he must drink milk afterwards.
upary upari sarvesam atisthat, he stood at the very head of all.
atmanam tasya upari ksiptva, having thrown himself upon him.
mamopari vikaritatih, changed in feeling with regard to me.
putrasyopari kruddhah, enraged towards his son.
muhurtad upari, after a minute.
upari kuti, an upper room.
upary upari, higher and higher, repeatedly, continuously (R.V.)

iya-ey-eytu. eytutal = (ce.kuṉṟā vī). 1. aṇukutal. neṭiyō yeyta vantaṉam (puṟanā.10). 2. ceṉṟaṭital, cērtal. tūniṟak kaṅkiyāṉ cūḻa veytiṉāṉ (pārata. kurukula.34). 3. aṭital, peṟutal. ēmavika leytiṉṟā lulakē (kuṟun. kaṭavuḷ vāḻttu.). - (ce.ku.vi.) 1. nikaḻtal. eṭṭi kumaraṉeytiya turippōṉ (maṇi. 4:64). 2. vantucērtal. māceṉak keytavum (kamparā.ciṟap.6). 3. poruntutal. kālattō ṭeyta vuṇarntu ceyal (kuṟaḷ.516). 4. pōtiyatātal. cuṉivāyc ciṟunīri yeytā teṉṟeṇṇi (ain.aim.38).
ma.eytu, te.eyitu.
ey-ēy. ēytal = (ce.ku.vi.) 1. poruntutal. ēynta pēḻvāy (tiv.periyati. 1:7:3). 2. takutal. tilliyūraṉukkiṉ ṟēyāppaḻi (tirukkō.374). 3. ottal. cēlēy kaṇṇiyarum (tiv.tiruvāy. 5:1:8). - (ce.kuṉṟā.vi.) etirppaṭutal. puṉattu maṟiyiṉā lēyiṉā riṉṟiyiṉitu (ain.aim.11).
ēyttal = 1. ottal. allip pāvi yāṭuvaṉap pēyppa (puṟanā.33). 2. poruntac collutal. poy kuṟaḷi yēyppār (paḻa.77). 3. vañcittal, ēmāṟṟutal. (u.va.).
ēy-ē-yā-yāttiri = vaḻiccelavu. takaṭūr yāttiri.
yāttiri-va. yātrā.
ē-ēku. ēkutal = cellutal, pōtal. tāyttāyk koṇṭēku maḷittiv vulaku (nālaṭi.15). 2. naṭattal. (piṅ.) 3. kaḻalutal. nalvaḷiyēka (pu.ve.11, peṇpāṟ.12). o.nō: pō-pōku.
yā-jā. (inti). jā-kā (ga). kayā (gaya) = pōṉāṉ.
kā (ga)-t. kam (gam), kamana (gamana) = celavu.
OE, OS. gan, OHG. gan, gen. E. go.
pakaram jakaramāvatu inti āriya moḻi yiyalpu.
e-ṭu: inti. ít‹ (t.)-jath‹, yātrā-jātrā-yōki-jōki.
t.bastd-L. juvenilis, E.juvenile.
va. íf (g.)-E. yoke, L.jugo, conjugo, to yoke to gether. E. conjugate.
iy-va. i (i), to go, walk; to flow; to blow; to advance, spread, get about; to go to or towards (with acc.) come, RV.; AV.; S Br.; M Bh.; R.; Hit; Ragh. & c.; to go away, escape, pass, retire, RV.; AV.; SBr, to arise from, come from, RV.; Ch up.; to return (in this sense only fut). M Bh.; R.; (with punar) to come back agin, return, M Bh.; R.; Pancat. & c.; to succeed, Mn. iii, 127; to arrive at, reach, obtin, RV.; AV.; SBr.; Sak.; Hit. & c.; to fall into, come to.; to approach with prayers, gin by asking (of. ita); to undertake anything (with acc.); to be employed in, go on with, continue in any condition or relation (with a part, or instr., e.g. asura rakshasani mridyananani yanti, “the Asuras and Rakshasas are being continually crushed,” SBr. i, 1, 4, 14.
(māṉiyar ulliyamcu camaṟkiruta-āṅkila, akara mutali pak.163).
ē-va. V (a) to go, come near or towards, go near, approach, RV.; AV.; SBr. etc.; to reach, attin, enter, come into (a state or position), Mn. xil, 125.
eta, come near, approached, RV.
